                    Or (Left(nx, 1) = "+") Or (Left(nx, 1) = "-") Or (Left(nx, 3) = "and") _
                    Or (Left(nx, 2) = "or") Or (Left(nx, 1) = ".")
                If Not badn Then
                    ncand = ncand + 1
                    If ncand > UBound(cand) Then ReDim Preserve cand(1 To ncand * 2)
                    cand(ncand) = k
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next k
End Sub

Private Function L9_NameTaken(ByRef L() As String, ByVal n As Long, ByVal nm As String) As Boolean
    Dim k As Long
    L9_NameTaken = False
    For k = 1 To n
        If InStr(L(k), "procedure " & nm & ";") > 0 Then L9_NameTaken = True: Exit Function
    Next k
End Function

Private Function L9_RenameWord(ByVal s As String, ByVal oldw As String, ByVal neww As String) As String
    Dim out As String, q As Boolean, i As Long, c As String, j As Long, w As String, cc As String
    out = "": q = False: i = 1
    Do While i <= Len(s)
        c = Mid(s, i, 1)
        If c = "'" Then
            q = Not q: out = out & c: i = i + 1
        ElseIf (Not q) And ((c >= "a" And c <= "z") Or (c >= "A" And c <= "Z") Or c = "_") Then
            j = i
            Do While j <= Len(s)
                cc = Mid(s, j, 1)
                If (cc >= "a" And cc <= "z") Or (cc >= "A" And cc <= "Z") Or (cc >= "0" And cc <= "9") Or cc = "_" Then
                    j = j + 1
                Else
                    Exit Do
                End If
            Loop
            w = Mid(s, i, j - i)
            If StrComp(w, oldw, vbTextCompare) = 0 And (j > Len(s) Or Not ((Mid(s, j, 1) >= "a" And Mid(s, j, 1) <= "z") Or (Mid(s, j, 1) >= "A" And Mid(s, j, 1) <= "Z") Or (Mid(s, j, 1) >= "0" And Mid(s, j, 1) <= "9") Or Mid(s, j, 1) = "_")) Then
                out = out & neww
            Else
                out = out & w
            End If
            i = j
        ElseIf c = "/" And (Not q) And i < Len(s) And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "/" Then
            out = out & Mid(s, i)
            i = Len(s) + 1
        Else
            out = out & c: i = i + 1
        End If
    Loop
    L9_RenameWord = out
End Function

Private Function L9_FindDecl(ByRef L() As String, ByVal n As Long, ByVal nm As String) As Long
    Dim k As Long, t As String
    L9_FindDecl = 0
    For k = 1 To n
        t = Trim(L(k))
        If LCase(t) = "implementation" Then Exit For
        If LCase(Left(t, 10)) = "procedure " Then
            If Left(Mid(t, 11), Len(nm) + 1) = nm & ";" Then L9_FindDecl = k: Exit Function
        End If
    Next k
End Function

Private Sub L9_FindLoop(ByRef base1() As String, ByVal bn1 As Long, ByVal thrMono As Long, ByRef lbA As Long, ByRef lbE As Long)
    Dim i As Long, dd As Long, pv As Long, ltt As String
    Dim lws() As String, k As Long, lw As String, lhasB As Boolean, lhasE As Boolean
    Dim d2 As Long, j As Long
    lbA = 0: lbE = 0: dd = 0
    For i = 1 To bn1
        pv = dd
        dd = dd + L9_Delta(base1(i))
        If lbA = 0 Then
            ltt = LCase(L9_MonoStrip(base1(i)))
            If InStr(1, ltt, "for ") > 0 Or InStr(1, ltt, "while ") > 0 Then
                lhasB = False: lhasE = False
                lws = L9_MonoWords(ltt)
                For k = LBound(lws) To UBound(lws)
                    lw = lws(k)
                    If lw = "begin" Or lw = "repeat" Then lhasB = True
                    If lw = "end" Or lw = "until" Then lhasE = True
                Next k
                If lhasB And Not lhasE Then
                    d2 = dd
                    For j = i + 1 To bn1
                        d2 = d2 + L9_Delta(base1(j))
                        If d2 = pv Then
                            If (j - i - 1) > thrMono Then
                                lbA = i: lbE = j
                            End If
                            Exit For
                        End If
                    Next j
                    If lbA > 0 Then Exit For
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next i
End Sub

Private Function L9_MonoSplit(ByRef base1() As String, ByRef bn1 As Long, _
    ByVal thrMono As Long, ByRef nParts As Long, ByRef maxPart As Long, _
    ByRef msg As String, ByRef cycFlag As Long, ByRef joints() As Long, ByRef nj As Long) As Boolean
    Dim i As Long, j As Long, k As Long, dd As Long, pv As Long
    Dim bA As Long, be As Long, blen As Long
    Dim ifStart As Long, t As String, tt As String
    Dim cvars() As String, nc As Long, ws() As String, w As String
    Dim badCond As Boolean, oneSided As Long, hasB As Boolean, hasE As Boolean, hasC As Boolean
    Dim cand() As Long, ncand As Long, jn As Long, nx As String, badn As Boolean
    Dim np As Long, tg As Long, bS As Long, lastc As Long
    Dim cts() As Long
    Dim out2() As String, m2 As Long, cap2 As Long, pE2 As Long
    L9_MonoSplit = False: nParts = 0: maxPart = 0: msg = "": cycFlag = 0: nj = 0
    bA = 0: be = 0: dd = 0
    For i = 1 To bn1
        pv = dd
        dd = dd + L9_Delta(base1(i))
        If pv = 0 And dd > 0 Then bA = i
        If dd = 0 And pv > 0 And bA > 0 Then
            If (i - bA - 1) > thrMono Then
                be = i: Exit For
            Else
                bA = 0: be = 0
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    If bA = 0 Then Exit Function
    blen = be - bA - 1
    ifStart = bA
    For i = bA - 1 To 1 Step -1
        t = LCase(Trim(base1(i)))
        If Len(t) = 0 Then
            ifStart = i
        ElseIf Left(t, 3) = "if " Or Left(t, 3) = "if(" Or Left(t, 4) = "elseif" Then
            ifStart = i: Exit For
        Else
            ifStart = bA: Exit For
        End If
    Next i
    nc = 0: ReDim cvars(1 To 16)
    For i = ifStart To bA
        ws = L9_MonoWords(L9_MonoStrip(base1(i)))
        For j = LBound(ws) To UBound(ws)
            w = ws(j)
            If Len(w) > 0 And w <> "if" And w <> "then" And w <> "begin" _
               And w <> "and" And w <> "or" And w <> "not" And w <> "elseif" _
               And w <> "end" And w <> "=" Then
                If Not ((Left(w, 1) >= "0") And (Left(w, 1) <= "9")) Then
                    nc = nc + 1
                    If nc > UBound(cvars) Then ReDim Preserve cvars(1 To nc * 2)
                    cvars(nc) = w
                End If
            End If
        Next j
    Next i
    oneSided = 0: badCond = False: cycFlag = 0
    For i = bA + 1 To be - 1
        tt = LCase(L9_MonoStrip(base1(i)))
        ws = L9_MonoWords(tt)
        hasB = False: hasE = False: hasC = False
        For j = LBound(ws) To UBound(ws)
            w = ws(j)
            If w = "begin" Or w = "repeat" Then hasB = True
            If w = "end" Or w = "until" Then hasE = True
            If w = "for" Or w = "while" Or w = "case" Then hasC = True
        Next j
        If hasC And Not hasB And Not hasE Then oneSided = oneSided + 1
        If hasC And hasB And Not hasE Then cycFlag = 1
        If (Not badCond) And nc > 0 Then
            For j = 1 To nc
                If InStr(1, tt, cvars(j) & ":=") > 0 Then badCond = True: Exit For
                If InStr(1, tt, cvars(j) & " :=") > 0 Then badCond = True: Exit For
            Next j
        End If
    Next i
    If oneSided > 0 Then
        msg = "Ф32а: блок " & CStr(blen) & " НЕ РЕЗАН: одностороние цикл-строки (" & CStr(oneSided) & ")"
        Exit Function
    End If
    If badCond Then
        msg = "Ф32а: блок " & CStr(blen) & " НЕ РЕЗАН: переменная условия присваивается внутри блока"
        Exit Function
    End If
    If cycFlag = 1 Then
        msg = "Ф32а: блок " & CStr(blen) & " содержит многострочный цикл - путь Ф32б"
        Exit Function
    End If
    ncand = 0: ReDim cand(1 To 64)
    dd = 0
    For i = bA + 1 To be - 1
        t = base1(i)
        pv = dd
        dd = dd + L9_Delta(t)
        If dd = 0 And pv = 0 Then
            tt = Trim(t)
            If Left(tt, 2) = "//" Then
                ncand = ncand + 1
                If ncand > UBound(cand) Then ReDim Preserve cand(1 To ncand * 2)
                cand(ncand) = i - bA
            ElseIf Right(tt, 1) = ";" And LCase(Left(tt, 3)) <> "end" And LCase(Left(tt, 4)) <> "else" Then
                jn = i + 1
                Do While jn <= be - 1
                    If Len(Trim(base1(jn))) > 0 Then Exit Do
                    jn = jn + 1
                Loop
                nx = ""
                If jn <= be - 1 Then nx = LCase(Left(Trim(base1(jn)), 4))
                badn = (nx = "else") Or (Left(nx, 3) = "end") Or (Left(nx, 4) = "unti") _
                    Or (Left(nx, 1) = "+") Or (Left(nx, 1) = "-") Or (Left(nx, 3) = "and") _
                    Or (Left(nx, 2) = "or") Or (Left(nx, 1) = ".")
                If Not badn Then
                    ncand = ncand + 1
                    If ncand > UBound(cand) Then ReDim Preserve cand(1 To ncand * 2)
                    cand(ncand) = i - bA
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    If ncand = 0 Then
        msg = "Ф32а: блок " & CStr(blen) & " НЕ РЕЗАН: точек реза внутри блока нет"
        Exit Function
    End If
    np = (blen + thrMono - 1) \ thrMono
    If np < 2 Then np = 2
    ReDim cts(0 To np)
    lastc = 0
    For k = 1 To np - 1
        tg = k * blen \ np
        bS = 0
        For j = 1 To ncand
            If cand(j) > lastc Then
                If bS = 0 Or Abs(cand(j) - tg) < Abs(bS - tg) Then bS = cand(j)
            End If
        Next j
        If bS = 0 Then bS = blen - 1
        If bS >= blen Then bS = blen - 1
        If bS <= lastc Then bS = lastc + 1
        cts(k) = bS: lastc = bS
    Next k
    cts(np) = blen
    cap2 = bn1 + np * 4 + 16
    ReDim out2(1 To cap2): m2 = 0
    ReDim joints(1 To np): nj = 0
    For i = 1 To bA
        m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = base1(i)
    Next i
    For k = 1 To np
        For i = bA + cts(k - 1) + 1 To bA + cts(k)
            m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = base1(i)
        Next i
        If k < np Then
            pE2 = InStr(base1(be), ";")
            If pE2 > 0 Then
                m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = Left(base1(be), pE2)
            Else
                m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = base1(be)
            End If
            nj = nj + 1: joints(nj) = m2
            For i = ifStart To bA
                m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = base1(i)
            Next i
        End If
    Next k
    For i = be To bn1
        m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = base1(i)
    Next i
    ReDim Preserve base1(1 To m2)
    For i = 1 To m2
        base1(i) = out2(i)
    Next i
    bn1 = m2
    nParts = np
    maxPart = 0
    For k = 1 To np
        If (cts(k) - cts(k - 1)) > maxPart Then maxPart = cts(k) - cts(k - 1)
    Next k
    msg = "Ф32а: блок " & CStr(blen) & " -> " & CStr(np) & " кусков, макс. кусок " & CStr(maxPart) & " -> режет N-резку по стыкам"
    L9_MonoSplit = True
End Function

Private Sub L9_ApplyCut(ByRef L() As String, ByRef n As Long, ByVal cls As String, ByVal nm As String, _
    ByVal marker As String, ByRef names() As String, ByVal np As Long, _
    ByRef bodyTxt() As String, ByRef inner2() As String, ByVal inn2 As Long, ByRef exc() As String, ByVal td1 As Long, _
    ByRef fldNm() As String, ByRef fldTp() As String, ByVal nfl As Long, ByRef outRes() As String, ByRef mRes As Long)
    Dim out() As String, m As Long, cap As Long
    cap = n + np * 4 + 512
    ReDim out(1 To cap): m = 0
    Dim i As Long, j As Long, k As Long
    Dim hdr As Long, bi As Long
    hdr = 0: bi = 0
    For i = 1 To n
        If (Len(cls) > 0 And Trim(L(i)) = "procedure " & cls & "." & nm & ";") Or (Len(cls) = 0 And Trim(L(i)) = "procedure " & nm & ";") Then
            hdr = i: Exit For
        End If
    Next i
    If hdr = 0 Then Exit Sub
    For j = hdr + 1 To n
        If Trim(L(j)) = "begin" Then bi = j: Exit For
    Next j
    If bi = 0 Then Exit Sub
    Dim dd As Long, ei As Long
    dd = 1: ei = 0
    For j = bi + 1 To n
        dd = dd + L9_Delta(L(j))
        If dd <= 0 Then ei = j: Exit For
    Next j
    For i = 1 To bi
        m = m + 1: out(m) = L(i)
    Next i
    If td1 = 1 Then
        m = m + 1: out(m) = "  try"
    End If
    If inn2 > 0 Then
        For j = 1 To inn2
            m = m + 1: out(m) = inner2(j)
        Next j
    Else
        For k = 1 To np
            m = m + 1: out(m) = "    " & names(k) & ";  // " & marker & ": часть 1 из " & CStr(np) & " процедуры " & nm
        Next k
    End If
    For k = 1 To UBound(exc)
        m = m + 1: out(m) = exc(k)
    Next k
    m = m + 1: out(m) = "end;"
    For i = ei + 1 To n
        m = m + 1: out(m) = L(i)
    Next i
    Dim diNew As Long
    diNew = 0
    For i = 1 To m
        If LCase(Left(Trim(out(i)), 10)) = "procedure " Then
            If Left(Mid(Trim(out(i)), 11), Len(nm) + 1) = nm & ";" Then diNew = i: Exit For
        End If
    Next i
    If diNew > 0 Then
        Dim nl() As String, out2() As String, m2 As Long
        ReDim nl(1 To np)
        For k = 1 To np
            nl(k) = "    procedure " & names(k) & ";      // " & marker & ": часть " & CStr(k) & " из " & CStr(np) & " процедуры " & nm
        Next k
        m2 = 0: cap = m + np + 8
        ReDim out2(1 To cap)
        For i = 1 To diNew: m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = out(i): Next i
        For k = 1 To np: m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = nl(k): Next k
        For i = diNew + 1 To m: m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = out(i): Next i
        m = m2: out = out2
    End If
    If nfl > 0 Then
        Dim clsLn As Long
        clsLn = 0
        For i = 1 To m
            If InStr(LCase(out(i)), "=class(") > 0 Then clsLn = i: Exit For
        Next i
        If clsLn > 0 Then
            m2 = 0: cap = m + nfl + 8
            ReDim out2(1 To cap)
            For i = 1 To clsLn: m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = out(i): Next i
            For k = 1 To nfl
                m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = "    " & fldNm(k) & ": " & fldTp(k) & ";  // " & marker & ": локал процедуры " & nm
            Next k
            For i = clsLn + 1 To m: m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = out(i): Next i
            m = m2: out = out2
        End If
    End If
    Dim hdr2 As Long, beg2 As Long, ei2 As Long, dd3 As Long
    hdr2 = 0
    For i = 1 To m
        If (Len(cls) > 0 And Trim(out(i)) = "procedure " & cls & "." & nm & ";") Or (Len(cls) = 0 And Trim(out(i)) = "procedure " & nm & ";") Then
            hdr2 = i: Exit For
        End If
    Next i
    If hdr2 > 0 Then
        For j = hdr2 + 1 To m
            If Trim(out(j)) = "begin" Then beg2 = j: Exit For
        Next j
        dd3 = 1: ei2 = 0
        For j = beg2 + 1 To m
            dd3 = dd3 + L9_Delta(out(j))
            If dd3 <= 0 Then ei2 = j: Exit For
        Next j
        If ei2 > 0 Then
            m2 = 0: cap = m + np * 8 + 64
            ReDim out2(1 To cap)
            For i = 1 To ei2: m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = out(i): Next i
            Dim lines2() As String
            For k = 1 To np
                m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = ""
                m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = "procedure " & cls & "." & names(k) & ";"
                m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = "begin"
                lines2 = Split(bodyTxt(k), vbCrLf)
                For j = LBound(lines2) To UBound(lines2)
                    If Len(lines2(j)) > 0 Then
                        m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = lines2(j)
                    End If
                Next j
                m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = "    Miss_comm:=1; // " & marker & ": расчёт дошёл до конца части"
                m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = "end;"
            Next k
            For i = ei2 + 1 To m: m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = out(i): Next i
            m = m2: out = out2
        End If
    End If
    ReDim Preserve out(1 To m)
    outRes = out: mRes = m
End Sub

Private Sub L9_CutOne(ByRef L() As String, ByRef n As Long, ByVal cls As String, ByVal nm As String, _
    ByVal marker As String, ByRef nCut As Long, ByRef nPartsAll As Long, ByRef nMaxAll As Long, ByRef msgLog As String, ByVal fp As String)
    Const L9_THR_LINE As Long = 5000: Const L9_THR_INNER As Long = 4000   ' LH2 v40 Ф36: локальные
    Dim hdr As Long, bi As Long, ei As Long, declI As Long
    Dim i As Long, j As Long, k As Long, dd As Long, tt As String, pp As Long
    Dim out() As String, m As Long
    msgLog = ""
    declI = L9_FindDecl(L, n, nm)
    If declI = 0 Then msgLog = "skip: нет декларации": Exit Sub
    hdr = 0
    For i = 1 To n
        If (Len(cls) > 0 And Trim(L(i)) = "procedure " & cls & "." & nm & ";") Or (Len(cls) = 0 And Trim(L(i)) = "procedure " & nm & ";") Then
            hdr = i: Exit For
        End If
    Next i
    If hdr = 0 Then msgLog = "skip: нет реализации": Exit Sub
    bi = 0
    For j = hdr + 1 To n
        If Trim(L(j)) = "begin" Then bi = j: Exit For
    Next j
    If bi = 0 Then msgLog = "skip: нет begin": Exit Sub
    dd = 1: ei = 0
    For j = bi + 1 To n
        dd = dd + L9_Delta(L(j))
        If dd <= 0 Then ei = j: Exit For
    Next j
    If ei = 0 Then msgLog = "skip: нет конца": Exit Sub
    If (ei - hdr) <= L9_THR_LINE Then msgLog = "skip: в пороге": Exit Sub
    Dim bL() As String, bln As Long
    bln = ei - bi - 1
    ReDim bL(1 To bln)
    For j = 1 To bln: bL(j) = L(bi + j): Next j
    Dim idxExc As Long, dd2 As Long
    dd2 = 0: idxExc = 0
    For j = 1 To bln
        tt = Trim(bL(j))
        If dd2 = 1 And (tt = "except" Or Left(tt, 7) = "except ") Then idxExc = j: Exit For
        dd2 = dd2 + L9_Delta(bL(j))
    Next j
    Dim inner() As String, inn As Long, exc() As String, exn As Long
    inn = 0: exn = 0
    If idxExc > 0 Then
        ReDim inner(1 To idxExc - 1)
        For j = 1 To idxExc - 1: inn = inn + 1: inner(inn) = bL(j): Next j
        ReDim exc(1 To bln - idxExc + 1)
        For j = idxExc To bln: exn = exn + 1: exc(exn) = bL(j): Next j
    Else
        ReDim inner(1 To bln)
        For j = 1 To bln: inn = inn + 1: inner(inn) = bL(j): Next j
        ReDim exc(1 To 1): exc(1) = ""
    End If
    Dim td1 As Long
    td1 = 0
    If inn > 0 And (Trim(inner(1)) = "try" Or Left(Trim(inner(1)), 4) = "try ") Then td1 = 1
    Dim base1() As String, bn1 As Long
    If td1 = 1 Then
        bn1 = inn - 1
        ReDim base1(1 To bn1)
        For j = 2 To inn: base1(j - 1) = inner(j): Next j
    Else
        bn1 = inn
        ReDim base1(1 To bn1)
        For j = 1 To inn: base1(j) = inner(j): Next j
    End If
    If bn1 <= L9_THR_LINE Then msgLog = "skip: в пороге": Exit Sub
    Dim fldNm() As String, fldTp() As String, nfl As Long
    ReDim fldNm(1 To 16): ReDim fldTp(1 To 16): nfl = 0
    For j = hdr + 1 To bi - 1
        tt = Trim(L(j))
        If Len(tt) > 0 And LCase(tt) <> "var" And Left(tt, 2) <> "//" And Left(tt, 1) <> "{" Then
            Dim lhs As String, tp As String, vv() As String, vi As Long, vn As String
            pp = InStr(tt, ":")
            If pp > 1 Then
                lhs = Trim(Left(tt, pp - 1))
                tp = Trim(Mid(tt, pp + 1))
                If Right(tp, 1) = ";" Then tp = Trim(Left(tp, Len(tp) - 1))
                vv = Split(lhs, ",")
                For vi = LBound(vv) To UBound(vv)
                    vn = Trim(vv(vi))
                    If Len(vn) > 0 And L9_Simple(vn) Then
                        nfl = nfl + 1
                        If nfl > UBound(fldNm) Then
                            ReDim Preserve fldNm(1 To nfl * 2): ReDim Preserve fldTp(1 To nfl * 2)
                        End If
                        fldNm(nfl) = nm & "_" & vn
                        fldTp(nfl) = tp
                        For k = 1 To bn1: base1(k) = L9_RenameWord(base1(k), vn, fldNm(nfl)): Next k
                        For k = 1 To exn: exc(k) = L9_RenameWord(exc(k), vn, fldNm(nfl)): Next k
                    End If
                Next vi
            End If
        End If
    Next j
    Dim lbA As Long, lbE As Long
    L9_FindLoop base1, bn1, L9_THR_INNER, lbA, lbE
    Dim names() As String, np As Long
    Dim bodyTxt() As String
    Dim inner2() As String, inn2 As Long
    Dim note As String, maxp As Long
    Dim qn As String, seg As String, seg2 As String
    If lbA > 0 Then
        Dim llen As Long
        llen = lbE - lbA - 1
        Dim tbody() As String
        ReDim tbody(1 To llen)
        For j = 1 To llen: tbody(j) = base1(lbA + j): Next j
        Dim tcand() As Long, tncand As Long
        ReDim tcand(1 To 64)
        L9_CutCand tbody, llen, tcand, tncand, 0
        If tncand = 0 Then
            msgLog = "skip: цикл " & CStr(llen) & " - точек реза в теле цикла нет"   ' Ф51 v45
            Exit Sub
        End If
        np = (llen + L9_THR_INNER - 1) \ L9_THR_INNER
        If np < 2 Then np = 2
        Dim lcts() As Long, llast As Long, ltg As Long, lbs As Long
        ReDim lcts(0 To np): llast = 0
        For k = 1 To np - 1
            ltg = k * llen \ np
            lbs = 0
            For j = 1 To tncand
                If tcand(j) > llast Then
                    If lbs = 0 Or Abs(tcand(j) - ltg) < Abs(lbs - ltg) Then lbs = tcand(j)
                End If
            Next j
            If lbs = 0 Then lbs = llen - 1
            If lbs >= llen Then lbs = llen - 1
            If lbs <= llast Then lbs = llast + 1
            lcts(k) = lbs: llast = lbs
        Next k
        lcts(np) = llen
        ReDim names(1 To np)
        For k = 1 To np
            qn = nm & L9_Suf(k)
            Do While L9_NameTaken(L, n, qn)
                qn = qn & "x"
            Loop
            names(k) = qn
        Next k
        ReDim inner2(1 To bn1 + np + 8): inn2 = 0
        For j = 1 To lbA + 1
            inn2 = inn2 + 1: inner2(inn2) = base1(j)
        Next j
        For k = 1 To np
            inn2 = inn2 + 1: inner2(inn2) = "    " & names(k) & ";  // " & marker & ": часть " & CStr(k) & " из " & CStr(np) & " цикла " & nm
        Next k
        For j = lbE To bn1
            inn2 = inn2 + 1: inner2(inn2) = base1(j)
        Next j
        ReDim Preserve inner2(1 To inn2)
        maxp = 0
        ReDim bodyTxt(1 To np)
        For k = 1 To np
            seg = ""
            For j = lcts(k - 1) + 1 To lcts(k)
                seg = seg & tbody(j) & vbCrLf
            Next j
            bodyTxt(k) = seg
            If (lcts(k) - lcts(k - 1)) > maxp Then maxp = lcts(k) - lcts(k - 1)
        Next k
        note = "Ф32б: цикл " & CStr(llen) & " -> " & CStr(np) & " частей, макс. часть " & CStr(maxp)
    Else
        Dim joints() As Long, nj As Long
        Dim monoApplied As Boolean, monoParts As Long, monoMax As Long, monoMsg As String, monoCyc As Long
        monoApplied = L9_MonoSplit(base1, bn1, L9_THR_INNER, monoParts, monoMax, monoMsg, monoCyc, joints, nj)
        ' Ф51 v45 (LH2): отказ Ф32а больше НЕ skip (корень "Procedure too complex" на 19.801):
        '      если в теле есть точки реза на глубине 0 (кандидаты L9_CutCand) - режем
        '      по кандидатам на N частей; если точек нет - прежний skip (тело выходит
        '      неразрезанным, как было). Отказ MonoSplit НЕ портит base1/bn1: все выходы
        '      отказа - до перестройки out2.
        If Not monoApplied Then nj = 0
        Dim cand() As Long, ncand As Long
        ReDim cand(1 To 64)
        L9_CutCand base1, bn1, cand, ncand, 0
        For k = 1 To nj
            ncand = ncand + 1
            If ncand > UBound(cand) Then ReDim Preserve cand(1 To ncand * 2)
            cand(ncand) = joints(k)
        Next k
        ' Ф37 (LH2 v41): "Dim n As Long" удалён - дубль параметра ByRef n As Long (VBA: Duplicate declaration)
        n = bn1
        If n <= L9_THR_LINE Then
            msgLog = "skip: в пороге после Ф32а"
            Exit Sub
        End If
        If ncand = 0 Then
            msgLog = "skip: " & monoMsg & "; точек реза на глубине 0 нет"   ' Ф51 v45
            Exit Sub
        End If
        np = (n + L9_THR_LINE - 1) \ L9_THR_LINE
        If np < 2 Then np = 2
        Dim cts() As Long, last As Long, tg As Long, bS As Long
        ReDim cts(0 To np): last = 0
        For k = 1 To np - 1
            tg = k * n \ np
            bS = 0
            For j = 1 To ncand
                If cand(j) > last Then
                    If bS = 0 Or Abs(cand(j) - tg) < Abs(bS - tg) Then bS = cand(j)
                End If
            Next j
            If bS = 0 Then bS = n - 1
            If bS >= n Then bS = n - 1
            If bS <= last Then bS = last + 1
            cts(k) = bS: last = bS
        Next k
        cts(np) = n
        ReDim names(1 To np)
        For k = 1 To np
            qn = nm & L9_Suf(k)
            Do While L9_NameTaken(L, n, qn)
                qn = qn & "x"
            Loop
            names(k) = qn
        Next k
        maxp = 0
        ReDim bodyTxt(1 To np)
        For k = 1 To np
            seg2 = ""
            For j = cts(k - 1) + 1 To cts(k)
                seg2 = seg2 & base1(j) & vbCrLf
            Next j
            bodyTxt(k) = seg2
            If (cts(k) - cts(k - 1)) > maxp Then maxp = cts(k) - cts(k - 1)
        Next k
        ReDim inner2(1 To 1): inn2 = 0
        note = "Ф32а+N-резка: " & CStr(np) & " частей, макс. часть " & CStr(maxp) & IIf(monoApplied, "", "; Ф32а не резал: " & monoMsg)
    End If
    L9_ApplyCut L, n, cls, nm, marker, names, np, bodyTxt, inner2, inn2, exc, td1, fldNm, fldTp, nfl, out, m
    n = m
    Dim f2 As Integer
    f2 = FreeFile
    Open fp For Output As #f2
    For i = 1 To n
        Print #f2, out(i)
    Next i
    Close #f2
    nCut = nCut + 1
    nPartsAll = nPartsAll + np
    If maxp > nMaxAll Then nMaxAll = maxp
    msgLog = note
End Sub

Private Sub L9_CutFile(ByVal pth As String, ByVal fn As String, ByRef nCut As Long, ByRef nPartsAll As Long, ByRef nMaxAll As Long, ByVal marker As String)
    Const L9_THR_LINE As Long = 5000   ' LH2 v40 Ф36: локальные - вставка самодостаточна
    Dim fPath As String, f1 As Integer
    Dim L() As String, n As Long, ln As String
    Dim i As Long, k As Long, e As Long
    Dim prs() As Long, npr As Long
    Dim skipFrom As Long, it As Long
    Dim cls As String, nm As String, msgLog As String
    Dim tgt As Long
    fPath = pth & fn
    f1 = FreeFile
    ReDim L(1 To 4096): n = 0
    Open fPath For Input As #f1
    Do While Not EOF(f1)
        If n = UBound(L) Then ReDim Preserve L(1 To UBound(L) * 2)
        n = n + 1
        Line Input #f1, ln
        If InStr(ln, "Ф32-пост-резка " & marker) > 0 Then
            Close #f1
            Exit Sub
        End If
        L(n) = ln
    Loop
    Close #f1
    ReDim Preserve L(1 To n)
    skipFrom = 0
    For it = 1 To 200
        ReDim L(1 To 4096): n = 0
        f1 = FreeFile
        Open fPath For Input As #f1
        Do While Not EOF(f1)
            If n = UBound(L) Then ReDim Preserve L(1 To UBound(L) * 2)
            n = n + 1
            Line Input #f1, ln
            L(n) = ln
        Loop
        Close #f1
        ReDim Preserve L(1 To n)
        ReDim prs(1 To n \ 8 + 8): npr = 0
        For i = 1 To n
            If Left(Trim(L(i)), 10) = "procedure " Then
                If npr = UBound(prs) Then ReDim Preserve prs(1 To npr * 2)
                npr = npr + 1: prs(npr) = i
            End If
        Next i
        tgt = 0
        For k = 1 To npr
            e = n
            If k < npr Then e = prs(k + 1) - 1
            If e - prs(k) > L9_THR_LINE And prs(k) > skipFrom Then
                nm = Trim(L(prs(k)))
                nm = Left(nm, Len(nm) - 1)
                If InStr(nm, "(") > 0 Then GoTo NextCandK   ' Ф14: процедуры с параметрами пост-резкой не режем (части теряют параметры)
                cls = ""
                If InStr(nm, ".") > 0 Then cls = Left(nm, InStr(nm, ".") - 1): nm = Mid(nm, InStr(nm, ".") + 1)
                tgt = prs(k)
                Exit For
            End If
NextCandK:
        Next k
        If tgt = 0 Then Exit For
        L9_CutOne L, n, cls, nm, marker, nCut, nPartsAll, nMaxAll, msgLog, fPath
        If Left(msgLog, 4) = "skip" Then
            skipFrom = tgt
        Else
            skipFrom = 0
        End If
    Next it
    f1 = FreeFile
    Open fPath For Append As #f1
    Print #f1, "// Ф32-пост-резка " & marker & ": процедур разрезано " & CStr(nCut) & ", частей добавлено " & CStr(nPartsAll) & ", макс. часть " & CStr(nMaxAll)
    Close #f1
End Sub
' Ф57: СТОП-инвариант приёмки. Меряет ТЕЛО процедуры dd-сканом от begin до парного end.
' Вызывается ПОСЛЕ последнего прохода резки по выходному файлу; накапливает нарушения.
' Решение о выпуске комплекта принимает основной процесс по CG4_StopFlag.
Private Sub CG4_StopCheck(ByVal pth As String, ByVal outn As String)
    Dim fPath As String, f1 As Integer
    Dim L() As String, n As Long, ln As String
    Dim i As Long, k As Long, hdr As Long, bi As Long, ei As Long
    Dim dd As Long, body As Long, t As String, nm As String
    fPath = pth & outn
    If Dir(fPath) = "" Then Exit Sub
    ReDim L(1 To 4096): n = 0
    f1 = FreeFile
    Open fPath For Input As #f1
    Do While Not EOF(f1)
        If n = UBound(L) Then ReDim Preserve L(1 To UBound(L) * 2)
        n = n + 1
        Line Input #f1, ln
        L(n) = ln
    Loop
    Close #f1
    If n = 0 Then Exit Sub
    ReDim Preserve L(1 To n)
    i = 1
    Do While i <= n
        t = Trim(L(i))
        If Left(t, 10) = "procedure " Then
            hdr = i
            Do While hdr <= n
                If Right(Trim(L(hdr)), 1) = ";" Then Exit Do
                hdr = hdr + 1
            Loop
            If hdr > n Then Exit Do
            nm = Trim(L(hdr))
            nm = Left(nm, Len(nm) - 1)
            If InStr(nm, "(") > 0 Then nm = Trim(Left(nm, InStr(nm, "(") - 1))
            bi = 0
            For k = hdr + 1 To n
                t = Trim(L(k))
                If t = "begin" Then
                    bi = k
                    Exit For
                End If
                If Left(t, 10) = "procedure " Or Left(t, 9) = "function " Then Exit For
                If t = "implementation" Or t = "end." Then Exit For
            Next k
            If bi > 0 Then
                dd = 0: ei = 0
                For k = bi + 1 To n
                    dd = dd + L2_Delta(L(k))
                    If dd < 0 Then
                        ei = k
                        Exit For
                    End If
                Next k
                If ei > bi Then
                    body = ei - bi - 1
                    If body > CG4_SAFE_LINE Then
                        CG4_StopCnt = CG4_StopCnt + 1
                        CG4_StopFlag = True
                        If CG4_StopCnt <= 10 Then
                            CG4_StopMsg = CG4_StopMsg & "  " & nm & " = " & CStr(body) & " строк  (" & outn & ")" & vbCrLf
                        End If
                    End If
                    i = ei
                End If
            End If
        End If
        i = i + 1
    Loop
End Sub

' --- v3: гейт XX0[51] снят (A3); процедура CG4_CloseGate больше не вызывается ---
Private Sub CG4_CloseGate(ByVal f As Integer, ByVal csh As Boolean)
    If csh Then
        Print #f, "}"
    Else
        Print #f, "end;"
    End If
End Sub

' ============================================================================
'  CG4-B: СБОРКА ВКЛАДА БЛОКА (реплика BuildAssembly, с NaN-стражами)
' ============================================================================
Private Function CG4_BuildAssembly(ByVal m As Long, ByVal r As Long, ByVal nSolver As Long, _
                                   ByVal csh As Boolean, ByVal doNaN As Boolean, _
                                   ByRef totB As Long, ByRef totL As Long) As String
    Dim sb As String, p As String, n As Long, fm As String, gt As String
    Dim links As Variant, cntL As Long
    Dim kk As Long, ps As String, pn As String, nb As String
    Dim j As Long, colv As Variant
    Dim termIn As String, termOut As String, termDiag As String, termNg3 As String
    Dim dpl As String

    p = bP_cg(m): n = bN_cg(m): fm = bFm_cg(m): gt = bGt_cg(m)
    links = Split(bLinks_cg(m), ";")
    cntL = UBound(links) + 1
    termIn = "": termOut = "": termDiag = "": termNg3 = ""
    sb = ""

    If csh Then
        sb = sb & "// CG4-B: сборка вклада для " & p & " (#" & n & ", строка " & r & ")" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "aa2 = " & p & " + INT_PRT - 1;" & vbCrLf
        ' LH1 v21/CG4 v3: страж входа в сборке - ВСЕГДА; РЕФОРМА ГАШЕНИЙ:
        ' последнее живое значение из зеркала ISTO_p -> крайний 100; диапазон LIM_P;
        ' alarm: Log при каждом гашении (kerr -795/-796)
        sb = sb & "if (double.IsNaN(" & p & ")||Math.Abs(" & p & ")>1e300) {" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  if ((NaN_in[" & n & "]==0) && (NaN_seq<1000)) { NaN_seq++; NaN_in[" & n & "]=NaN_seq; NaN_nm[NaN_seq]="" & P & ""; NaN_uz[NaN_seq]=" & n & """; NaN_tp[NaN_seq]=1; }" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  GR_val=" & p & ";" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  if (!(double.IsNaN(ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "])) && (Math.Abs(ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "])<=1e300) && (ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "]>1e-9)) {" & vbCrLf
        If CG4_Simple(p) Then sb = sb & "    " & p & "=ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "];" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "    aa2=ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "];" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  } else {" & vbCrLf
        If CG4_Simple(p) Then sb = sb & "    " & p & "=100;" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "    aa2=100;" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  }" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  GR_ev++; GR_c1++;" & vbCrLf
        nGR_cg = nGR_cg + 1
        sb = sb & "  if ((GROFF==0) && (GR_rep[" & CStr(n) & "]==0)) {" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "    GR_rep[" & CStr(n) & "]=1;" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "    Log("" LH1 guard: узел #"" + " & CStr(n) & " + "" - " & p & " NaN/бред, v=""+cstrf(GR_val,3));  kerr = -795;" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  }" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "} else if ((LIM_P_max>0) && (((" & p & ")<LIM_P_min) || ((" & p & ")>LIM_P_max))) {" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  if ((NaN_in[" & n & "]==0) && (NaN_seq<1000)) { NaN_seq++; NaN_in[" & n & "]=NaN_seq; NaN_nm[NaN_seq]=""диапазон: "" & P & ""; NaN_uz[NaN_seq]=" & n & """; NaN_tp[NaN_seq]=1; }" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  GR_val=" & p & ";" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  if (!(double.IsNaN(ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "])) && (Math.Abs(ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "])<=1e300) && (ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "]>1e-9)) {" & vbCrLf
        If CG4_Simple(p) Then sb = sb & "    " & p & "=ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "];" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "    aa2=ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "];" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  } else {" & vbCrLf
        If CG4_Simple(p) Then sb = sb & "    " & p & "=100;" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "    aa2=100;" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  }" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  GR_ev++; GR_c2++;" & vbCrLf
        nGR_cg = nGR_cg + 1
        sb = sb & "  if ((GROFF==0) && (GR_rep[" & CStr(n) & "]==0)) {" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "    GR_rep[" & CStr(n) & "]=1;" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "    Log("" LH1 guard: узел #"" + " & CStr(n) & " + "" - " & p & " вне диапазона, v=""+cstrf(GR_val,3));  kerr = -796;" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  }" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "}" & vbCrLf
        totB = totB + 1

        If Len(bCond_cg(m)) > 0 Then
            sb = sb & "if (" & bCond_cg(m) & ") { NGFix[" & r & "] = 0; } else { NGFix[" & r & "] = 1; }" & vbCrLf
        Else
            sb = sb & "if (" & fm & " * " & gt & " < 100 * e27) { NGFix[" & r & "] = 1; } else { NGFix[" & r & "] = 0; }" & vbCrLf
        End If
        sb = sb & "NGP0[" & r & "] = " & p & " + INT_PRT - 1;  NGPmax[" & r & "] = pmax;  NGPmin[" & r & "] = pmin;  NGNmax[" & r & "] = n_max;  NGaa05[" & r & "] = aa05;  kerr = 530000 + " & n & ";" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "if (NGFix[" & r & "] < 1) {" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  for (NGj2 = 1; NGj2 <= " & nSolver & "; NGj2++) { NGJ[" & r & ",NGj2] = 0; }" & vbCrLf
        For j = 0 To cntL - 1
            CG4_SplitLink CStr(links(j)), kk, ps, pn, nb
            dpl = "dPL_r[" & n & "," & kk & "]"
            sb = sb & "  V_r[" & kk & "] = 1;  if (aa2 + " & dpl & " - " & nb & " < 0) V_r[" & kk & "] = -1;" & vbCrLf
            sb = sb & "  dP_r[" & kk & "] = (1 - " & ps & " * (1 - " & pn & ")) * Math.Pow(Math.Abs(aa2 + " & dpl & " - " & nb & "), 0.5);  kerr = 531000 + " & n & "*10 + " & kk & ";" & vbCrLf
            termIn = termIn & IIf(Len(termIn) > 0, "+", "") & "U_ri[" & n & "," & kk & "]*dP_r[" & kk & "]*(1-V_r[" & kk & "])/2"
            termOut = termOut & IIf(Len(termOut) > 0, "+", "") & "U_ri[" & n & "," & kk & "]*dP_r[" & kk & "]*(1+V_r[" & kk & "])/2"
            termDiag = termDiag & IIf(Len(termDiag) > 0, "+", "") & "U_ri[" & n & "," & kk & "]*(1-" & ps & "*(1-" & pn & "))*0.5/Math.Pow(Math.Abs(aa2 + " & dpl & " - " & nb & ")+1e-6,0.5)"
            termNg3 = termNg3 & IIf(Len(termNg3) > 0, "+", "") & "U_ri[" & n & "," & kk & "]"
        Next j
        sb = sb & "  S_in = K_fff*(" & termIn & ");" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  S_out = K_fff*(" & termOut & ");" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  aa1 = K_svod*(S_out - S_in)/K_fff;" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  NGR[" & r & "] = -aa1;" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  NGJ[" & r & "," & r & "] = " & termDiag & ";" & vbCrLf
        For j = 0 To cntL - 1
            CG4_SplitLink CStr(links(j)), kk, ps, pn, nb
            dpl = "dPL_r[" & n & "," & kk & "]"
            If colByNode_cg.Exists(nb) Then
                colv = colByNode_cg(nb)
                sb = sb & "  NGJ[" & r & "," & colv & "] = NGJ[" & r & "," & colv & "] - U_ri[" & n & "," & kk & "]*(1-" & ps & "*(1-" & pn & "))*0.5/Math.Pow(Math.Abs(aa2 + " & dpl & " - " & nb & ")+1e-6,0.5);" & vbCrLf
            End If
        Next j
        sb = sb & "  NGaa3[" & r & "] = (e27 + (" & termNg3 & "))*XX0[55]/10;  kerr = 539000 + " & n & ";" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "}" & vbCrLf
    Else
        sb = sb & "// CG4-B: сборка вклада для " & p & " (#" & n & ", строка " & r & ")" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "aa2:=" & p & "+INT_PRT-1;" & vbCrLf
        ' LH1 v21/CG4 v3: страж входа в сборке - ВСЕГДА; РЕФОРМА ГАШЕНИЙ:
        ' последнее живое значение из зеркала ISTO_p -> крайний 100; диапазон LIM_P;
        ' alarm: Log при каждом гашении (kerr -795/-796)
        sb = sb & "if IsNan(" & p & ") or (Abs(" & p & ")>1e300) then begin" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  if (NaN_in[" & n & "]=0) and (NaN_seq<1000) then begin NaN_seq:=NaN_seq+1; NaN_in[" & n & "]:=NaN_seq; NaN_nm[NaN_seq]:='" & p & "'; NaN_uz[NaN_seq]:=" & CStr(n) & "; NaN_tp[NaN_seq]:=1; end;" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  GR_val:=" & p & ";" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  if (Not IsNan(ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "])) and (Abs(ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "])<=1e300) and (ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "]>1e-9) then begin" & vbCrLf
        If CG4_Simple(p) Then sb = sb & "    " & p & ":=ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "];" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "    aa2:=ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "];" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  end else begin" & vbCrLf
        If CG4_Simple(p) Then sb = sb & "    " & p & ":=100;" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "    aa2:=100;" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  end;" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  GR_ev:=GR_ev+1; GR_c1:=GR_c1+1;" & vbCrLf
        nGR_cg = nGR_cg + 1
        sb = sb & "  if (GROFF=0) and (GR_rep[" & CStr(n) & "]=0) then begin" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "    GR_rep[" & CStr(n) & "]:=1;" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "    Log(' LH1 guard: узел #" & CStr(n) & " - " & p & " NaN/бред, v='+cstrf(GR_val,3));  kerr:= -795;" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  end;" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "end else if ((LIM_P_max>0) and (((" & p & ")<LIM_P_min) or ((" & p & ")>LIM_P_max))) then begin" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  if (NaN_in[" & n & "]=0) and (NaN_seq<1000) then begin NaN_seq:=NaN_seq+1; NaN_in[" & n & "]:=NaN_seq; NaN_nm[NaN_seq]:='диапазон: " & p & "'; NaN_uz[NaN_seq]:=" & CStr(n) & "; NaN_tp[NaN_seq]:=1; end;" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  GR_val:=" & p & ";" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  if (Not IsNan(ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "])) and (Abs(ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "])<=1e300) and (ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "]>1e-9) then begin" & vbCrLf
        If CG4_Simple(p) Then sb = sb & "    " & p & ":=ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "];" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "    aa2:=ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "];" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  end else begin" & vbCrLf
        If CG4_Simple(p) Then sb = sb & "    " & p & ":=100;" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "    aa2:=100;" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  end;" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  GR_ev:=GR_ev+1; GR_c2:=GR_c2+1;" & vbCrLf
        nGR_cg = nGR_cg + 1
        sb = sb & "  if (GROFF=0) and (GR_rep[" & CStr(n) & "]=0) then begin" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "    GR_rep[" & CStr(n) & "]:=1;" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "    Log(' LH1 guard: узел #" & CStr(n) & " - " & p & " вне диапазона, v='+cstrf(GR_val,3));  kerr:= -796;" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  end;" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "end;" & vbCrLf
        totB = totB + 1

        If Len(bCond_cg(m)) > 0 Then
            sb = sb & "if (" & bCond_cg(m) & ") then NGFix[" & r & "]:=0 else NGFix[" & r & "]:=1;" & vbCrLf
        Else
            sb = sb & "if (" & fm & "*" & gt & "<100*e27) then NGFix[" & r & "]:=1 else NGFix[" & r & "]:=0;" & vbCrLf
        End If
        sb = sb & "NGP0[" & r & "]:=" & p & "+INT_PRT-1;  NGPmax[" & r & "]:=pmax;  NGPmin[" & r & "]:=pmin;  NGNmax[" & r & "]:=n_max;  NGaa05[" & r & "]:=aa05;  kerr:= 530000+" & n & ";" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "if (NGFix[" & r & "]<1) then" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "begin" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  NGj2:=1;  while (NGj2<=" & nSolver & ") do begin NGJ[" & r & ",NGj2]:=0; NGj2:=NGj2+1; end;" & vbCrLf
        For j = 0 To cntL - 1
            CG4_SplitLink CStr(links(j)), kk, ps, pn, nb
            dpl = "dPL_r[" & n & "," & kk & "]"
            sb = sb & "  V_r[" & kk & "]:=1;  if (aa2+" & dpl & "-" & nb & "<0) then V_r[" & kk & "]:=-1;" & vbCrLf
            sb = sb & "  dP_r[" & kk & "]:=(1-" & ps & "*(1-" & pn & "))*Power(Abs(aa2+" & dpl & "-" & nb & "),0.5);  kerr:= 531000+" & n & "*10+" & kk & ";" & vbCrLf
            termIn = termIn & IIf(Len(termIn) > 0, "+", "") & "U_ri[" & n & "," & kk & "]*dP_r[" & kk & "]*(1-V_r[" & kk & "])/2"
            termOut = termOut & IIf(Len(termOut) > 0, "+", "") & "U_ri[" & n & "," & kk & "]*dP_r[" & kk & "]*(1+V_r[" & kk & "])/2"
            termDiag = termDiag & IIf(Len(termDiag) > 0, "+", "") & "U_ri[" & n & "," & kk & "]*(1-" & ps & "*(1-" & pn & "))*0.5/Power(Abs(aa2+" & dpl & "-" & nb & ")+1e-6,0.5)"
            termNg3 = termNg3 & IIf(Len(termNg3) > 0, "+", "") & "U_ri[" & n & "," & kk & "]"
        Next j
        sb = sb & "  S_in:=K_fff*(" & termIn & ");" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  S_out:=K_fff*(" & termOut & ");" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  aa1:=K_svod*(S_out-S_in)/K_fff;" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  NGR[" & r & "]:=-aa1;" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "  NGJ[" & r & "," & r & "]:=" & termDiag & ";" & vbCrLf
        For j = 0 To cntL - 1
            CG4_SplitLink CStr(links(j)), kk, ps, pn, nb
            dpl = "dPL_r[" & n & "," & kk & "]"
            If colByNode_cg.Exists(nb) Then
                colv = colByNode_cg(nb)
                sb = sb & "  NGJ[" & r & "," & colv & "]:=NGJ[" & r & "," & colv & "]-U_ri[" & n & "," & kk & "]*(1-" & ps & "*(1-" & pn & "))*0.5/Power(Abs(aa2+" & dpl & "-" & nb & ")+1e-6,0.5);" & vbCrLf
            End If
        Next j
        sb = sb & "  NGaa3[" & r & "]:=(e27+(" & termNg3 & "))*XX0[55]/10;  kerr:= 539000+" & n & ";" & vbCrLf
        sb = sb & "end;" & vbCrLf
    End If
    CG4_BuildAssembly = sb
End Function

Private Sub CG4_SplitLink(ByVal s As String, ByRef kk As Long, ByRef ps As String, ByRef pn As String, ByRef nb As String)
    Dim a As Variant
    a = Split(s, "|")
    kk = Val(a(0))
    ps = a(1): pn = a(2): nb = a(3)
End Sub

' ============================================================================
'  CG4-S: РЕШАТЕЛЬ НЬЮТОН (1 шаг за тик) + CG4-D: ПРИМЕНЕНИЯ
' ============================================================================
Private Sub CG4_SolverTail(ByVal f As Integer, ByVal nSolver As Long, ByVal hasLog As Boolean, ByVal csh As Boolean)
    Dim n As String
    n = CStr(nSolver)
    If csh Then
        Print #f, "// CG4-S: решатель Ньютон (1 шаг за тик), N=" & n
        Print #f, "Krelax = 1.15;   // Ф37.2/P2: lambda=1/1.15~0.87 (preset; was 1.0)"
        Print #f, "for (NGi2 = 1; NGi2 <= " & n & "; NGi2++)"
        Print #f, "  if (NGFix[NGi2] > 0) {"
        Print #f, "    for (NGj2 = 1; NGj2 <= " & n & "; NGj2++) { NGJ[NGi2,NGj2] = 0; NGJ[NGj2,NGi2] = 0; }"
        Print #f, "    NGJ[NGi2,NGi2] = 1; NGR[NGi2] = 0;"
        Print #f, "  }"
        Print #f, "for (NGi2 = 1; NGi2 <= " & n & "; NGi2++) {"
        Print #f, "  NGpiv = NGi2; NGmv = Math.Abs(NGJ[NGi2,NGi2]);"
        Print #f, "  for (NGk2 = NGi2 + 1; NGk2 <= " & n & "; NGk2++) if (Math.Abs(NGJ[NGk2,NGi2]) > NGmv) { NGpiv = NGk2; NGmv = Math.Abs(NGJ[NGk2,NGi2]); }"
        Print #f, "  if (NGpiv != NGi2) {"
        Print #f, "    for (NGj2 = NGi2; NGj2 <= " & n & "; NGj2++) { NGtmp = NGJ[NGi2,NGj2]; NGJ[NGi2,NGj2] = NGJ[NGpiv,NGj2]; NGJ[NGpiv,NGj2] = NGtmp; }"
        Print #f, "    NGtmp = NGR[NGi2]; NGR[NGi2] = NGR[NGpiv]; NGR[NGpiv] = NGtmp;"
        Print #f, "  }"
        Print #f, "  if (Math.Abs(NGJ[NGi2,NGi2]) < 1e-300) NGJ[NGi2,NGi2] = 1e-300;"
        Print #f, "  for (NGk2 = NGi2 + 1; NGk2 <= " & n & "; NGk2++) {"
        Print #f, "    NGm = NGJ[NGk2,NGi2]/NGJ[NGi2,NGi2];"
        Print #f, "    if (NGm != 0) {"
        Print #f, "      for (NGj2 = NGi2 + 1; NGj2 <= " & n & "; NGj2++) NGJ[NGk2,NGj2] = NGJ[NGk2,NGj2] - NGm*NGJ[NGi2,NGj2];"
        Print #f, "      NGR[NGk2] = NGR[NGk2] - NGm*NGR[NGi2];"
        Print #f, "    }"
        Print #f, "  }"
        Print #f, "}"
        Print #f, "for (NGi2 = " & n & "; NGi2 >= 1; NGi2--) {"
        Print #f, "  NGs = NGR[NGi2];"
        Print #f, "  for (NGj2 = NGi2 + 1; NGj2 <= " & n & "; NGj2++) NGs = NGs - NGJ[NGi2,NGj2]*NGX[NGj2];"
        Print #f, "  NGX[NGi2] = NGs/NGJ[NGi2,NGi2];"
        Print #f, "}"
        Print #f, "// релаксация 0.9 + кламп окна (семантика pmax/pmin метода 2)"
        Print #f, "for (NGi2 = 1; NGi2 <= " & n & "; NGi2++) {"
        Print #f, "  if (Krelax < 1) Krelax = 1;"
        Print #f, "  NGX[NGi2] = NGP0[NGi2] + NGX[NGi2]/Krelax;"
        Print #f, "  if (NGX[NGi2] > NGP0[NGi2]+CG4_DP_MAX) NGX[NGi2] = NGP0[NGi2]+CG4_DP_MAX;   // Ф40: trust-region"
        Print #f, "  if (NGX[NGi2] < NGP0[NGi2]-CG4_DP_MAX) NGX[NGi2] = NGP0[NGi2]-CG4_DP_MAX;   // Ф40"
        Print #f, "  if (NGX[NGi2] > LIM_P_max) NGX[NGi2] = LIM_P_max;   // Ф37.1: физ.страховка (остаётся)"
        Print #f, "  if (NGX[NGi2] < LIM_P_min) NGX[NGi2] = LIM_P_min;"
        Print #f, "}"
        If hasLog Then Print #f, "Log(""markN2 solve N=" & n & " iter=1 rel=0.9"");"
    Else
        Print #f, "// CG4-S: решатель Ньютон (1 шаг за тик), N=" & n
        Print #f, "Krelax:=1.15;   // Ф37.2/P2: lambda=1/1.15~0.87 (пресет; было 1.0 - перелёты -> осцилляция)"
        Print #f, "NGi2:=1;"
        Print #f, "while (NGi2<=" & n & ") do begin"
        Print #f, "  if (NGFix[NGi2]>0) then begin"
        Print #f, "    NGj2:=1;"
        Print #f, "    while (NGj2<=" & n & ") do begin NGJ[NGi2,NGj2]:=0; NGJ[NGj2,NGi2]:=0; NGj2:=NGj2+1; end;"
        Print #f, "    NGJ[NGi2,NGi2]:=1; NGR[NGi2]:=0;"
        Print #f, "  end;"
        Print #f, "  NGi2:=NGi2+1;"
        Print #f, "end;"
        Print #f, "NGi2:=1;"
        Print #f, "while (NGi2<=" & n & ") do begin"
        Print #f, "  NGpiv:=NGi2; NGmv:=Abs(NGJ[NGi2,NGi2]);"
        Print #f, "  NGk2:=NGi2+1;"
        Print #f, "  while (NGk2<=" & n & ") do begin"
        Print #f, "    if (Abs(NGJ[NGk2,NGi2])>NGmv) then begin NGpiv:=NGk2; NGmv:=Abs(NGJ[NGk2,NGi2]); end;"
        Print #f, "    NGk2:=NGk2+1;"
        Print #f, "  end;"
        Print #f, "  if (NGpiv<>NGi2) then begin"
        Print #f, "    NGj2:=NGi2;"
        Print #f, "    while (NGj2<=" & n & ") do begin NGtmp:=NGJ[NGi2,NGj2]; NGJ[NGi2,NGj2]:=NGJ[NGpiv,NGj2]; NGJ[NGpiv,NGj2]:=NGtmp; NGj2:=NGj2+1; end;"
        Print #f, "    NGtmp:=NGR[NGi2]; NGR[NGi2]:=NGR[NGpiv]; NGR[NGpiv]:=NGtmp;"
        Print #f, "  end;"
        Print #f, "  if (Abs(NGJ[NGi2,NGi2])<1e-300) then NGJ[NGi2,NGi2]:=1e-300;"
        Print #f, "  NGk2:=NGi2+1;"
        Print #f, "  while (NGk2<=" & n & ") do begin"
        Print #f, "    NGm:=NGJ[NGk2,NGi2]/NGJ[NGi2,NGi2];"
        Print #f, "    if (NGm<>0) then begin"
        Print #f, "      NGj2:=NGi2+1;"
        Print #f, "      while (NGj2<=" & n & ") do begin NGJ[NGk2,NGj2]:=NGJ[NGk2,NGj2]-NGm*NGJ[NGi2,NGj2]; NGj2:=NGj2+1; end;"
        Print #f, "      NGR[NGk2]:=NGR[NGk2]-NGm*NGR[NGi2];"
        Print #f, "    end;"
        Print #f, "    NGk2:=NGk2+1;"
        Print #f, "  end;"
        Print #f, "  NGi2:=NGi2+1;"
        Print #f, "end;"
        Print #f, "NGi2:=" & n & ";"
        Print #f, "while (NGi2>=1) do begin"
        Print #f, "  NGs:=NGR[NGi2];"
        Print #f, "  NGj2:=NGi2+1;"
        Print #f, "  while (NGj2<=" & n & ") do begin NGs:=NGs-NGJ[NGi2,NGj2]*NGX[NGj2]; NGj2:=NGj2+1; end;"
        Print #f, "  NGX[NGi2]:=NGs/NGJ[NGi2,NGi2];"
        Print #f, "  NGi2:=NGi2-1;"
        Print #f, "end;"
        Print #f, "// релаксация 0.9 + кламп окна (семантика pmax/pmin метода 2)"
        Print #f, "NGi2:=1;"
        Print #f, "while (NGi2<=" & n & ") do begin"
        Print #f, "  if (Krelax<1) then Krelax:=1;"
        Print #f, "  NGX[NGi2]:=NGP0[NGi2]+NGX[NGi2]/Krelax;"
        Print #f, "  if (NGX[NGi2]>NGP0[NGi2]+CG4_DP_MAX) then NGX[NGi2]:=NGP0[NGi2]+CG4_DP_MAX;   // Ф40: trust-region шага (ати/подскан)"
        Print #f, "  if (NGX[NGi2]<NGP0[NGi2]-CG4_DP_MAX) then NGX[NGi2]:=NGP0[NGi2]-CG4_DP_MAX;   // Ф40"
        Print #f, "  if (NGX[NGi2]>LIM_P_max) then NGX[NGi2]:=LIM_P_max;   // Ф37.1: физ.страховка (остаётся)"
        Print #f, "  if (NGX[NGi2]<LIM_P_min) then NGX[NGi2]:=LIM_P_min;"
        Print #f, "  NGi2:=NGi2+1;"
        Print #f, "end;"
        If hasLog Then Print #f, "Log('markN2 solve N=" & n & " iter=1 rel=0.9');"
    End If
End Sub

Private Sub CG4_Apply(ByVal f As Integer, ByVal m As Long, ByVal r As Long, ByVal csh As Boolean)
    Dim p As String, n As Long
    Dim links As Variant, cntL As Long, j As Long
    Dim kk As Long, ps As String, pn As String, nb As String
    Dim dpl As String
    Dim cutIdx As Long, i As Long

    p = bP_cg(m): n = bN_cg(m)
    If csh Then
        Print #f, "// CG4-D: применение для " & p & " (#" & n & ")"
        Print #f, "aa2 = NGX[" & r & "];  pmax = NGPmax[" & r & "];  pmin = NGPmin[" & r & "];  n_max = NGNmax[" & r & "];  aa05 = NGaa05[" & r & "];  kerr = 540000 + " & n & ";"
        links = Split(bLinks_cg(m), ";")
        For j = 0 To UBound(links)
            CG4_SplitLink CStr(links(j)), kk, ps, pn, nb
            dpl = "dPL_r[" & n & "," & kk & "]"
            Print #f, "V_r[" & kk & "] = 1;  if (aa2 + " & dpl & " - " & nb & " < 0) V_r[" & kk & "] = -1;"
        Next j
    Else
        Print #f, "// CG4-D: применение для " & p & " (#" & n & ")"
        Print #f, "aa2:=NGX[" & r & "];  pmax:=NGPmax[" & r & "];  pmin:=NGPmin[" & r & "];  n_max:=NGNmax[" & r & "];  aa05:=NGaa05[" & r & "];  kerr:= 540000+" & n & ";"
        links = Split(bLinks_cg(m), ";")
        For j = 0 To UBound(links)
            CG4_SplitLink CStr(links(j)), kk, ps, pn, nb
            dpl = "dPL_r[" & n & "," & kk & "]"
            Print #f, "V_r[" & kk & "]:=1;  if (aa2+" & dpl & "-" & nb & "<0) then V_r[" & kk & "]:=-1;"
        Next j
    End If
    ' хвост: до ПЕРВОГО закрывающего end НА ГЛУБИНЕ 0 (v2j: в м2+LH1-входе в хвостах
    ' живут v20-стражи с begin..end - резать можно только end самой математики блока)
    Dim dpth As Long
    dpth = 0
    cutIdx = bTail_cg(m).Count + 1
    For i = 1 To bTail_cg(m).Count
        If dpth = 0 And Left$(Trim$(bTail_cg(m)(i)), 3) = "end" Then cutIdx = i: Exit For
        dpth = dpth + CG4_CountBeginEnd2(bTail_cg(m)(i))
    Next i
    For i = 1 To cutIdx - 1
        Print #f, bTail_cg(m)(i)
    Next i
End Sub

' ============================================================================
'  ДЕКЛАРАЦИИ NG И NaN-БЛОК «НУЛЕВОГО ПАЦИЕНТА»
' ============================================================================
Private Sub CG4_NgDecls(ByVal f As Integer, ByVal n As Long, ByVal csh As Boolean)
    If csh Then
        Print #f, "// CG4_OWNED_DECLS: декларации Ньютон-решателя (вставлено CG4_Convert, VBA)"
        Print #f, "double[,] NGJ = new double[" & n + 1 & "," & n + 1 & "];"
        Print #f, "double[] NGR = new double[" & n + 1 & "], NGX = new double[" & n + 1 & "], NGP0 = new double[" & n + 1 & "], NGPmax = new double[" & n + 1 & "], NGPmin = new double[" & n + 1 & "], NGaa05 = new double[" & n + 1 & "], NGaa3 = new double[" & n + 1 & "];"
        Print #f, "int[] NGNmax = new int[" & n + 1 & "]; // v4: NGNmax Double->Integer (номер узла, не счётчик)"
        Print #f, "int[] NGFix = new int[" & n + 1 & "];"
        Print #f, "int NGi2, NGj2, NGk2, NGpiv; double NGmv, NGm, NGs, NGtmp;"
        Print #f, "double Krelax;"
        Print #f, "double nCond; // v7 (R3): counter of evaporate/condense conditions (was XX0[7])"
        Print #f, "double CG4_DP_MAX; // Ф40 (v13): trust-region radius of the Newton step"
    Else
        Print #f, "// CG4_OWNED_DECLS: декларации Ньютон-решателя (вставлено CG4_Convert, VBA)"
        Print #f, "NGJ: array[1.." & n & ",1.." & n & "] of Double;"
        Print #f, "NGR, NGX, NGP0, NGPmax, NGPmin, NGaa05, NGaa3: array[1.." & n & "] of Double;"
        Print #f, "NGNmax: array[1.." & n & "] of Integer; // v4: NGNmax Double->Integer (номер узла, не счётчик)"
        Print #f, "NGFix: array[1.." & n & "] of Integer;"
        Print #f, "NGi2, NGj2, NGk2, NGpiv: Integer; NGmv, NGm, NGs, NGtmp: Double;"
        Print #f, "Krelax: Double;"
        Print #f, "nCond: Double; // v7 (R3): счётчик условий испарения/конденсации (был в XX0[7])"
        Print #f, "CG4_DP_MAX: Double; // Ф40 (v13): радиус доверительной области ньютон-шага (ати/подскан)"
    End If
End Sub

' --- v2: ключи IN_OFF/IN_ON в начало WorkIn (после try).
' Ячейки читаем НАПРЯМУЮ (main.bph.param9/param10.ValS), как FIRE_1/K_tau= в _0.WorkAdd, -
' срабатывает на ПЕРВОМ скане, т.к. блок лога (WorkLog1..BU) вызывается ПОСЛЕ In.
Private Sub CG4_InKeys(ByVal f As Integer, ByVal csh As Boolean)
    Dim sp As Variant, i As Long
    If csh Then
            sp = Array("// LH1-IN: ключи IN_OFF/IN_ON (ячейки лога 9/10) - читаем напрямую, чтобы сработать на первом скане", _
            "IN_str=main.bph.param9.ValS.replace("" "","""");", _
            "if (IN_str==""IN_OFF"") INOFF=1;", _
            "if (IN_str==""IN_ON"") INOFF=0;", _
            "IN_str=main.bph.param10.ValS.replace("" "","""");", _
            "if (IN_str==""IN_OFF"") INOFF=1;", _
            "if (IN_str==""IN_ON"") INOFF=0;", _
            "if (INOFF!=INOFF_prev) {", _
            "  if (INOFF==1) Log("" IN: In-данные заказчика ОТКЛЮЧЕНЫ (IN_OFF) - пресеты нашей математики, арматуры активны"");", _
            "  if (INOFF==0) Log("" IN: In-данные заказчика ВКЛЮЧЕНЫ (IN_ON)"");", _
            "  INOFF_prev=INOFF;", _
            "}")
    Else
            sp = Array("// LH1-IN: ключи IN_OFF/IN_ON (ячейки лога 9/10) - читаем напрямую, чтобы сработать на первом скане", _
            "IN_str:=main.bph.param9.ValS.replace(' ','');", _
            "if (IN_str='IN_OFF') then INOFF:=1;", _
            "if (IN_str='IN_ON') then INOFF:=0;", _
            "IN_str:=main.bph.param10.ValS.replace(' ','');", _
            "if (IN_str='IN_OFF') then INOFF:=1;", _
            "if (IN_str='IN_ON') then INOFF:=0;", _
            "if (INOFF<>INOFF_prev) then begin", _
            "  if (INOFF=1) then begin Log(' IN: In-данные заказчика ОТКЛЮЧЕНЫ (IN_OFF) - пресеты нашей математики, арматуры активны'); kerr:= -800; end;", _
            "  if (INOFF=0) then begin Log(' IN: In-данные заказчика ВКЛЮЧЕНЫ (IN_ON)'); kerr:= -801; end;", _
            "  INOFF_prev:=INOFF;", _
            "end;", _
            "kerr:= -804;")
    End If
    For i = LBound(sp) To UBound(sp)
        Print #f, sp(i)
    Next i
End Sub

' --- v2: классификация In-строки WorkIn (как в LH1 v7/v8): арматура KOD 02 и KOD 05-08;
' гейт KOD 01,03,04,09,12,13,14,17,18 с kerr>=250006; KOD 00 - не гейтится.
' Возвращает 1=гейт (gated=строка-гейт), 2=арматура, 0=прочее.
Private Function CG4_TryGate(ByVal ln As String, ByVal csh As Boolean, ByRef gated As String) As Long
    Dim p1 As Long, p2 As Long, p3 As Long, kod As Long, ke As Long
    CG4_TryGate = 0: gated = ""
    p1 = InStr(ln, "kerr:=")
    If p1 <= 0 Then Exit Function
    p2 = InStr(p1, ln, "//")
    If p2 <= 0 Then Exit Function
    p3 = InStr(p2, ln, "KOD:")
    If p3 <= 0 Then Exit Function
    kod = Val(Mid$(ln, p3 + 4, 3))
    ke = Val(Mid$(ln, p1 + 6, 8))
    If kod = 2 Or (kod >= 5 And kod <= 8) Then CG4_TryGate = 2: Exit Function
    If kod < 1 Or kod > 18 Then Exit Function
    If ke < 250006 Then Exit Function
    If csh Then
        gated = "if (INOFF==0) " & Trim$(ln)
    Else
        gated = "if (INOFF=0) then " & Trim$(ln)
    End If
    CG4_TryGate = 1
End Function

' --- v2: IN-гейты в HEALTH-юнит (WorkLogBU) после «У И: n=», перед NaN-сканом.
Private Sub CG4_InLogGates(ByVal f As Integer, ByVal csh As Boolean)
    Dim sp As Variant, i As Long
    If csh Then
            sp = Array("// LH1-IN: команды IN_OFF/IN_ON из лог-ячеек 9/10 (принцип HEALTH)", _
            "if (LOG0[9]==""IN_OFF"") INOFF=1;", _
            "if (LOG0[10]==""IN_OFF"") INOFF=1;", _
            "if (LOG0[9]==""IN_ON"") INOFF=0;", _
            "if (LOG0[10]==""IN_ON"") INOFF=0;", _
            "// Ф41: ключи GR_OFF/GR_ON (лог-ячейка 11) - печать стражей вкл/выкл без пересборки", _
            "if (LOG0[11]==""GR_OFF"") GROFF=1;", _
            "if (LOG0[11]==""GR_ON"") GROFF=0;")
    Else
            sp = Array("// LH1-IN: команды IN_OFF/IN_ON из лог-ячеек 9/10 (принцип HEALTH)", _
            "if (LOG0[9]='IN_OFF') then INOFF:=1;  kerr:= -802;", _
            "if (LOG0[10]='IN_OFF') then INOFF:=1;  kerr:= -803;", _
            "if (LOG0[9]='IN_ON') then INOFF:=0;  kerr:= -804;", _
            "if (LOG0[10]='IN_ON') then INOFF:=0;  kerr:= -805;", _
            "// Ф41: ключи GR_OFF/GR_ON (лог-ячейка 11) - печать стражей вкл/выкл без пересборки", _
            "if (LOG0[11]='GR_OFF') then GROFF:=1;  kerr:= -806;", _
            "if (LOG0[11]='GR_ON') then GROFF:=0;  kerr:= -807;")
    End If
    For i = LBound(sp) To UBound(sp)
        Print #f, sp(i)
    Next i
End Sub

Private Sub CG4_NanDecls(ByVal f As Integer, ByVal SZ As Long, ByVal csh As Boolean, ByVal doNaN As Boolean, ByVal doIN As Boolean)
    ' К3 v2j: декларации НОВАЯ СХЕМА LH1 v20 (только давления) + IN-поля (если нужны)
    Dim sp As Variant, i As Long
    If csh Then
        If doNaN Then
            sp = Array("int[] NaN_t = new int[#SZ1#]; // LH1 v20: номер события по хвосту ISTO_p (0=чист)", _
            "int[] NaN_in = new int[#SZ1#]; // LH1 v20: номер события на входе узла (0=чист)", _
            "int NaN_seq; // LH1 v20: порядковый номер NaN/бред-события (первый = 1)", _
            "int NaN_call, NaN_cnt, NaN_i; // LH1 v20: счётчики скана", _
            "string[] NaN_nm = new string[1001]; // LH1 v20: имена виновников (по номеру события)", _
            "int[] NaN_uz = new int[1001]; // LH1 v20: узел события", _
            "int[] NaN_tp = new int[1001]; // LH1 v20: тип: 1=вход 2=хвост", _
            "string NaN_str = "";")
        ElseIf doIN Then
            sp = Array("int INOFF, INOFF_prev; // LH1-IN: 1=In-данные заказчика отключены (IN_OFF), 0=активны (IN_ON)", _
            "string IN_str = ""; // LH1-IN")
        End If
        If IsArray(sp) Then
            For i = LBound(sp) To UBound(sp)
                Print #f, Replace(Replace(sp(i), "#SZ#", CStr(SZ)), "#SZ1#", CStr(SZ + 1))
            Next i
        End If
        ' Ф41 (v13): лог-диета стражей - счётчики и дедуп-печати (всегда, независимо от doNaN/doIN)
        Print #f, "int GR_ev; // Ф41: события стражей за скан (сводка в NaN-скане)"
        Print #f, "int GR_c1, GR_c2, GR_c3, GR_c4; // Ф41: входNaN/входДиап/хвостNaN/хвостДиап"
        Print #f, "int[] GR_rep = new int[" & CStr(SZ + 1) & "]; // Ф41: печать один раз на узел за прогон"
        Print #f, "double GR_val; int GROFF; // Ф41: значение вне диапазона; ключ GR_OFF/GR_ON"
        If doNaN And doIN Then
            Print #f, "int INOFF, INOFF_prev; // LH1-IN: 1=In-данные заказчика отключены (IN_OFF), 0=активны (IN_ON)"
            Print #f, "string IN_str = ""; // LH1-IN"
        End If
    Else
        If doNaN Then
            sp = Array("NaN_t: array[1.. #SZ#] of integer; // LH1 v20: номер события по хвосту ISTO_p (0=чист)", _
            "NaN_in: array[1.. #SZ#] of integer; // LH1 v20: номер события на входе узла (0=чист)", _
            "NaN_seq: integer; // LH1 v20: порядковый номер NaN/бред-события (первый = 1)", _
            "NaN_call, NaN_cnt, NaN_i: integer; // LH1 v20: счётчики скана", _
            "NaN_nm: array[1..1000] of string; // LH1 v20: имена виновников (по номеру события)", _
            "NaN_uz: array[1..1000] of integer; // LH1 v20: узел события", _
            "NaN_tp: array[1..1000] of integer; // LH1 v20: тип: 1=вход 2=хвост", _
            "NaN_str: string; // LH1 v20")
        ElseIf doIN Then
            sp = Array("INOFF, INOFF_prev: integer; // LH1-IN: 1=In-данные заказчика отключены (IN_OFF), 0=активны (IN_ON)", _
            "IN_str: string; // LH1-IN")
        End If
        If IsArray(sp) Then
            For i = LBound(sp) To UBound(sp)
                Print #f, Replace(sp(i), "#SZ#", CStr(SZ))
            Next i
        End If
        ' Ф41 (v13): лог-диета стражей - счётчики и дедуп-печати (всегда, независимо от doNaN/doIN)
        Print #f, "GR_ev: integer; // Ф41: события стражей за скан (сводка в NaN-скане)"
        Print #f, "GR_c1, GR_c2, GR_c3, GR_c4: integer; // Ф41: входNaN/входДиап/хвостNaN/хвостДиап"
        Print #f, "GR_rep: array[1.. " & CStr(SZ) & "] of integer; // Ф41: печать один раз на узел за прогон (0=можно)"
        Print #f, "GR_val: double; // Ф41: значение вне диапазона (v= в первой печати)"
        Print #f, "GROFF: integer; // Ф41: 1=GR_OFF (печать стражей выключена), 0=GR_ON"
        If doNaN And doIN Then
            Print #f, "INOFF, INOFF_prev: integer; // LH1-IN: 1=In-данные заказчика отключены (IN_OFF), 0=активны (IN_ON)"
            Print #f, "IN_str: string; // LH1-IN"
        End If
    End If
End Sub


Private Sub CG4_NanScan(ByVal f As Integer, ByVal SZ As Long, ByVal pasStyle As Boolean)
    ' К3 v2j: HEALTH-скан НОВАЯ СХЕМА LH1 v20 (только давления): построчные виновники
    ' + контроль зеркал ISTO_p; kerr -790..-794
    Dim sp As Collection, i As Long
    Dim szv As String
    szv = CStr(SZ)
    Set sp = New Collection
    If pasStyle Then
        sp.Add "// LH1-NaN-scan start (v20, только давления)"
        ' Ф41 (v13): сводка событий стражей за минувший скан + сброс счётчиков скана
        sp.Add "if (GR_ev>0) then Log(' LH1 guard: событий='+cstrf(GR_ev,0)+' [входNaN='+cstrf(GR_c1,0)+' входДиап='+cstrf(GR_c2,0)+' хвостNaN='+cstrf(GR_c3,0)+' хвостДиап='+cstrf(GR_c4,0)+']');  kerr:= -799;"
        sp.Add "GR_ev:=0; GR_c1:=0; GR_c2:=0; GR_c3:=0; GR_c4:=0;"
        sp.Add "if (NaN_seq>0) then begin"
        sp.Add "  Log(' LH1 NaN-виновники (давления): всего='+cstrf(NaN_seq,0)+' - NaN/бред гашены');  kerr:= -790;"
        sp.Add "  NaN_i:=1;"
        sp.Add "  while (NaN_i<=NaN_seq) do begin"
        sp.Add "    if (NaN_i<31) then begin"
        sp.Add "      NaN_str:='';"
        sp.Add "      if (NaN_tp[NaN_i]=1) then NaN_str:='вход';"
        sp.Add "      if (NaN_tp[NaN_i]=2) then NaN_str:='хвост';"
        sp.Add "      if (NaN_uz[NaN_i]>0) then begin Log(' LH1 виновник №'+cstrf(NaN_i,0)+': узел #'+cstrf(NaN_uz[NaN_i],0)+' - '+NaN_nm[NaN_i]+' ('+NaN_str+')');  kerr:= -791; end;"
        sp.Add "      if (NaN_uz[NaN_i]=0) then begin Log(' LH1 виновник №'+cstrf(NaN_i,0)+': граница - '+NaN_nm[NaN_i]+' ('+NaN_str+')');  kerr:= -791; end;"
        sp.Add "    end else begin"
        sp.Add "      if (NaN_i=31) then begin Log(' LH1 ... всего '+cstrf(NaN_seq,0)+' событий - показаны первые 30');  kerr:= -792; end;"
        sp.Add "    end;"
        sp.Add "    NaN_i:=NaN_i+1;"
        sp.Add "  end;"
        sp.Add "end;"
        sp.Add "NaN_cnt:=0;"
        sp.Add "NaN_i:=1;"
        sp.Add "while (NaN_i<=" & szv & ") do begin"
        sp.Add "  if IsNan(ISTO_p[NaN_i]) or (Abs(ISTO_p[NaN_i])>1e300) then NaN_cnt:=NaN_cnt+1;"
        sp.Add "  NaN_i:=NaN_i+1;"
        sp.Add "end;"
        sp.Add "if (NaN_cnt=0) then begin NaN_seq:=0; NaN_i:=1; while (NaN_i<=" & szv & ") do begin NaN_t[NaN_i]:=0; NaN_i:=NaN_i+1; end; end;"
        sp.Add "// LH1 v24: реестр виновников - только текущий скан"
        sp.Add "if (NaN_cnt>0) then begin Log(' LH1 ВНИМАНИЕ: NaN/бред в зеркалах ISTO_p: '+cstrf(NaN_cnt,0)+' (прорвалось мимо стражей)');  kerr:= -793; end;"
        sp.Add "if (NaN_seq=0) and (NaN_cnt=0) then begin Log(' LH1 NaN-скан: чисто - NaN/бред не обнаружены (скан #'+cstrf(NaN_call,0)+')');  kerr:= -794; end;"
        sp.Add "NaN_call:=NaN_call+1;"
        sp.Add "// LH1-NaN-scan end"
    Else
        sp.Add "// LH1-NaN-scan start (v20, только давления)"
        ' Ф41 (v13): сводка событий стражей за минувший скан + сброс счётчиков скана
        sp.Add "if (GR_ev>0) Log("" LH1 guard: событий=""+cstrf(GR_ev,0)+"" [входNaN=""+cstrf(GR_c1,0)+"" входДиап=""+cstrf(GR_c2,0)+"" хвостNaN=""+cstrf(GR_c3,0)+"" хвостДиап=""+cstrf(GR_c4,0)+""]"");"
        sp.Add "GR_ev=0; GR_c1=0; GR_c2=0; GR_c3=0; GR_c4=0;"
        sp.Add "if (NaN_seq>0)"
        sp.Add "{"
        sp.Add "  Log("" LH1 NaN-виновники (давления): всего=""+cstrf(NaN_seq,0)+"" - NaN/бред гашены"");"
        sp.Add "  for (NaN_i=1; NaN_i<=NaN_seq; NaN_i++)"
        sp.Add "  {"
        sp.Add "    if (NaN_i<31)"
        sp.Add "    {"
        sp.Add "      NaN_str="""";"
        sp.Add "      if (NaN_tp[NaN_i]==1) NaN_str=""вход"";"
        sp.Add "      if (NaN_tp[NaN_i]==2) NaN_str=""хвост"";"
        sp.Add "      if (NaN_uz[NaN_i]>0) Log("" LH1 виновник №""+cstrf(NaN_i,0)+"": узел #""+cstrf(NaN_uz[NaN_i],0)+"" - ""+NaN_nm[NaN_i]+"" (""+NaN_str+"")"");"
        sp.Add "      else Log("" LH1 виновник №""+cstrf(NaN_i,0)+"": граница - ""+NaN_nm[NaN_i]+"" (""+NaN_str+"")"");"
        sp.Add "    }"
        sp.Add "    else"
        sp.Add "    {"
        sp.Add "      if (NaN_i==31) Log("" LH1 ... всего ""+cstrf(NaN_seq,0)+"" событий - показаны первые 30"");"
        sp.Add "    }"
        sp.Add "  }"
        sp.Add "}"
        sp.Add "NaN_cnt=0;"
        sp.Add "for (NaN_i=1; NaN_i<=" & szv & "; NaN_i++)"
        sp.Add "{"
        sp.Add "  if (double.IsNaN(ISTO_p[NaN_i]) || Math.Abs(ISTO_p[NaN_i])>1e300) NaN_cnt++;"
        sp.Add "}"
        sp.Add "if (NaN_cnt==0) { NaN_seq=0; for (NaN_i=1; NaN_i<=" & szv & "; NaN_i++) NaN_t[NaN_i]=0; }"
        sp.Add "// LH1 v24: реестр виновников - только текущий скан"
        sp.Add "if (NaN_cnt>0) Log("" LH1 ВНИМАНИЕ: NaN/бред в зеркалах ISTO_p: ""+cstrf(NaN_cnt,0)+"" (прорвалось мимо стражей)"");"
        sp.Add "if ((NaN_seq==0) && (NaN_cnt==0)) Log("" LH1 NaN-скан: чисто - NaN/бред не обнаружены (скан #""+cstrf(NaN_call,0)+"")"");"
        sp.Add "NaN_call++;"
        sp.Add "// LH1-NaN-scan end"
    End If
    For i = 1 To sp.Count
        Print #f, sp(i)
    Next i
End Sub


' Ф45 (v14): сводка событий стражей GR за минувший скан + сброс счётчиков - вызывается
'      ВСЕГДА на якоре "У И: n=". При doNaN сводку печатает CG4_NanScan (как раньше),
'      при doNaN=False (вход м2+LH1) - только CG4_GrScan.
Private Sub CG4_GrScan(ByVal f As Integer, ByVal pasStyle As Boolean)
    If pasStyle Then
        Print #f, "if (GR_ev>0) then Log(' LH1 guard: событий='+cstrf(GR_ev,0)+' [входNaN='+cstrf(GR_c1,0)+' входДиап='+cstrf(GR_c2,0)+' хвостNaN='+cstrf(GR_c3,0)+' хвостДиап='+cstrf(GR_c4,0)+']');  kerr:= -799;"
        Print #f, "GR_ev:=0; GR_c1:=0; GR_c2:=0; GR_c3:=0; GR_c4:=0;"
    Else
        Print #f, "if (GR_ev>0) Log("" LH1 guard: событий=""+cstrf(GR_ev,0)+"" [входNaN=""+cstrf(GR_c1,0)+"" входДиап=""+cstrf(GR_c2,0)+"" хвостNaN=""+cstrf(GR_c3,0)+"" хвостДиап=""+cstrf(GR_c4,0)+""]"");"
        Print #f, "GR_ev=0; GR_c1=0; GR_c2=0; GR_c3=0; GR_c4=0;"
    End If
End Sub
' --- LH1-стражи на строке-присвоении (вне заменённых сборок) ---
Private Sub CG4_LineGuards(ByVal f As Integer, ByVal ln As String, ByVal curN As Long, ByVal SZ As Long, _
                           ByRef totB As Long, ByRef totL As Long, ByRef totH As Long)
    ' К3 v2j: LH1 v20 (только давления): страж входа (aa2) и хвоста (ISTO_p) на
    ' нетронутых местах; связь dP_r НЕ стражится (решение владельца)
    Dim t As String, nm As String
    Dim p1 As Long, p2 As Long, nn As Long
    t = Trim$(ln)
    If Len(t) = 0 Then Exit Sub

    If InStr(t, "aa2") = 1 And InStr(ln, "INT_PRT-1") > 0 And curN > 0 And curN <= SZ Then     ' v2n: запись NaN_in[curN] при curN>SZ шла ЗА ГРАНИЦУ массива (порча памяти)
        nm = Left$(ln, InStr(ln, "INT_PRT-1") - 1)
        p2 = InStrRev(nm, "+")
        If p2 > 0 Then nm = Left$(nm, p2 - 1)
        p2 = InStrRev(nm, "=")
        If p2 > 0 Then nm = Trim$(Mid$(nm, p2 + 1))
        If Len(nm) > 0 Then
            Print #f, CG4_GuardIn(curN, nm)
            totB = totB + 1
        End If

    ElseIf InStr(t, "ISTO_p[") = 1 Then
        p1 = InStr(ln, "ISTO_p[") + 7
        p2 = InStr(p1, ln, "]")
        nn = Val(Trim$(Mid$(ln, p1, p2 - p1)))
        p1 = InStr(p2, ln, "=")
        nm = Trim$(Mid$(ln, p1 + 1))
        p2 = InStr(nm, ";")
        If p2 > 0 Then nm = Left$(nm, p2 - 1)
        nm = Trim$(nm)
        If nn > 0 And nn <= SZ And Len(nm) > 0 And (Mid$(nm, 1, 1) < "0" Or Mid$(nm, 1, 1) > "9") Then
            Print #f, CG4_GuardTail(nn, nm)
            totH = totH + 1
        End If
    End If
End Sub

' --- простой идентификатор (можно ли присваивать: буквы/цифры/_) ---
Private Function CG4_Simple(ByVal nm As String) As Boolean
    Dim i As Long, c As String
    CG4_Simple = False
    If Len(nm) = 0 Then Exit Function
    For i = 1 To Len(nm)
        c = Mid(nm, i, 1)
        If Not ((c >= "a" And c <= "z") Or (c >= "A" And c <= "Z") Or (c >= "0" And c <= "9") Or c = "_") Then Exit Function
    Next i
    CG4_Simple = True
End Function


' --- LH1 v21/CG4 v3: страж входа (нетронутые места) - активный, РЕФОРМА ГАШЕНИЙ ---
Private Function CG4_GuardIn(ByVal n As Long, ByVal nm As String) As String
    Dim r As String
    r = "if IsNan(" & nm & ") or (Abs(" & nm & ")>1e300) then begin" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  if (NaN_in[" & n & "]=0) and (NaN_seq<1000) then begin NaN_seq:=NaN_seq+1; NaN_in[" & n & "]:=NaN_seq; NaN_nm[NaN_seq]:='" & nm & "'; NaN_uz[NaN_seq]:=" & CStr(n) & "; NaN_tp[NaN_seq]:=1; end;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  GR_val:=" & nm & ";" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  if (Not IsNan(ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "])) and (Abs(ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "])<=1e300) and (ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "]>1e-9) then begin" & vbCrLf
    If CG4_Simple(nm) Then r = r & "    " & nm & ":=ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "];" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "    aa2:=ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "];" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  end else begin" & vbCrLf
    If CG4_Simple(nm) Then r = r & "    " & nm & ":=100;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "    aa2:=100;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  end;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  GR_ev:=GR_ev+1; GR_c1:=GR_c1+1;" & vbCrLf
    nGR_cg = nGR_cg + 1
    r = r & "  if (GROFF=0) and (GR_rep[" & CStr(n) & "]=0) then begin" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "    GR_rep[" & CStr(n) & "]:=1;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "    Log(' LH1 guard: узел #" & CStr(n) & " - " & nm & " NaN/бред, v='+cstrf(GR_val,3));  kerr:= -795;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  end;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "end else if ((LIM_P_max>0) and (((" & nm & ")<LIM_P_min) or ((" & nm & ")>LIM_P_max))) then begin" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  if (NaN_in[" & n & "]=0) and (NaN_seq<1000) then begin NaN_seq:=NaN_seq+1; NaN_in[" & n & "]:=NaN_seq; NaN_nm[NaN_seq]:='диапазон: " & nm & "'; NaN_uz[NaN_seq]:=" & CStr(n) & "; NaN_tp[NaN_seq]:=1; end;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  GR_val:=" & nm & ";" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  if (Not IsNan(ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "])) and (Abs(ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "])<=1e300) and (ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "]>1e-9) then begin" & vbCrLf
    If CG4_Simple(nm) Then r = r & "    " & nm & ":=ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "];" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "    aa2:=ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "];" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  end else begin" & vbCrLf
    If CG4_Simple(nm) Then r = r & "    " & nm & ":=100;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "    aa2:=100;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  end;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  GR_ev:=GR_ev+1; GR_c2:=GR_c2+1;" & vbCrLf
    nGR_cg = nGR_cg + 1
    r = r & "  if (GROFF=0) and (GR_rep[" & CStr(n) & "]=0) then begin" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "    GR_rep[" & CStr(n) & "]:=1;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "    Log(' LH1 guard: узел #" & CStr(n) & " - " & nm & " вне диапазона, v='+cstrf(GR_val,3));  kerr:= -796;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  end;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "end;"
    CG4_GuardIn = r
End Function


' --- LH1 v21/CG4 v3: страж хвоста зеркал - активный, ЗАМОРОЗКА (зеркало НЕ обновляется бредом) ---
Private Function CG4_GuardTail(ByVal n As Long, ByVal nm As String) As String
    Dim r As String
    r = "if IsNan(" & nm & ") or (Abs(" & nm & ")>1e300) then begin" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  if (NaN_t[" & n & "]=0) and (NaN_seq<1000) then begin NaN_seq:=NaN_seq+1; NaN_t[" & n & "]:=NaN_seq; NaN_nm[NaN_seq]:='" & nm & "'; NaN_uz[NaN_seq]:=" & CStr(n) & "; NaN_tp[NaN_seq]:=2; end;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  GR_val:=" & nm & ";" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  if (Not IsNan(ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "])) and (Abs(ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "])<=1e300) and (ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "]>1e-9) then begin" & vbCrLf
    If CG4_Simple(nm) Then r = r & "    " & nm & ":=ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "];" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "    GR_ev:=GR_ev+1; GR_c3:=GR_c3+1;" & vbCrLf
    nGR_cg = nGR_cg + 1
    r = r & "    if (GROFF=0) and (GR_rep[" & CStr(n) & "]=0) then begin" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "      GR_rep[" & CStr(n) & "]:=1;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "      Log(' LH1 guard: узел #" & CStr(n) & " - " & nm & " NaN/бред, v='+cstrf(GR_val,3));  kerr:= -797;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "    end;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  end else begin" & vbCrLf
    If CG4_Simple(nm) Then r = r & "    " & nm & ":=100;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "    ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "]:=100;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  end;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "end else if ((LIM_P_max>0) and (((" & nm & ")<LIM_P_min) or ((" & nm & ")>LIM_P_max))) then begin" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  if (NaN_t[" & n & "]=0) and (NaN_seq<1000) then begin NaN_seq:=NaN_seq+1; NaN_t[" & n & "]:=NaN_seq; NaN_nm[NaN_seq]:='диапазон: " & nm & "'; NaN_uz[NaN_seq]:=" & CStr(n) & "; NaN_tp[NaN_seq]:=2; end;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  GR_val:=" & nm & ";" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  if (Not IsNan(ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "])) and (Abs(ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "])<=1e300) and (ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "]>1e-9) then begin" & vbCrLf
    If CG4_Simple(nm) Then r = r & "    " & nm & ":=ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "];" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "    GR_ev:=GR_ev+1; GR_c4:=GR_c4+1;" & vbCrLf
    nGR_cg = nGR_cg + 1
    r = r & "    if (GROFF=0) and (GR_rep[" & CStr(n) & "]=0) then begin" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "      GR_rep[" & CStr(n) & "]:=1;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "      Log(' LH1 guard: узел #" & CStr(n) & " - " & nm & " вне диапазона, v='+cstrf(GR_val,3));  kerr:= -798;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "    end;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  end else begin" & vbCrLf
    If CG4_Simple(nm) Then r = r & "    " & nm & ":=100;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "    ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "]:=100;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  end;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "end;"
    CG4_GuardTail = r
End Function
'=====================================================================
' CG4 v5 (Ф20): a/b-РАЗБИЕНИЕ ПРОЦЕДУР - порт механики LH2 v37 дословно
' (источник: LH3_Convert v15..v20 - работающий эталон LH3-этапа).
' Гард Ф20i (CG4 v5): уже разрезанные (тело части в implementation) не режутся
' повторно; декларации частей не дублируются в interface (урок Ф19).
'=====================================================================
'=====================================================================
' LH3 v15 (Ф20): a/b-РАЗБИЕНИЕ ПРОЦЕДУР - порт механики LH2 v37 дословно
' (L2_Split/L2_Disp/хелперы; пост-проход ПОСЛЕ вставок; Ф14/Ф15/Ф11/Ф12 учтены).
' Имена маркеров в эмите частей оставлены 'LH2 v22/v28' - как в рабочем эталоне.
'=====================================================================
Private Function L2_Simple(ByVal s As String) As Boolean
    Dim i As Long, c As String
    L2_Simple = False
    If Len(s) = 0 Then Exit Function
    For i = 1 To Len(s)
        c = Mid(s, i, 1)
        If Not ((c >= "a" And c <= "z") Or (c >= "A" And c <= "Z") Or (c >= "0" And c <= "9") Or c = "_") Then Exit Function
    Next i
    L2_Simple = True
End Function

' --- условие NaN/бред (pas-юниты) ---
Private Function L2_IsName(ByVal s As String) As Boolean
    Dim i As Long, c As String
    L2_IsName = False
    If Len(s) = 0 Then Exit Function
    For i = 1 To Len(s)
        c = Mid(s, i, 1)
        If Not ((c >= "a" And c <= "z") Or (c >= "A" And c <= "Z") Or (c >= "0" And c <= "9") Or c = "_" Or c = ".") Then Exit Function
    Next i
    L2_IsName = True
End Function

' --- имя процедуры из строки заголовка ---
Private Function L2_ProcName(ByVal t As String) As String
    Dim s As String, p As Long, head As String
    L2_ProcName = ""
    s = Trim(t)
    If LCase(Left(s, 10)) = "procedure " Then
        s = Mid(s, 11)
    ElseIf LCase(Left(s, 9)) = "function " Then
        s = Mid(s, 10)
    Else
        Exit Function
    End If
    s = Trim(s)
    p = InStr(s, "(")
    If p = 0 Then p = InStr(s, ":")
    If p = 0 Then p = InStr(s, ";")
    If p <= 1 Then Exit Function
    head = Trim(Left(s, p - 1))
    If InStr(head, ".") > 0 Then head = Trim(Mid(head, InStr(head, ".") + 1))
    If L2_Simple(head) Then L2_ProcName = head
End Function

' --- дельта глубины begin/try/repeat/case - end/until (вне строк-литералов) ---
Private Function L2_Tok(ByVal tok As String, ByVal oldw As String, ByVal neww As String) As String
    If StrComp(tok, oldw, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then L2_Tok = neww Else L2_Tok = tok
End Function

Private Function L2_RenameWord(ByVal s As String, ByVal oldw As String, ByVal neww As String) As String
    Dim r As String, i As Long, c As String, tok As String, q As Boolean
    r = "": tok = "": q = False
    For i = 1 To Len(s)
        c = Mid(s, i, 1)
        If c = "'" Then
            q = Not q
            If tok <> "" Then r = r & L2_Tok(tok, oldw, neww): tok = ""
            r = r & c
