            out = out & Mid(s, i)
            i = Len(s) + 1
        Else
            out = out & c: i = i + 1
        End If
    Loop
    L9_RenameWord = out
End Function

Private Function L9_FindDecl(ByRef L() As String, ByVal n As Long, ByVal nm As String) As Long
    Dim k As Long, t As String
    L9_FindDecl = 0
    For k = 1 To n
        t = Trim(L(k))
        If LCase(t) = "implementation" Then Exit For
        If LCase(Left(t, 10)) = "procedure " Then
            If Left(Mid(t, 11), Len(nm) + 1) = nm & ";" Then L9_FindDecl = k: Exit Function
        End If
    Next k
End Function

Private Sub L9_FindLoop(ByRef base1() As String, ByVal bn1 As Long, ByVal thrMono As Long, ByRef lbA As Long, ByRef lbE As Long)
    Dim i As Long, dd As Long, pv As Long, ltt As String
    Dim lws() As String, k As Long, lw As String, lhasB As Boolean, lhasE As Boolean
    Dim d2 As Long, j As Long
    lbA = 0: lbE = 0: dd = 0
    For i = 1 To bn1
        pv = dd
        dd = dd + L9_Delta(base1(i))
        If lbA = 0 Then
            ltt = LCase(L9_MonoStrip(base1(i)))
            If InStr(1, ltt, "for ") > 0 Or InStr(1, ltt, "while ") > 0 Then
                lhasB = False: lhasE = False
                lws = L9_MonoWords(ltt)
                For k = LBound(lws) To UBound(lws)
                    lw = lws(k)
                    If lw = "begin" Or lw = "repeat" Then lhasB = True
                    If lw = "end" Or lw = "until" Then lhasE = True
                Next k
                If lhasB And Not lhasE Then
                    d2 = dd
                    For j = i + 1 To bn1
                        d2 = d2 + L9_Delta(base1(j))
                        If d2 = pv Then
                            If (j - i - 1) > thrMono Then
                                lbA = i: lbE = j
                            End If
                            Exit For
                        End If
                    Next j
                    If lbA > 0 Then Exit For
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next i
End Sub

Private Function L9_MonoSplit(ByRef base1() As String, ByRef bn1 As Long, _
    ByVal thrMono As Long, ByRef nParts As Long, ByRef maxPart As Long, _
    ByRef msg As String, ByRef cycFlag As Long, ByRef joints() As Long, ByRef nj As Long) As Boolean
    Dim i As Long, j As Long, k As Long, dd As Long, pv As Long
    Dim bA As Long, be As Long, blen As Long
    Dim ifStart As Long, t As String, tt As String
    Dim cvars() As String, nc As Long, ws() As String, w As String
    Dim badCond As Boolean, oneSided As Long, hasB As Boolean, hasE As Boolean, hasC As Boolean
    Dim cand() As Long, ncand As Long, jn As Long, nx As String, badn As Boolean
    Dim np As Long, tg As Long, bS As Long, lastc As Long
    Dim cts() As Long
    Dim out2() As String, m2 As Long, cap2 As Long, pE2 As Long
    L9_MonoSplit = False: nParts = 0: maxPart = 0: msg = "": cycFlag = 0: nj = 0
    bA = 0: be = 0: dd = 0
    For i = 1 To bn1
        pv = dd
        dd = dd + L9_Delta(base1(i))
        If pv = 0 And dd > 0 Then bA = i
        If dd = 0 And pv > 0 And bA > 0 Then
            If (i - bA - 1) > thrMono Then
                be = i: Exit For
            Else
                bA = 0: be = 0
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    If bA = 0 Then Exit Function
    blen = be - bA - 1
    ifStart = bA
    For i = bA - 1 To 1 Step -1
        t = LCase(Trim(base1(i)))
        If Len(t) = 0 Then
            ifStart = i
        ElseIf Left(t, 3) = "if " Or Left(t, 3) = "if(" Or Left(t, 4) = "elseif" Then
            ifStart = i: Exit For
        Else
            ifStart = bA: Exit For
        End If
    Next i
    nc = 0: ReDim cvars(1 To 16)
    For i = ifStart To bA
        ws = L9_MonoWords(L9_MonoStrip(base1(i)))
        For j = LBound(ws) To UBound(ws)
            w = ws(j)
            If Len(w) > 0 And w <> "if" And w <> "then" And w <> "begin" _
               And w <> "and" And w <> "or" And w <> "not" And w <> "elseif" _
               And w <> "end" And w <> "=" Then
                If Not ((Left(w, 1) >= "0") And (Left(w, 1) <= "9")) Then
                    nc = nc + 1
                    If nc > UBound(cvars) Then ReDim Preserve cvars(1 To nc * 2)
                    cvars(nc) = w
                End If
            End If
        Next j
    Next i
    oneSided = 0: badCond = False: cycFlag = 0
    For i = bA + 1 To be - 1
        tt = LCase(L9_MonoStrip(base1(i)))
        ws = L9_MonoWords(tt)
        hasB = False: hasE = False: hasC = False
        For j = LBound(ws) To UBound(ws)
            w = ws(j)
            If w = "begin" Or w = "repeat" Then hasB = True
            If w = "end" Or w = "until" Then hasE = True
            If w = "for" Or w = "while" Or w = "case" Then hasC = True
        Next j
        If hasC And Not hasB And Not hasE Then oneSided = oneSided + 1
        If hasC And hasB And Not hasE Then cycFlag = 1
        If (Not badCond) And nc > 0 Then
            For j = 1 To nc
                If InStr(1, tt, cvars(j) & ":=") > 0 Then badCond = True: Exit For
                If InStr(1, tt, cvars(j) & " :=") > 0 Then badCond = True: Exit For
            Next j
        End If
    Next i
    If oneSided > 0 Then
        msg = "Ф32а: блок " & CStr(blen) & " НЕ РЕЗАН: одностороние цикл-строки (" & CStr(oneSided) & ")"
        Exit Function
    End If
    If badCond Then
        msg = "Ф32а: блок " & CStr(blen) & " НЕ РЕЗАН: переменная условия присваивается внутри блока"
        Exit Function
    End If
    If cycFlag = 1 Then
        msg = "Ф32а: блок " & CStr(blen) & " содержит многострочный цикл - путь Ф32б"
        Exit Function
    End If
    ncand = 0: ReDim cand(1 To 64)
    dd = 0
    For i = bA + 1 To be - 1
        t = base1(i)
        pv = dd
        dd = dd + L9_Delta(t)
        If dd = 0 And pv = 0 Then
            tt = Trim(t)
            If Left(tt, 2) = "//" Then
                ncand = ncand + 1
                If ncand > UBound(cand) Then ReDim Preserve cand(1 To ncand * 2)
                cand(ncand) = i - bA
            ElseIf Right(tt, 1) = ";" And LCase(Left(tt, 3)) <> "end" And LCase(Left(tt, 4)) <> "else" Then
                jn = i + 1
                Do While jn <= be - 1
                    If Len(Trim(base1(jn))) > 0 Then Exit Do
                    jn = jn + 1
                Loop
                nx = ""
                If jn <= be - 1 Then nx = LCase(Left(Trim(base1(jn)), 4))
                badn = (nx = "else") Or (Left(nx, 3) = "end") Or (Left(nx, 4) = "unti") _
                    Or (Left(nx, 1) = "+") Or (Left(nx, 1) = "-") Or (Left(nx, 3) = "and") _
                    Or (Left(nx, 2) = "or") Or (Left(nx, 1) = ".")
                If Not badn Then
                    ncand = ncand + 1
                    If ncand > UBound(cand) Then ReDim Preserve cand(1 To ncand * 2)
                    cand(ncand) = i - bA
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    If ncand = 0 Then
        msg = "Ф32а: блок " & CStr(blen) & " НЕ РЕЗАН: точек реза внутри блока нет"
        Exit Function
    End If
    np = (blen + thrMono - 1) \ thrMono
    If np < 2 Then np = 2
    ReDim cts(0 To np)
    lastc = 0
    For k = 1 To np - 1
        tg = k * blen \ np
        bS = 0
        For j = 1 To ncand
            If cand(j) > lastc Then
                If bS = 0 Or Abs(cand(j) - tg) < Abs(bS - tg) Then bS = cand(j)
            End If
        Next j
        If bS = 0 Then bS = blen - 1
        If bS >= blen Then bS = blen - 1
        If bS <= lastc Then bS = lastc + 1
        cts(k) = bS: lastc = bS
    Next k
    cts(np) = blen
    cap2 = bn1 + np * 4 + 16
    ReDim out2(1 To cap2): m2 = 0
    ReDim joints(1 To np): nj = 0
    For i = 1 To bA
        m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = base1(i)
    Next i
    For k = 1 To np
        For i = bA + cts(k - 1) + 1 To bA + cts(k)
            m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = base1(i)
        Next i
        If k < np Then
            pE2 = InStr(base1(be), ";")
            If pE2 > 0 Then
                m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = Left(base1(be), pE2)
            Else
                m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = base1(be)
            End If
            nj = nj + 1: joints(nj) = m2
            For i = ifStart To bA
                m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = base1(i)
            Next i
        End If
    Next k
    For i = be To bn1
        m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = base1(i)
    Next i
    ReDim Preserve base1(1 To m2)
    For i = 1 To m2
        base1(i) = out2(i)
    Next i
    bn1 = m2
    nParts = np
    maxPart = 0
    For k = 1 To np
        If (cts(k) - cts(k - 1)) > maxPart Then maxPart = cts(k) - cts(k - 1)
    Next k
    msg = "Ф32а: блок " & CStr(blen) & " -> " & CStr(np) & " кусков, макс. кусок " & CStr(maxPart) & " -> режет N-резку по стыкам"
    L9_MonoSplit = True
End Function

Private Sub L9_ApplyCut(ByRef L() As String, ByRef n As Long, ByVal cls As String, ByVal nm As String, _
    ByVal marker As String, ByRef names() As String, ByVal np As Long, _
    ByRef bodyTxt() As String, ByRef inner2() As String, ByVal inn2 As Long, ByRef exc() As String, ByVal td1 As Long, _
    ByRef fldNm() As String, ByRef fldTp() As String, ByVal nfl As Long, ByRef outRes() As String, ByRef mRes As Long)
    Dim out() As String, m As Long, cap As Long
    cap = n + np * 4 + 512
    ReDim out(1 To cap): m = 0
    Dim i As Long, j As Long, k As Long
    Dim hdr As Long, bi As Long
    hdr = 0: bi = 0
    For i = 1 To n
        If (Len(cls) > 0 And Trim(L(i)) = "procedure " & cls & "." & nm & ";") Or (Len(cls) = 0 And Trim(L(i)) = "procedure " & nm & ";") Then
            hdr = i: Exit For
        End If
    Next i
    If hdr = 0 Then Exit Sub
    For j = hdr + 1 To n
        If Trim(L(j)) = "begin" Then bi = j: Exit For
    Next j
    If bi = 0 Then Exit Sub
    Dim dd As Long, ei As Long
    dd = 1: ei = 0
    For j = bi + 1 To n
        dd = dd + L9_Delta(L(j))
        If dd <= 0 Then ei = j: Exit For
    Next j
    For i = 1 To bi
        m = m + 1: out(m) = L(i)
    Next i
    If td1 = 1 Then
        m = m + 1: out(m) = "  try"
    End If
    If inn2 > 0 Then
        For j = 1 To inn2
            m = m + 1: out(m) = inner2(j)
        Next j
    Else
        For k = 1 To np
            m = m + 1: out(m) = "    " & names(k) & ";  // " & marker & ": часть 1 из " & CStr(np) & " процедуры " & nm
        Next k
    End If
    For k = 1 To UBound(exc)
        m = m + 1: out(m) = exc(k)
    Next k
    m = m + 1: out(m) = "end;"
    For i = ei + 1 To n
        m = m + 1: out(m) = L(i)
    Next i
    Dim diNew As Long
    diNew = 0
    For i = 1 To m
        If LCase(Left(Trim(out(i)), 10)) = "procedure " Then
            If Left(Mid(Trim(out(i)), 11), Len(nm) + 1) = nm & ";" Then diNew = i: Exit For
        End If
    Next i
    If diNew > 0 Then
        Dim nl() As String, out2() As String, m2 As Long
        ReDim nl(1 To np)
        For k = 1 To np
            nl(k) = "    procedure " & names(k) & ";      // " & marker & ": часть " & CStr(k) & " из " & CStr(np) & " процедуры " & nm
        Next k
        m2 = 0: cap = m + np + 8
        ReDim out2(1 To cap)
        For i = 1 To diNew: m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = out(i): Next i
        For k = 1 To np: m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = nl(k): Next k
        For i = diNew + 1 To m: m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = out(i): Next i
        m = m2: out = out2
    End If
    If nfl > 0 Then
        Dim clsLn As Long
        clsLn = 0
        For i = 1 To m
            If InStr(LCase(out(i)), "=class(") > 0 Then clsLn = i: Exit For
        Next i
        If clsLn > 0 Then
            m2 = 0: cap = m + nfl + 8
            ReDim out2(1 To cap)
            For i = 1 To clsLn: m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = out(i): Next i
            For k = 1 To nfl
                m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = "    " & fldNm(k) & ": " & fldTp(k) & ";  // " & marker & ": локал процедуры " & nm
            Next k
            For i = clsLn + 1 To m: m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = out(i): Next i
            m = m2: out = out2
        End If
    End If
    Dim hdr2 As Long, beg2 As Long, ei2 As Long, dd3 As Long
    hdr2 = 0
    For i = 1 To m
        If (Len(cls) > 0 And Trim(out(i)) = "procedure " & cls & "." & nm & ";") Or (Len(cls) = 0 And Trim(out(i)) = "procedure " & nm & ";") Then
            hdr2 = i: Exit For
        End If
    Next i
    If hdr2 > 0 Then
        For j = hdr2 + 1 To m
            If Trim(out(j)) = "begin" Then beg2 = j: Exit For
        Next j
        dd3 = 1: ei2 = 0
        For j = beg2 + 1 To m
            dd3 = dd3 + L9_Delta(out(j))
            If dd3 <= 0 Then ei2 = j: Exit For
        Next j
        If ei2 > 0 Then
            m2 = 0: cap = m + np * 8 + 64
            ReDim out2(1 To cap)
            For i = 1 To ei2: m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = out(i): Next i
            Dim lines2() As String
            For k = 1 To np
                m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = ""
                m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = "procedure " & cls & "." & names(k) & ";"
                m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = "begin"
                lines2 = Split(bodyTxt(k), vbCrLf)
                For j = LBound(lines2) To UBound(lines2)
                    If Len(lines2(j)) > 0 Then
                        m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = lines2(j)
                    End If
                Next j
                m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = "    Miss_comm:=1; // " & marker & ": расчёт дошёл до конца части"
                m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = "end;"
            Next k
            For i = ei2 + 1 To m: m2 = m2 + 1: out2(m2) = out(i): Next i
            m = m2: out = out2
        End If
    End If
    ReDim Preserve out(1 To m)
    outRes = out: mRes = m
End Sub

Private Sub L9_CutOne(ByRef L() As String, ByRef n As Long, ByVal cls As String, ByVal nm As String, _
    ByVal marker As String, ByRef nCut As Long, ByRef nPartsAll As Long, ByRef nMaxAll As Long, ByRef msgLog As String, ByVal fp As String)
    Const L9_THR_LINE As Long = 5000: Const L9_THR_INNER As Long = 4000   ' LH2 v40 Ф36: локальные
    Dim hdr As Long, bi As Long, ei As Long, declI As Long
    Dim i As Long, j As Long, k As Long, dd As Long, tt As String, pp As Long
    Dim out() As String, m As Long
    msgLog = ""
    declI = L9_FindDecl(L, n, nm)
    If declI = 0 Then msgLog = "skip: нет декларации": Exit Sub
    hdr = 0
    For i = 1 To n
        If (Len(cls) > 0 And Trim(L(i)) = "procedure " & cls & "." & nm & ";") Or (Len(cls) = 0 And Trim(L(i)) = "procedure " & nm & ";") Then
            hdr = i: Exit For
        End If
    Next i
    If hdr = 0 Then msgLog = "skip: нет реализации": Exit Sub
    bi = 0
    For j = hdr + 1 To n
        If Trim(L(j)) = "begin" Then bi = j: Exit For
    Next j
    If bi = 0 Then msgLog = "skip: нет begin": Exit Sub
    dd = 1: ei = 0
    For j = bi + 1 To n
        dd = dd + L9_Delta(L(j))
        If dd <= 0 Then ei = j: Exit For
    Next j
    If ei = 0 Then msgLog = "skip: нет конца": Exit Sub
    If (ei - hdr) <= L9_THR_LINE Then msgLog = "skip: в пороге": Exit Sub
    Dim bL() As String, bln As Long
    bln = ei - bi - 1
    ReDim bL(1 To bln)
    For j = 1 To bln: bL(j) = L(bi + j): Next j
    Dim idxExc As Long, dd2 As Long
    dd2 = 0: idxExc = 0
    For j = 1 To bln
        tt = Trim(bL(j))
        If dd2 = 1 And (tt = "except" Or Left(tt, 7) = "except ") Then idxExc = j: Exit For
        dd2 = dd2 + L9_Delta(bL(j))
    Next j
    Dim inner() As String, inn As Long, exc() As String, exn As Long
    inn = 0: exn = 0
    If idxExc > 0 Then
        ReDim inner(1 To idxExc - 1)
        For j = 1 To idxExc - 1: inn = inn + 1: inner(inn) = bL(j): Next j
        ReDim exc(1 To bln - idxExc + 1)
        For j = idxExc To bln: exn = exn + 1: exc(exn) = bL(j): Next j
    Else
        ReDim inner(1 To bln)
        For j = 1 To bln: inn = inn + 1: inner(inn) = bL(j): Next j
        ReDim exc(1 To 1): exc(1) = ""
    End If
    Dim td1 As Long
    td1 = 0
    If inn > 0 And (Trim(inner(1)) = "try" Or Left(Trim(inner(1)), 4) = "try ") Then td1 = 1
    Dim base1() As String, bn1 As Long
    If td1 = 1 Then
        bn1 = inn - 1
        ReDim base1(1 To bn1)
        For j = 2 To inn: base1(j - 1) = inner(j): Next j
    Else
        bn1 = inn
        ReDim base1(1 To bn1)
        For j = 1 To inn: base1(j) = inner(j): Next j
    End If
    If bn1 <= L9_THR_LINE Then msgLog = "skip: в пороге": Exit Sub
    Dim fldNm() As String, fldTp() As String, nfl As Long
    ReDim fldNm(1 To 16): ReDim fldTp(1 To 16): nfl = 0
    For j = hdr + 1 To bi - 1
        tt = Trim(L(j))
        If Len(tt) > 0 And LCase(tt) <> "var" And Left(tt, 2) <> "//" And Left(tt, 1) <> "{" Then
            Dim lhs As String, tp As String, vv() As String, vi As Long, vn As String
            pp = InStr(tt, ":")
            If pp > 1 Then
                lhs = Trim(Left(tt, pp - 1))
                tp = Trim(Mid(tt, pp + 1))
                If Right(tp, 1) = ";" Then tp = Trim(Left(tp, Len(tp) - 1))
                vv = Split(lhs, ",")
                For vi = LBound(vv) To UBound(vv)
                    vn = Trim(vv(vi))
                    If Len(vn) > 0 And L9_Simple(vn) Then
                        nfl = nfl + 1
                        If nfl > UBound(fldNm) Then
                            ReDim Preserve fldNm(1 To nfl * 2): ReDim Preserve fldTp(1 To nfl * 2)
                        End If
                        fldNm(nfl) = nm & "_" & vn
                        fldTp(nfl) = tp
                        For k = 1 To bn1: base1(k) = L9_RenameWord(base1(k), vn, fldNm(nfl)): Next k
                        For k = 1 To exn: exc(k) = L9_RenameWord(exc(k), vn, fldNm(nfl)): Next k
                    End If
                Next vi
            End If
        End If
    Next j
    Dim lbA As Long, lbE As Long
    L9_FindLoop base1, bn1, L9_THR_INNER, lbA, lbE
    Dim names() As String, np As Long
    Dim bodyTxt() As String
    Dim inner2() As String, inn2 As Long
    Dim note As String, maxp As Long
    Dim qn As String, seg As String, seg2 As String
    If lbA > 0 Then
        Dim llen As Long
        llen = lbE - lbA - 1
        Dim tbody() As String
        ReDim tbody(1 To llen)
        For j = 1 To llen: tbody(j) = base1(lbA + j): Next j
        Dim tcand() As Long, tncand As Long
        ReDim tcand(1 To 64)
        L9_CutCand tbody, llen, tcand, tncand, 0
        If tncand = 0 Then
            msgLog = "skip: цикл " & CStr(llen) & " - точек реза в теле цикла нет"   ' Ф51 v45
            Exit Sub
        End If
        np = (llen + L9_THR_INNER - 1) \ L9_THR_INNER
        If np < 2 Then np = 2
        Dim lcts() As Long, llast As Long, ltg As Long, lbs As Long
        ReDim lcts(0 To np): llast = 0
        For k = 1 To np - 1
            ltg = k * llen \ np
            lbs = 0
            For j = 1 To tncand
                If tcand(j) > llast Then
                    If lbs = 0 Or Abs(tcand(j) - ltg) < Abs(lbs - ltg) Then lbs = tcand(j)
                End If
            Next j
            If lbs = 0 Then lbs = llen - 1
            If lbs >= llen Then lbs = llen - 1
            If lbs <= llast Then lbs = llast + 1
            lcts(k) = lbs: llast = lbs
        Next k
        lcts(np) = llen
        ReDim names(1 To np)
        For k = 1 To np
            qn = nm & L9_Suf(k)
            Do While L9_NameTaken(L, n, qn)
                qn = qn & "x"
            Loop
            names(k) = qn
        Next k
        ReDim inner2(1 To bn1 + np + 8): inn2 = 0
        For j = 1 To lbA + 1
            inn2 = inn2 + 1: inner2(inn2) = base1(j)
        Next j
        For k = 1 To np
            inn2 = inn2 + 1: inner2(inn2) = "    " & names(k) & ";  // " & marker & ": часть " & CStr(k) & " из " & CStr(np) & " цикла " & nm
        Next k
        For j = lbE To bn1
            inn2 = inn2 + 1: inner2(inn2) = base1(j)
        Next j
        ReDim Preserve inner2(1 To inn2)
        maxp = 0
        ReDim bodyTxt(1 To np)
        For k = 1 To np
            seg = ""
            For j = lcts(k - 1) + 1 To lcts(k)
                seg = seg & tbody(j) & vbCrLf
            Next j
            bodyTxt(k) = seg
            If (lcts(k) - lcts(k - 1)) > maxp Then maxp = lcts(k) - lcts(k - 1)
        Next k
        note = "Ф32б: цикл " & CStr(llen) & " -> " & CStr(np) & " частей, макс. часть " & CStr(maxp)
    Else
        Dim joints() As Long, nj As Long
        Dim monoApplied As Boolean, monoParts As Long, monoMax As Long, monoMsg As String, monoCyc As Long
        monoApplied = L9_MonoSplit(base1, bn1, L9_THR_INNER, monoParts, monoMax, monoMsg, monoCyc, joints, nj)
        ' Ф51 v45 (LH2): отказ Ф32а больше НЕ skip (корень "Procedure too complex" на 19.801):
        '      если в теле есть точки реза на глубине 0 (кандидаты L9_CutCand) - режем
        '      по кандидатам на N частей; если точек нет - прежний skip (тело выходит
        '      неразрезанным, как было). Отказ MonoSplit НЕ портит base1/bn1: все выходы
        '      отказа - до перестройки out2.
        If Not monoApplied Then nj = 0
        Dim cand() As Long, ncand As Long
        ReDim cand(1 To 64)
        L9_CutCand base1, bn1, cand, ncand, 0
        For k = 1 To nj
            ncand = ncand + 1
            If ncand > UBound(cand) Then ReDim Preserve cand(1 To ncand * 2)
            cand(ncand) = joints(k)
        Next k
        ' Ф37 (LH2 v41): "Dim n As Long" удалён - дубль параметра ByRef n As Long (VBA: Duplicate declaration)
        n = bn1
        If n <= L9_THR_LINE Then
            msgLog = "skip: в пороге после Ф32а"
            Exit Sub
        End If
        If ncand = 0 Then
            msgLog = "skip: " & monoMsg & "; точек реза на глубине 0 нет"   ' Ф51 v45
            Exit Sub
        End If
        np = (n + L9_THR_LINE - 1) \ L9_THR_LINE
        If np < 2 Then np = 2
        Dim cts() As Long, last As Long, tg As Long, bS As Long
        ReDim cts(0 To np): last = 0
        For k = 1 To np - 1
            tg = k * n \ np
            bS = 0
            For j = 1 To ncand
                If cand(j) > last Then
                    If bS = 0 Or Abs(cand(j) - tg) < Abs(bS - tg) Then bS = cand(j)
                End If
            Next j
            If bS = 0 Then bS = n - 1
            If bS >= n Then bS = n - 1
            If bS <= last Then bS = last + 1
            cts(k) = bS: last = bS
        Next k
        cts(np) = n
        ReDim names(1 To np)
        For k = 1 To np
            qn = nm & L9_Suf(k)
            Do While L9_NameTaken(L, n, qn)
                qn = qn & "x"
            Loop
            names(k) = qn
        Next k
        maxp = 0
        ReDim bodyTxt(1 To np)
        For k = 1 To np
            seg2 = ""
            For j = cts(k - 1) + 1 To cts(k)
                seg2 = seg2 & base1(j) & vbCrLf
            Next j
            bodyTxt(k) = seg2
            If (cts(k) - cts(k - 1)) > maxp Then maxp = cts(k) - cts(k - 1)
        Next k
        ReDim inner2(1 To 1): inn2 = 0
        note = "Ф32а+N-резка: " & CStr(np) & " частей, макс. часть " & CStr(maxp) & IIf(monoApplied, "", "; Ф32а не резал: " & monoMsg)
    End If
    L9_ApplyCut L, n, cls, nm, marker, names, np, bodyTxt, inner2, inn2, exc, td1, fldNm, fldTp, nfl, out, m
    n = m
    Dim f2 As Integer
    f2 = FreeFile
    Open fp For Output As #f2
    For i = 1 To n
        Print #f2, out(i)
    Next i
    Close #f2
    nCut = nCut + 1
    nPartsAll = nPartsAll + np
    If maxp > nMaxAll Then nMaxAll = maxp
    msgLog = note
End Sub

Private Sub L9_CutFile(ByVal pth As String, ByVal fn As String, ByRef nCut As Long, ByRef nPartsAll As Long, ByRef nMaxAll As Long, ByVal marker As String)
    Const L9_THR_LINE As Long = 5000   ' LH2 v40 Ф36: локальные - вставка самодостаточна
    Dim fPath As String, f1 As Integer
    Dim L() As String, n As Long, ln As String
    Dim i As Long, k As Long, e As Long
    Dim prs() As Long, npr As Long
    Dim skipFrom As Long, it As Long
    Dim cls As String, nm As String, msgLog As String
    Dim tgt As Long
    fPath = pth & fn
    f1 = FreeFile
    ReDim L(1 To 4096): n = 0
    Open fPath For Input As #f1
    Do While Not EOF(f1)
        If n = UBound(L) Then ReDim Preserve L(1 To UBound(L) * 2)
        n = n + 1
        Line Input #f1, ln
        If InStr(ln, "Ф32-пост-резка " & marker) > 0 Then
            Close #f1
            Exit Sub
        End If
        L(n) = ln
    Loop
    Close #f1
    ReDim Preserve L(1 To n)
    skipFrom = 0
    For it = 1 To 200
        ReDim L(1 To 4096): n = 0
        f1 = FreeFile
        Open fPath For Input As #f1
        Do While Not EOF(f1)
            If n = UBound(L) Then ReDim Preserve L(1 To UBound(L) * 2)
            n = n + 1
            Line Input #f1, ln
            L(n) = ln
        Loop
        Close #f1
        ReDim Preserve L(1 To n)
        ReDim prs(1 To n \ 8 + 8): npr = 0
        For i = 1 To n
            If Left(Trim(L(i)), 10) = "procedure " Then
                If npr = UBound(prs) Then ReDim Preserve prs(1 To npr * 2)
                npr = npr + 1: prs(npr) = i
            End If
        Next i
        tgt = 0
        For k = 1 To npr
            e = n
            If k < npr Then e = prs(k + 1) - 1
            If e - prs(k) > L9_THR_LINE And prs(k) > skipFrom Then
                nm = Trim(L(prs(k)))
                nm = Left(nm, Len(nm) - 1)
                If InStr(nm, "(") > 0 Then GoTo NextCandK   ' Ф14: процедуры с параметрами пост-резкой не режем (части теряют параметры)
                cls = ""
                If InStr(nm, ".") > 0 Then cls = Left(nm, InStr(nm, ".") - 1): nm = Mid(nm, InStr(nm, ".") + 1)
                tgt = prs(k)
                Exit For
            End If
NextCandK:
        Next k
        If tgt = 0 Then Exit For
        L9_CutOne L, n, cls, nm, marker, nCut, nPartsAll, nMaxAll, msgLog, fPath
        If Left(msgLog, 4) = "skip" Then
            skipFrom = tgt
        Else
            skipFrom = 0
        End If
    Next it
    f1 = FreeFile
    Open fPath For Append As #f1
    Print #f1, "// Ф32-пост-резка " & marker & ": процедур разрезано " & CStr(nCut) & ", частей добавлено " & CStr(nPartsAll) & ", макс. часть " & CStr(nMaxAll)
    Close #f1
End Sub

' Ф56: СТОП-инвариант приёмки. Меряет ТЕЛО процедуры dd-сканом от begin до парного end.
' Вызывается ПОСЛЕ последнего прохода резки по выходному файлу; накапливает нарушения.
' Решение о выпуске комплекта принимает основной процесс по LH2_StopFlag.
Private Sub LH2_StopCheck(ByVal pth As String, ByVal outn As String)
    Dim fPath As String, f1 As Integer
    Dim L() As String, n As Long, ln As String
    Dim i As Long, k As Long, hdr As Long, bi As Long, ei As Long
    Dim dd As Long, body As Long, t As String, nm As String
    fPath = pth & outn
    If Dir(fPath) = "" Then Exit Sub
    ReDim L(1 To 4096): n = 0
    f1 = FreeFile
    Open fPath For Input As #f1
    Do While Not EOF(f1)
        If n = UBound(L) Then ReDim Preserve L(1 To UBound(L) * 2)
        n = n + 1
        Line Input #f1, ln
        L(n) = ln
    Loop
    Close #f1
    If n = 0 Then Exit Sub
    ReDim Preserve L(1 To n)
    i = 1
    Do While i <= n
        t = Trim(L(i))
        If Left(t, 10) = "procedure " Then
            hdr = i
            Do While hdr <= n
                If Right(Trim(L(hdr)), 1) = ";" Then Exit Do
                hdr = hdr + 1
            Loop
            If hdr > n Then Exit Do
            nm = Trim(L(hdr))
            nm = Left(nm, Len(nm) - 1)
            If InStr(nm, "(") > 0 Then nm = Trim(Left(nm, InStr(nm, "(") - 1))
            bi = 0
            For k = hdr + 1 To n
                t = Trim(L(k))
                If t = "begin" Then
                    bi = k
                    Exit For
                End If
                If Left(t, 10) = "procedure " Or Left(t, 9) = "function " Then Exit For
                If t = "implementation" Or t = "end." Then Exit For
            Next k
            If bi > 0 Then
                dd = 0: ei = 0
                For k = bi + 1 To n
                    dd = dd + L9_Delta(L(k))
                    If dd < 0 Then
                        ei = k
                        Exit For
                    End If
                Next k
                If ei > bi Then
                    body = ei - bi - 1
                    If body > LH2_SAFE_LINE Then
                        LH2_StopCnt = LH2_StopCnt + 1
                        LH2_StopFlag = True
                        If LH2_StopCnt <= 10 Then
                            LH2_StopMsg = LH2_StopMsg & "  " & nm & " = " & CStr(body) & " строк  (" & outn & ")" & vbCrLf
                        End If
                    End If
                    i = ei
                End If
            End If
        End If
        i = i + 1
    Loop
End Sub
Sub LH2_Convert()
    Dim pth As String, fn As String, outn As String, ln As String
    Dim nCutAll As Long, nPartsAll As Long, nMaxAll As Long   ' Ф32-пост-резка v39
    Dim s As String, nm As String, s2 As String, nb As String
    Dim i As Long, p1 As Long, p2 As Long, p3 As Long, n As Long, k As Long
    Dim chi As Long, SZ As Long
    Dim f1 As Integer, f2 As Integer
    Dim curN As Long, hDone As Boolean, dDone As Boolean, noTail As Boolean
    Dim inW As Long, gat As Long, arm As Long, noc As Long
    Dim totB As Long, totL As Long, totH As Long
    Dim totI As Long
    Dim totU As Long, totR As Long, totF As Long, totO As Long, totK As Long, totS As Long
    Dim rnC As Long, kcC As Long, rgC As Long, plC As Long, totG As Long, totP As Long
    Dim s7 As String, kod As Long, ke As Long, gh As Long
    Dim fileList() As String, nFiles As Long, fi As Long
    Dim PD As Object
    Dim totSp As Long, totCh As Long, totD As Long
    Dim thr As Long, mainOut As String, tb As Long
    Dim gv As String, rest As String, ddA As Long   ' Ф1-Ф3 v29: объявлены (Option Explicit)
    Dim totC As Long   ' Ф5 v34: счётчик закомментированных бред-деклараций
    Dim splitsD As Object: Set splitsD = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Dim wFlag As Long, ddWA As Long, cls0 As String, pDone As Boolean, aDone As Boolean, implSeen As Boolean
    Dim t As String ' ФИКС 19.11 00:5x: Option Explicit — t (t = Trim(ln) в LH2_Convert); AB_SLOT уже Private на уровне модуля
    AB_SLOT = 53     ' v22: слоты 1..52 - части a/b разбитых Work_N; 53..99 - доп. части (c, d, ...)

    ' --- выбор папки ---
    ' Ф40 (v44): U4-интеграция БЕЗ compile-зависимости от модуля U4_UI (универсальный модуль):
    '      путь запрашивается через Application.Run("U4_GetPTH") - в Utilite_4 возвращает U4_PTH
    '      (кнопка кладёт туда ячейку C4 листа U4, после прогона U4_UI сбрасывает); в основном
    '      генераторе U4_UI нет -> Err 1004, путь остаётся пустым -> открывается свой диалог.
    '      Один и тот же модуль компилируется и в утилите, и в генераторе (при обеих открытых
    '      книгах Run берёт первую найденную U4_GetPTH - путь, как правило, пуст -> свой диалог).
    pth = ""
    On Error Resume Next
    pth = Application.Run("U4_GetPTH")   ' U4_UI: Public U4_GetPTH -> U4_PTH
    On Error GoTo 0
    If Len(pth) > 0 Then
        If Right(pth, 1) <> "\" Then pth = pth & "\" 
    Else
        With Application.FileDialog(msoFileDialogFolderPicker)
            .Title = "LH2 v34: папка с юнитами проекта 19.785"
            If Len(ThisWorkbook.path) > 0 Then .InitialFileName = ThisWorkbook.path & "\"     ' v24: по умолчанию - папка книги Excel
            If .Show <> -1 Then Exit Sub
            pth = .SelectedItems(1)
            If Right(pth, 1) <> "\" Then pth = pth & "\"
        End With
    End If

    ' --- форма прогресса (как в Мой_VBA: ufProgress + PrPro) ---
    ufProgress.Show (0)
    PrPro 0, "LH2: подготовка..."
    Set PD = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary") ' пресеты _0i для гашения In-каналов

    ' --- v23: порог разбиения УСТРАНЁН (безоговорочное a/b) ---
    thr = 0

    ' --- проход 1: список файлов, повторы, chi, размер массива, стиль, пресеты ---
    chi = 0: SZ = 0: nFiles = 0
    ReDim fileList(1 To 200)
    fn = Dir(pth & "*.txt")
    Do While fn <> ""
        If fn Like "*_LH2.txt" Then
            MsgBox "LH2: в папке уже есть файлы _LH2 (" & fn & ") - детектор уже применён к проекту." & vbCrLf & _
                   "Удалите старые *_LH2-файлы и запустите заново. Конверсия не выполнялась.", vbExclamation, "LH2 v34"
            On Error Resume Next: Unload ufProgress: On Error GoTo 0   ' Ф39 v43: форма не должна висеть
            Exit Sub
        End If
        nFiles = nFiles + 1
        If nFiles > UBound(fileList) Then ReDim Preserve fileList(1 To UBound(fileList) + 200)
        fileList(nFiles) = fn
        fn = Dir
    Loop

    For fi = 1 To nFiles
        fn = fileList(fi)
        PrPro fi / nFiles * 10, "Скан: " & fn
        tb = L2_MaxSourceBody(pth & fn)
        If tb > thr Then thr = tb
        f1 = FreeFile: Open pth & fn For Input As #f1
        Do While Not EOF(f1)
            Line Input #f1, ln
            If InStr(ln, "LH2 v") > 0 Or InStr(ln, "NaN_t[") > 0 Then
                Close #f1
                MsgBox "LH2: детектор уже стоит в проекте (маркер найден в " & fn & ")." & vbCrLf & _
                       "Конверсия не выполнялась.", vbExclamation, "LH2 v28"
                Unload ufProgress
                Exit Sub
            End If
            ' пресеты _0i - для гашения In-каналов по пресету
            If fn Like "*_0i.txt" Then
                s2 = Trim(ln)
                p1 = InStr(s2, ":=")
                If p1 > 1 And InStr(s2, "kerr") > 0 And Left(s2, 2) <> "//" Then
                    nm = Trim(Left(s2, p1 - 1))
                    p2 = InStr(p1 + 2, s2, ";")
                    If p2 > 0 And L2_Simple(nm) Then
                        s2 = Trim(Mid(s2, p1 + 2, p2 - p1 - 2))
                        If Len(s2) > 0 And InStr(s2, "'") = 0 And InStr(s2, """") = 0 Then
                            If Not PD.Exists(LCase(nm)) Then PD.Add LCase(nm), s2
                        End If
                    End If
                End If
            End If
            p1 = InStr(ln, "Давление и расход")
            If p1 > 0 Then
                p2 = InStr(1, ln, "#")
                If p2 > 0 And p2 < p1 Then
                    n = Val(Trim(Mid(ln, p2 + 1, p1 - p2 - 1)))
                    If n > chi Then chi = n
                End If
            End If
            If InStr(ln, "ISTO_p") > 0 And InStr(ln, "array") > 0 Then
                p1 = InStr(ln, "array[1..")
                If p1 > 0 And SZ = 0 Then SZ = Val(Trim(Mid(ln, p1 + 9)))
            End If
        Loop
        Close #f1
    Next fi

    If chi = 0 Then
        MsgBox "LH2: блоки 'Давление и расход' не найдены - это сборка 19.785?", vbExclamation, "LH2 v34"
        On Error Resume Next: Unload ufProgress: On Error GoTo 0   ' Ф39 v43: форма не должна висеть
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If SZ = 0 Then SZ = chi + 1
    ' --- v23: порог УСТРАНЁН - разбиение безоговорочное (a/b при любой длине) ---
    thr = 0
    gMaxPart = 0
    LH2_StopFlag = False: LH2_StopMsg = "": LH2_StopCnt = 0   ' Ф56
    gNoParamSkipped = 0   ' Ф59: инициализация (сброс из L2_Split снят - накопительный)

    rnC = 0: kcC = 0: rgC = 0: plC = 0
    gLimFix = 0
    ' --- проход 2: конвертация каждого файла из собранного списка в *_LH2.txt ---
    For fi = 1 To nFiles
        fn = fileList(fi)
        If fn Like "*_LH2.txt" Then GoTo NextFile
        outn = Left(fn, Len(fn) - 4) & "_LH2.txt"
        noTail = (fn Like "*_0i.txt")
        curN = 0: hDone = False: dDone = False: inW = 0: gat = 0: arm = 0: noc = 0: wFlag = 0: ddA = 0: ddWA = 0: totC = 0: cls0 = "": pDone = False: aDone = False: implSeen = False
        f1 = FreeFile: Open pth & fn For Input As #f1
        f2 = FreeFile: Open pth & outn For Output As #f2
        PrPro 10 + (fi - 1) / nFiles * 85, "Стражи: " & fn
        Do While Not EOF(f1)
            Line Input #f1, ln
            If noTail Then
                If InStr(ln, "LIM_P_min:=") > 0 Then
                    ' FIX v37 (Ф17): окно стража -0.9 вплотную к родному клампу м2 -0.95.
                    p1 = InStr(ln, "LIM_P_min:=")
                    p2 = InStr(p1, ln, ";")
                    If p2 > p1 + 11 Then
                        If Abs(Val(Mid(ln, p1 + 11, p2 - p1 - 11)) + 1) > 0.0001 Then
                            ln = Left(ln, p1 + 10) & "-1.0;" & Mid(ln, p2 + 1) & " // LH2 v37 (Ф17): окно под родной кламп -0.95"
                            gLimFix = gLimFix + 1
                        End If
                    End If
                End If
                Print #f2, ln
            Else
                s = Trim(ln)
                If Trim(ln) = "implementation" Then implSeen = True
                ' --- режим WorkIn (_0): ключи IN_OFF/IN_ON + гейты In-присвоений ---
                If InStr(ln, ".WorkIn;") > 0 And InStr(ln, "procedure T_Hmodel") > 0 Then
                    inW = 1
                    Print #f2, ln
                ElseIf inW = 1 And s = "try" Then
                    Print #f2, ln
                    Print #f2, "IN_str:=main.bph.param9.ValS.replace(' ','');"
                    Print #f2, "if (IN_str='IN_OFF') then INOFF:=1;"
                    Print #f2, "if (IN_str='IN_ON') then INOFF:=0;"
                    Print #f2, "IN_str:=main.bph.param10.ValS.replace(' ','');"
                    Print #f2, "if (IN_str='IN_OFF') then INOFF:=1;"
                    Print #f2, "if (IN_str='IN_ON') then INOFF:=0;"
                    Print #f2, "if (INOFF<>INOFF_prev) then begin"
                    Print #f2, "  if (LH2_DBG >= 1) and (INOFF=1) then begin Log(' IN: In-данные заказчика ОТКЛЮЧЕНЫ (IN_OFF) - пресеты нашей математики, арматуры активны'); kerr:= -800; end;"
                    Print #f2, "  if (LH2_DBG >= 1) and (INOFF=0) then begin Log(' IN: In-данные заказчика ВКЛЮЧЕНЫ (IN_ON)'); kerr:= -801; end;"
                    Print #f2, "  INOFF_prev:=INOFF;"
                    Print #f2, "end;"
                    Print #f2, "kerr:= -804;"
                    inW = 2
                ElseIf inW = 2 Then
                    s7 = s
                    If Left(s7, 12) = "Miss_comm:=1" Then inW = 0
                    gh = 0
                    If InStr(ln, ".Get") > 0 And Left(s7, 2) <> "//" Then
                        p1 = InStr(ln, "kerr:=")
                        If p1 > 0 Then
                            p2 = InStr(p1, ln, "//")
                            If p2 > 0 Then
                                p3 = InStr(p2, ln, "KOD:")
                                If p3 > 0 Then
                                    kod = Val(Mid(ln, p3 + 4, 3))
                                    ke = Val(Mid(ln, p1 + 6, 8))
                                    If ke >= 250006 And kod >= 1 And kod <= 18 And kod <> 2 And (kod < 5 Or kod > 8) Then
                                        gh = 1
                                        Print #f2, "if (INOFF=0) then " & s7
                                        gat = gat + 1
                                        ' страж In-канала (крайней точки): NaN/бред фиксируем и гасим
                                        p2 = InStr(s7, ":=")
                                        If p2 > 1 Then nm = Trim(Left(s7, p2 - 1)) Else nm = ""
                                        p2 = InStr(s7, "fpIn[")
                                        k = 0
                                        If p2 > 0 Then
                                            p3 = InStr(p2, s7, "]")
                                            If p3 > p2 Then k = Val(Mid(s7, p2 + 5, p3 - p2 - 5))
                                        End If
                                        If L2_Simple(nm) And k > 0 And k <= 200 Then
                                            Print #f2, "if " & L2_Bad(nm) & " then begin"
                                            Print #f2, "  " & L2_Rec("NaN_ig[" & CStr(k) & "]", nm & " (In-" & CStr(k) & ")", 0, 4)
                                            If PD.Exists(LCase(nm)) Then
                                                Print #f2, "  " & nm & ":=" & PD(LCase(nm)) & ";"
                                            Else
                                                Print #f2, "  " & nm & ":=100;"

                                            End If
                                            Print #f2, "  if (LH2_DBG >= 1) then Log('LH2 ev#'+CStr(NaN_seq)+' " & nm & " v='+cstrf(" & nm & ",4)); // LH2 v28: виновник при гашении"
                                            Print #f2, "end;"
                                            totI = totI + 1
                                        End If
                                    ElseIf kod = 2 Or (kod >= 5 And kod <= 8) Then
                                        arm = arm + 1
                                    Else
                                        noc = noc + 1
                                    End If
                                Else
                                    noc = noc + 1
                                End If
                            End If
                        End If
                    End If
                    If gh = 0 Then
                        Print #f2, ln
                        ' страж арматуры GetProcH (IN_OFF арматуры НЕ гейтит!)
                        If InStr(s7, ".GetProcH") > 0 Then
                            p2 = InStr(s7, ":=")
                            If p2 > 1 Then nm = Trim(Left(s7, p2 - 1)) Else nm = ""
                            If L2_Simple(nm) And ddA > 0 Then
                                kcC = kcC + 1
                                If kcC <= 1000 Then
                                    Print #f2, "if " & L2_Bad(nm) & " then begin"
                                    Print #f2, "  " & L2_Rec("NaN_ka[" & CStr(kcC) & "]", nm & " (арматура)", 0, 9)
                                    If PD.Exists(LCase(nm)) Then
                                        Print #f2, "  " & nm & ":=" & PD(LCase(nm)) & ";"
                                    Else
                                        Print #f2, "  " & nm & ":=0;"
                                    End If
                                    Print #f2, "  if (LH2_DBG >= 1) then Log('LH2 ev#'+CStr(NaN_seq)+' " & nm & " v='+cstrf(" & nm & ",4)); // LH2 v28: виновник при гашении"
                                    Print #f2, "end;"
                                    totK = totK + 1
                                End If
                            End If
                        End If
                    End If
                Else
                    ' --- номер текущего узла ---
                    p1 = InStr(ln, "Давление и расход")
                    If p1 > 0 Then
                        p2 = InStr(1, ln, "#")
                        If p2 > 0 And p2 < p1 Then curN = Val(Trim(Mid(ln, p2 + 1, p1 - p2 - 1)))
                    End If
                    ' --- декларации ---
                    If (Not dDone) And (InStr(ln, "ISTO_p") > 0 Or InStr(ln, "XX0") > 0) And InStr(ln, "array") > 0 Then
                        Print #f2, ln
                        Print #f2, L2_Decls(SZ)
                        dDone = True
                    ' --- страж входа: aa2 := X + INT_PRT - 1 ---
                    ElseIf curN > 0 And InStr(s, "aa2") = 1 And InStr(ln, "INT_PRT-1") > 0 Then
                        nm = Mid(ln, 1, InStr(ln, "INT_PRT-1") - 1)
                        p2 = InStrRev(nm, "+")
                        If p2 > 0 Then nm = Mid(nm, 1, p2 - 1)
                        p2 = InStrRev(nm, "=")
                        If p2 > 0 Then nm = Trim(Mid(nm, p2 + 1))
                        Print #f2, ln
                        If Len(nm) > 0 And ddA > 0 Then
                            totB = totB + 1
                            If PD.Exists(LCase(nm)) Then gv = PD(LCase(nm)) Else gv = "100"
                            Print #f2, "if " & L2_Bad(nm) & " then begin"
                            Print #f2, "  " & L2_Rec("NaN_in[" & CStr(curN) & "]", nm, curN, 1)
                            Print #f2, "  if " & L2_Live("ISTO_p[" & CStr(curN) & "]") & " then begin"
                            If L2_Simple(nm) Then Print #f2, "    " & nm & ":=ISTO_p[" & CStr(curN) & "];"
                            Print #f2, "    aa2:=ISTO_p[" & CStr(curN) & "];"
                            Print #f2, "  end else begin"
                            If L2_Simple(nm) Then Print #f2, "    " & nm & ":=" & gv & ";"
                            Print #f2, "    aa2:=" & gv & ";"
                            Print #f2, "  end;"
                            Print #f2, "  kerr:= -795;"
                            Print #f2, "  if (LH2_DBG >= 1) and (Not LH2_frzI[" & CStr(curN) & "]) then begin"
                            Print #f2, "    Log('LH2 ev#'+CStr(NaN_seq)+' " & nm & " v='+cstrf(" & nm & ",4)); // LH2 v28: last-good/пресет"
                            Print #f2, "    Log(' LH2 freeze: узел #" & CStr(curN) & " - " & nm & " NaN/бред - last-good/пресет');"
                            Print #f2, "    LH2_frzI[" & CStr(curN) & "] := true;"
                            Print #f2, "  end;"
                            Print #f2, "end else if " & L2_RngBad(nm) & " then begin"
                            Print #f2, "  " & L2_Rec("NaN_in[" & CStr(curN) & "]", "диапазон: " & nm, curN, 1)
                            Print #f2, "  if " & L2_Live("ISTO_p[" & CStr(curN) & "]") & " then begin"
                            If L2_Simple(nm) Then Print #f2, "    " & nm & ":=ISTO_p[" & CStr(curN) & "];"
                            Print #f2, "    aa2:=ISTO_p[" & CStr(curN) & "];"
                            Print #f2, "  end else begin"
                            If L2_Simple(nm) Then Print #f2, "    " & nm & ":=" & gv & ";"
                            Print #f2, "    aa2:=" & gv & ";"
                            Print #f2, "  end;"
                            Print #f2, "  kerr:= -796;"
                            Print #f2, "  if (LH2_DBG >= 1) and (Not LH2_frzI[" & CStr(curN) & "]) then begin"
                            Print #f2, "    Log('LH2 ev#'+CStr(NaN_seq)+' " & nm & " (диап) v='+cstrf(" & nm & ",4)); // LH2 v37: значение при гашении"
                            Print #f2, "    Log(' LH2 freeze: узел #" & CStr(curN) & " - " & nm & " вне диапазона LIM_P - last-good/пресет');"
                            Print #f2, "    LH2_frzI[" & CStr(curN) & "] := true;"
                            Print #f2, "  end;"
                            Print #f2, "end else LH2_frzI[" & CStr(curN) & "] := false; // Ф52: значение вернулось - сброс флага"
                        End If
                    ' --- страж связи: dP_r[k] := ... ---
                    ElseIf curN > 0 And InStr(s, "dP_r[") = 1 Then
                        p1 = InStr(ln, "dP_r[") + 5
                        p2 = InStr(p1, ln, "]")
                        k = 0
                        If p2 > p1 Then k = Val(Trim(Mid(ln, p1, p2 - p1)))
                        Print #f2, ln
                        If k > 0 And k <= 19 And ddA > 0 Then
                            nb = ""
                            p1 = InStr(ln, "Power(Abs(aa2+")
                            If p1 > 0 Then
                                s2 = Mid(ln, p1)
                                p2 = InStr(s2, "-")
                                If p2 > 0 Then
                                    p3 = InStr(p2, s2, ")")
                                    If p3 > p2 Then nb = Trim(Mid(s2, p2 + 1, p3 - p2 - 1))
                                End If
                            End If
                            If Len(nb) = 0 Then nb = "связь " & CStr(k)
                            Print #f2, "if " & L2_Bad("dP_r[" & CStr(k) & "]") & " then begin"
                            Print #f2, "  " & L2_Rec("NaN_link[" & CStr(curN) & "," & CStr(k) & "]", nb, curN, 3)
                            Print #f2, "  dP_r[" & CStr(k) & "]:=0;"
                            Print #f2, "  if (LH2_DBG >= 1) and (Not LH2_frzL[" & CStr(k) & "]) then begin"
                            Print #f2, "    Log('LH2 ev#'+CStr(NaN_seq)+' " & nb & " v='+cstrf(dP_r[" & CStr(k) & "],4)); // LH2 v28: виновник при гашении"
                            Print #f2, "    LH2_frzL[" & CStr(k) & "] := true;"
                            Print #f2, "  end;"
                            Print #f2, "end else LH2_frzL[" & CStr(k) & "] := false; // Ф52: значение вернулось - сброс флага"
                            totL = totL + 1
                        End If
                    ' --- страж хвоста: ISTO_p[N] := X ---
                    ElseIf InStr(s, "ISTO_p[") = 1 Then
                        p1 = InStr(ln, "ISTO_p[") + 7
                        p2 = InStr(p1, ln, "]")
                        n = 0
                        If p2 > p1 Then n = Val(Trim(Mid(ln, p1, p2 - p1)))
                        p1 = InStr(p2, ln, "=")
                        nm = ""
                        If p1 > 0 Then
                            nm = Trim(Mid(ln, p1 + 1))
                            i = InStr(nm, ";")
                            If i > 0 Then nm = Left(nm, i - 1)
                            nm = Trim(nm)
                        End If
                        If n > 0 And n <= SZ And Len(nm) > 0 And (Mid(nm, 1, 1) < "0" Or Mid(nm, 1, 1) > "9") And ddA > 0 Then
                            totH = totH + 1
                            If PD.Exists(LCase(nm)) Then gv = PD(LCase(nm)) Else gv = "100"
                            i = InStr(ln, ";")
                            If i > 0 Then rest = Trim(Mid(ln, i + 1)) Else rest = ""
                            Print #f2, "if (Not IsNan(" & nm & ")) and (Abs(" & nm & ")<=1e300) and (Not (" & L2_RngBad(nm) & ")) then ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "]:=" & nm & ";"
                            If Len(rest) > 0 Then Print #f2, rest
                            Print #f2, "if " & L2_Bad(nm) & " then begin"
                            Print #f2, "  " & L2_Rec("NaN_t[" & CStr(n) & "]", nm, n, 2)
                            Print #f2, "  if " & L2_Live("ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "]") & " then begin"
                            If L2_Simple(nm) Then Print #f2, "    " & nm & ":=ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "];"
                            Print #f2, "  end else begin"
                            If L2_Simple(nm) Then Print #f2, "    " & nm & ":=" & gv & ";"
                            Print #f2, "  end;"
                            Print #f2, "  kerr:= -797;"
                            Print #f2, "  if (LH2_DBG >= 1) and (Not LH2_frzT[" & CStr(n) & "]) then begin"
                            Print #f2, "    Log(' LH2 freeze: узел #" & CStr(n) & " - " & nm & " NaN/бред - зеркало НЕ обновлено');"
                            Print #f2, "    LH2_frzT[" & CStr(n) & "] := true;"
                            Print #f2, "  end;"
                            Print #f2, "end else if " & L2_RngBad(nm) & " then begin"
                            Print #f2, "  " & L2_Rec("NaN_t[" & CStr(n) & "]", "диапазон: " & nm, n, 2)
                            Print #f2, "  if " & L2_Live("ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "]") & " then begin"
                            If L2_Simple(nm) Then Print #f2, "    " & nm & ":=ISTO_p[" & CStr(n) & "];"
                            Print #f2, "  end else begin"
                            If L2_Simple(nm) Then Print #f2, "    " & nm & ":=" & gv & ";"
                            Print #f2, "  end;"
                            Print #f2, "  kerr:= -798;"
                            Print #f2, "  if (LH2_DBG >= 1) and (Not LH2_frzT[" & CStr(n) & "]) then begin"
                            Print #f2, "    Log('LH2 ev#'+CStr(NaN_seq)+' " & nm & " (диап) v='+cstrf(" & nm & ",4)); // LH2 v37: значение при гашении"
                            Print #f2, "    Log(' LH2 freeze: узел #" & CStr(n) & " - " & nm & " вне диапазона - зеркало НЕ обновлено');"
                            Print #f2, "    LH2_frzT[" & CStr(n) & "] := true;"
                            Print #f2, "  end;"
                            Print #f2, "end else LH2_frzT[" & CStr(n) & "] := false; // Ф52: значение вернулось - сброс флага"
                        Else
                            Print #f2, ln
                        End If
                    ' --- страж проводимости U_ri[N,k] ---
                    ElseIf InStr(s, "U_ri[") = 1 And InStr(ln, ":=") > 0 Then
                        p1 = InStr(s, "U_ri[") + 5
                        p2 = InStr(p1, ln, "]")
                        n = 0: k = 0
                        If p2 > p1 Then
                            p3 = InStr(p1, ln, ",")
                            If p3 > 0 And p3 < p2 Then
                                n = Val(Trim(Mid(ln, p1, p3 - p1)))
                                k = Val(Trim(Mid(ln, p3 + 1, p2 - p3 - 1)))
                            Else
                                n = Val(Trim(Mid(ln, p1, p2 - p1)))
                            End If
                        End If
                        nm = Trim(Left(s, InStr(s, ":=") - 1))
                        Print #f2, ln
                        If n > 0 And n <= SZ And k > 0 And k <= 19 And ddA > 0 Then
                            Print #f2, "if " & L2_Bad(nm) & " then begin"
                            Print #f2, "  " & L2_Rec("NaN_u[" & CStr(n) & "," & CStr(k) & "]", nm, n, 5)
                            Print #f2, "  " & nm & ":=0;"
                            Print #f2, "  if (LH2_DBG >= 1) then Log('LH2 ev#'+CStr(NaN_seq)+' " & nm & " v='+cstrf(" & nm & ",4)); // LH2 v28: виновник при гашении"

                            Print #f2, "end;"
                            totU = totU + 1
                        End If
                    ' --- страж плотности Ro_r[N,k] ---
                    ElseIf InStr(s, "Ro_r[") = 1 And InStr(ln, ":=") > 0 Then
                        p1 = InStr(s, "Ro_r[") + 5
                        p2 = InStr(p1, ln, "]")
                        n = 0: k = 0
                        If p2 > p1 Then
                            p3 = InStr(p1, ln, ",")
                            If p3 > 0 And p3 < p2 Then
                                n = Val(Trim(Mid(ln, p1, p3 - p1)))
                                k = Val(Trim(Mid(ln, p3 + 1, p2 - p3 - 1)))
                            Else
                                n = Val(Trim(Mid(ln, p1, p2 - p1)))
                            End If
                        End If
                        nm = Trim(Left(s, InStr(s, ":=") - 1))
                        Print #f2, ln
                        If n > 0 And n <= SZ And k > 0 And k <= 19 And ddA > 0 Then
                            Print #f2, "if " & L2_Bad(nm) & " then begin"
                            Print #f2, "  " & L2_Rec("NaN_ro[" & CStr(n) & "," & CStr(k) & "]", nm, n, 6)
                            Print #f2, "  " & nm & ":=0;"
                            Print #f2, "  if (LH2_DBG >= 1) then Log('LH2 ev#'+CStr(NaN_seq)+' " & nm & " v='+cstrf(" & nm & ",4)); // LH2 v28: виновник при гашении"

                            Print #f2, "end;"
                            totR = totR + 1
                        End If
                    ' --- страж расхода Fc_r[N,k] ---
                    ElseIf InStr(s, "Fc_r[") = 1 And InStr(ln, ":=") > 0 Then
                        p1 = InStr(s, "Fc_r[") + 5
                        p2 = InStr(p1, ln, "]")
                        n = 0: k = 0
                        If p2 > p1 Then
                            p3 = InStr(p1, ln, ",")
                            If p3 > 0 And p3 < p2 Then
                                n = Val(Trim(Mid(ln, p1, p3 - p1)))
                                k = Val(Trim(Mid(ln, p3 + 1, p2 - p3 - 1)))
                            Else
                                n = Val(Trim(Mid(ln, p1, p2 - p1)))
                            End If
                        End If
                        nm = Trim(Left(s, InStr(s, ":=") - 1))
                        Print #f2, ln
                        If n > 0 And n <= SZ And k > 0 And k <= 19 And ddA > 0 Then
                            Print #f2, "if " & L2_Bad(nm) & " then begin"
                            Print #f2, "  " & L2_Rec("NaN_fc[" & CStr(n) & "," & CStr(k) & "]", nm, n, 7)
                            Print #f2, "  " & nm & ":=0;"
                            Print #f2, "  if (LH2_DBG >= 1) then Log('LH2 ev#'+CStr(NaN_seq)+' " & nm & " v='+cstrf(" & nm & ",4)); // LH2 v28: виновник при гашении"

                            Print #f2, "end;"
                            totF = totF + 1
                        End If
                    ' --- страж плотностей rol/rog := rs/sz ---
                    ElseIf (InStr(s, "rol") = 1 Or InStr(s, "rog") = 1) And (InStr(ln, ":=rs") > 0 Or InStr(ln, ":=sz") > 0) Then
                        p1 = InStr(s, ":=")
                        nm = Trim(Left(s, p1 - 1))
                        s2 = Trim(Mid(s, p1 + 2))
                        i = InStr(s2, ";")
                        If i > 0 Then s2 = Left(s2, i - 1)
                        s2 = Trim(s2)
                        If L2_Simple(nm) And (s2 = "rs" Or s2 = "sz") And ddA > 0 Then
                            Print #f2, ln
                            rgC = rgC + 1
                            If rgC <= 600 Then
                                Print #f2, "if " & L2_Bad(nm) & " then begin"
                                Print #f2, "  " & L2_Rec("NaN_rg[" & CStr(rgC) & "]", nm, 0, 11)
                                Print #f2, "  " & nm & ":=1; rs:=1; sz:=1;"
                                Print #f2, "  if (LH2_DBG >= 1) then Log('LH2 ev#'+CStr(NaN_seq)+' " & nm & " v='+cstrf(" & nm & ",4)); // LH2 v28: виновник при гашении"

                                Print #f2, "end;"
                                totG = totG + 1
                            End If
                        Else
                            Print #f2, ln
                        End If
                    ' --- страж потерь dPL_r (LR_C): v28 - ТОЛЬКО прямое присваивание в теле ---
                    ElseIf InStr(s, "dPL_r[NOMist,NOMshist]:=") = 1 And ddA > 0 Then
                        Print #f2, ln
                        plC = plC + 1
                        If plC <= 5 Then
                            Print #f2, "if " & L2_Bad("dPL_r[NOMist,NOMshist]") & " then begin"
                            Print #f2, "  " & L2_Rec("NaN_pl[" & CStr(plC) & "]", "dPL_r (потери LR_C)", 0, 12)
                            Print #f2, "  dPL_r[NOMist,NOMshist]:=0;"
                            Print #f2, "  if (LH2_DBG >= 1) then Log('LH2 ev#'+CStr(NaN_seq)+' dPL_r v='+cstrf(dPL_r[NOMist,NOMshist],4)); // LH2 v28: виновник при гашении"

                            Print #f2, "end;"
                            totP = totP + 1
                        End If
                    ' --- страж roI-плотности ---
                    ElseIf InStr(ln, ":=roI") > 0 And Left(s, 2) <> "//" Then
                        p1 = InStr(s, ":=")
                        nm = Trim(Left(s, p1 - 1))
                        s2 = Trim(Mid(s, p1 + 2))
                        i = InStr(s2, ";")
                        If i > 0 Then s2 = Left(s2, i - 1)
                        If Trim(s2) = "roI" And L2_Simple(nm) And ddA > 0 Then
                            Print #f2, ln
                            rnC = rnC + 1
                            If rnC <= 600 Then
                                Print #f2, "if " & L2_Bad(nm) & " then begin"
                                Print #f2, "  " & L2_Rec("NaN_rn[" & CStr(rnC) & "]", nm, 0, 8)
                                Print #f2, "  " & nm & ":=1;"
                                Print #f2, "  if (LH2_DBG >= 1) then Log('LH2 ev#'+CStr(NaN_seq)+' " & nm & " v='+cstrf(" & nm & ",4)); // LH2 v28: виновник при гашении"

                                Print #f2, "end;"
                                totO = totO + 1
                            End If
                        Else
                            Print #f2, ln
                        End If
                    ' --- страж K_v-сглаживания хвостов ---
                    ElseIf curN > 0 And s Like "[A-Za-z_]*:=*" And InStr(ln, "K_v*1") > 0 And InStr(ln, "(1+K_tau)*aa2") > 0 And ddA > 0 Then
                        p1 = InStr(s, ":=")
                        nm = Trim(Left(s, p1 - 1))
                        Print #f2, ln
                        totS = totS + 1
                        If PD.Exists(LCase(nm)) Then gv = PD(LCase(nm)) Else gv = "100"
                        Print #f2, "if " & L2_Bad("aa2") & " then begin"
                        Print #f2, "  " & L2_Rec("NaN_in[" & CStr(curN) & "]", "aa2 при сглаживании " & nm, curN, 10)
                        Print #f2, "  if " & L2_Live("ISTO_p[" & CStr(curN) & "]") & " then begin"
                        Print #f2, "    aa2:=ISTO_p[" & CStr(curN) & "];"
                        If L2_Simple(nm) Then Print #f2, "    " & nm & ":=ISTO_p[" & CStr(curN) & "];"
                        Print #f2, "  end else begin"
                        Print #f2, "    aa2:=" & gv & ";"
                        If L2_Simple(nm) Then Print #f2, "    " & nm & ":=" & gv & ";"
                        Print #f2, "  end;"
                        Print #f2, "  kerr:= -799;"
                        Print #f2, "  if (LH2_DBG >= 1) and (Not LH2_frzS[" & CStr(curN) & "]) then begin"
                        Print #f2, "    Log(' LH2 freeze: узел #" & CStr(curN) & " - aa2 NaN перед сглаживанием - last-good/пресет');"
                        Print #f2, "    LH2_frzS[" & CStr(curN) & "] := true;"
                        Print #f2, "  end;"
                        Print #f2, "end else LH2_frzS[" & CStr(curN) & "] := false; // Ф52: значение вернулось - сброс флага"
                    ' --- HEALTH-скан ---
                    ElseIf (Not hDone) And InStr(ln, "У И: n=") > 0 And InStr(ln, "Б У И") = 0 Then
                        Print #f2, ln
                        Print #f2, L2_Scan(SZ)
                        hDone = True
                    ' --- LH2 v21: интерфейсная декларация AI_Analyze (до implementation) ---
                    ElseIf (Not implSeen) And (Not aDone) And InStr(ln, "WorkAdd") > 0 And InStr(LCase(ln), "procedure") > 0 Then
                        Print #f2, ln
                        Print #f2, L2_AI_Decl()
                        aDone = True
                    ' --- LH2 v21: заголовок WorkAdd - перед ним модуль ИИ-справки ---
                    ElseIf (Not pDone) And InStr(ln, ".WorkAdd;") > 0 And InStr(ln, "procedure T_Hmodel") > 0 Then
                        t = Trim(ln)
                        cls0 = Mid(t, 11)
                        p1 = InStr(cls0, ".WorkAdd")
                        If p1 > 1 Then cls0 = Trim(Left(cls0, p1 - 1)) Else cls0 = ""
                        If L2_Simple(cls0) Then
                            Print #f2, L2_AI_Procs(cls0, SZ)
                            Print #f2, ""
                        End If
                        Print #f2, ln
                        pDone = True: wFlag = 1: ddWA = 0
                    ElseIf wFlag = 1 And Trim(ln) = "end;" And ddWA = 1 Then
                        ' финальный end; тела WorkAdd - перед ним вызов ИИ-анализа
                        Print #f2, "  AI_Analyze;  // LH2 v21: ИИ-справка - анализ сбоев и NaN (результат в HEALTH)"
                        Print #f2, ln
                        wFlag = 2
                    ElseIf wFlag = 1 Then
                        ddWA = ddWA + L2_Delta(ln)
                        Print #f2, ln
                    Else
                        If wFlag = 1 Then ddWA = ddWA + L2_Delta(ln)
                        If L2_CyrOut(ln) And InStr(ln, "LH2") = 0 Then
                            Print #f2, "// " & ln
                            totC = totC + 1
                        Else
                            Print #f2, ln
                        End If
                    End If
                    If Left(s, 10) = "procedure " Or Left(s, 9) = "function " Then ddA = 0 Else ddA = ddA + L2_Delta(ln)   ' Ф6 v34: сброс на заголовке процедуры - "внутри тела" честно (тело ddA>=1, вне тел ddA<=0)
                End If
            End If
        Loop
        Close #f1, #f2
    L9_CutFile pth, outn, nCutAll, nPartsAll, nMaxAll, "LH2 v39"   ' Ф32-пост-резка v39
        PrPro 10 + (fi - 0.5) / nFiles * 85, "Разбиение: " & fn
        ' --- пост-проход: a/b-разбиение (порог = макс. исходник) ---
        If Not noTail Then
            totSp = totSp + L2_Split(pth & outn, thr, totCh, splitsD, mainOut)
            LH2_StopCheck pth, outn   ' Ф56
        End If
NextFile:
    Next fi
    If LH2_StopFlag Then   ' СТОП-инвариант: комплект НЕ выпускается
        MsgBox "СТОП: тело процедуры выше порога " & CStr(LH2_SAFE_LINE) & " строк." & vbCrLf & _
               "Нарушений: " & CStr(LH2_StopCnt) & vbCrLf & LH2_StopMsg & _
               IIf(LH2_StopCnt > 10, "  ... и ещё " & CStr(LH2_StopCnt - 10), "") & vbCrLf & _
               "Комплект НЕ выпущен. Резка не довела тела до порога.", vbCritical, "LH2 v51 СТОП"
        Application.StatusBar = False
        On Error Resume Next: Unload ufProgress: On Error GoTo 0
        Exit Sub
    End If

    ' --- диспетчер main.Work: пары a/b с регистрацией ---
    PrPro 95, "Диспетчер Work..."
    If Len(mainOut) > 0 Then
        totD = L2_Disp(mainOut, splitsD)
        LH2_StopCheck pth, mainOut   ' Ф62
        If LH2_StopFlag Then   ' Ф62: СТОП-инвариант и ПОСЛЕ диспетчера (main.Work растёт от блоков L2_Disp)
            MsgBox "СТОП: тело процедуры выше порога " & CStr(LH2_SAFE_LINE) & " строк (после диспетчера)." & vbCrLf & _
               "Нарушений: " & CStr(LH2_StopCnt) & vbCrLf & LH2_StopMsg & _
               "Комплект НЕ выпущен.", vbCritical, "LH2 v51 СТОП (после диспетчера)"
            Application.StatusBar = False
            On Error Resume Next: Unload ufProgress: On Error GoTo 0
            Exit Sub
        End If
    End If

    PrPro 100, "Готово"
    MsgBox "LH2 v34: готово (полная индикация + ИИ-справка + РЕФОРМА ГАШЕНИЙ)." & vbCrLf & _
           "Узлов (chi) = " & CStr(chi) & ", размер массива = " & CStr(SZ) & _
           ", макс. часть = " & CStr(gMaxPart) & " строк; порог приёмки (СТОП-инвариант) = " & CStr(LH2_SAFE_LINE) & "" & vbCrLf & _
           "Давления - вход: " & CStr(totB) & ", связей dP_r: " & CStr(totL) & ", хвостов: " & CStr(totH) & ", In-каналов: " & CStr(totI) & vbCrLf & _
           "Расходная цепочка - U_ri: " & CStr(totU) & ", Ro_r: " & CStr(totR) & ", Fc_r: " & CStr(totF) & _
           ", roI: " & CStr(totO) & ", арматур: " & CStr(totK) & ", сглаживаний: " & CStr(totS) & vbCrLf & _
           "rs/sz-плотностей: " & CStr(totG) & ", потерь dPL_r (LR_C): " & CStr(totP) & vbCrLf & _
           "Пресетов _0i собрано: " & CStr(PD.Count) & vbCrLf & _
           "Гашение: давления -> last-good(зеркало) -> пресет _0i -> 100; хвосты - ЗАМОРОЗКА (зеркало НЕ обновляется бредом)." & vbCrLf & _
           "Диапазон давлений LIM_P_min..LIM_P_max (инициализация _0i; активен при LIM_P_max>0); dP_r/Fc_r/U_ri/Ro_r -> 0, roI/rol/rog -> 1, арматуры -> пресет/0." & vbCrLf & _
           "Стражи вставляются ТОЛЬКО ВНУТРИ тел процедур; декларации - сразу после class (поля до методов)." & vbCrLf & _
           "Процедур разбито: " & CStr(totSp) & ", частей: " & CStr(totCh) & ", блоков в диспетчере: " & CStr(totD) & ", слоты блоков с " & CStr(AB_SLOT) & vbCrLf & _
           "Умный разбор в HEALTH: сбойные слоты (Miss_ind>0), последняя часть, скачки зеркал." & vbCrLf & _
           "ИИ-справка: модуль в _0 рядом с WorkAdd (AI_Analyze), печать в HEALTH после NaN-скана; kerr -810/-811." & vbCrLf & _
           "Бред-деклараций закомментировано (Ф5): " & CStr(totC) & "Процедур с параметрами пропущено (не режем, Ф14): " & CStr(gNoParamSkipped) & vbCrLf & "LIM_P_min исправлено в _0i (Ф17): " & CStr(gLimFix) & vbCrLf & "Файлы: *_LH2.txt (исходные не тронуты).", vbInformation, "LH2 v37"
    Unload ufProgress
    If nCutAll > 0 Then MsgBox "Ф32-пост-резка v40: процедур разрезано " & nCutAll & ", частей добавлено " & nPartsAll & ", макс. часть " & nMaxAll & " строк.", vbInformation, "LH2 v39"
End Sub

'   гейты LHx_DBG >= 2 заменены на >= 1; база LHx_DBG_OUT: 0 - молчание (рабочий режим),
'   1 - всё включено. Универсальный U4-гейт сохранён: в генераторе (модули 11/12/15/6)
'   U4_UI нет - открывается свой диалог выбора папки; в UT4 - путь из ячейки C4 листа U4.

'   владельца 26.11 17:5x: журнал м2 (плагин ukr_hm) захламлён 127962 сообщениями

'  dL<-0.55*SK_L). При SK_L=1 числа совпадают; при SK_L<>1 - меняются (МЕНЯЕТ ЧИСЛА).
'  SW_SKL=1 дефолт; РИСК: при SK_L=0 границы клампа обнуляются (м2: SK_L:=0.2*pr_Ktime - >0).
'  Откат: SW_SKL=0 (эмит как в v4). С1б-7 (B13 донор) - НЕ внесён: решение владельца
'  (вариант (а) подстановка переменной при объявлении / (б) флаг данных «граница-донор»).
'  Откат: SW_TST=0, SW_SKL=0. Счётчики TST/SKL в итоговом MsgBox.
' НОВОЕ В v6 (23.11, С1б-2 остаток): жёсткие капы фаз в лимитерах (W_out/L_out/G_out := инвентарь+приход)
'  заменены плавным по-фазным масштабом ZZ0[93]/ZZ0[94]/ZZ0[95] (м2 их уже вычисляет, но не применяет).
'  Перекладки фаз (излишек W->L->G->L) убраны правилом A9 в v4. МЕНЯЕТ ЧИСЛА: SW_PH=1 дефолт,
'  сверка эталоном обязательна (границы _1: узлы с ZZ0-группами). Откат: SW_PH=0.
' НОВОЕ В v5 (23.11, партия Шага 0, остаток): С0-1 clamp/guard-счётчики (инкременты в стражах LH1
'  и клампах A1/A6/B11/B14 + печать в HEALTH-строке С0-1), С0-5 {$R+} за флагом SW_RP=0 (ВЫКЛ по
'  умолчанию; включать только после аудита границ SH_C 1..20 vs массивы 1..19 - п.5 реестра).
'  СВЕРКА 23.11: реестр v1.0 отставал от базы v4 - С0-2 (L1_FixPowers), С0-3 (PsReady+AA02-защита),
'  С0-4 (B15: eps в XX0[7]-делениях), С0-8 (B2-печать + замер K_fff, kerr -992/-993) и С1б-16
'  (ОИ-Ньютон: OI_MODE=1, бисекция 60 итераций, SW_OIM=1) УЖЕ внесены в v4.
'  НЕ включено: build(10) (перенос санитизации дублей в начало итерации) - следующая партия.
'---------------------------------------------------------------------
' БАЗА: LH3 v2 (стражи v21 + ПОСТ-ПРАВКИ A1/A2 + Ф4).
' НОВОЕ В v4 (по реестру lh3_fix_registry.txt и прогону пайплайна):
'  С0-1 счётчики LH3_cnt_OI/LH3_cnt_24K + HEALTH; С0-2 Power(a,2)->(a)*(a);
'  С0-3 кэш Psat: AA/BB - константы фита (вычислить один раз, НЕ по T);
'  С0-4 Б2-печать; С0-5 HEALTH-симметрия рёбер; С0-8 замер K_fff;
'  С1а-1 Power(2.7182,x)->Exp; С1а-2 Power(x,0.5)->Sqrt (синхронно с CG4 в будущем);
'  С1б-9 Т-2/Т-1: канонический расход ребра (вторая половина валидируется,
'        для границ - "я мёртв, сосед-граница -> явный ноль");
'  С1б-10 Т-3/LRC: гашение U_ri при aa3<=0; ЛРС без разрыва 0.2 и без знака-свопа;
'  С1б-10 A6: кламп подушки без ручного окна; С1б-12 ОИ-Ньютон (OI_MODE=1);
'  С1б-10/12.4 нормировка без доборов; eG=beta_mass; roI по rs;
'  С2-4 B8: 298->273.15; B11: нижний кламп dL; B12: гейт клапана роста;
'  B14: клампы долей; B15: eps в XX0[7]-делениях;
'  F11: невязка без K_svod + W_damp + относительный критерий;
'  A11: пересчёт фазовых расходов после петли ОИ (только ОИ-петли).
' Приёмка: FPC 3.2.2 - _000/_00/_01 чисто, _0 - известный класс заглушек UI2.
' Решение владельца: м2 НЕ ТРОГАЕТСЯ; CG4 вне обсуждения.
' ПРИМЕЧАНИЕ: ufProgress/PrPro - внешние (книга владельца, как в Мой_VBA); если их нет в книге -
'  VBA даст "Sub or Function not defined" - держать книгу с Мой_VBA открытой (как в LH2 v34).
'=====================================================================
'=====================================================================
Option Explicit
Const LH3_KEYLOG As Long = 1    ' Ф53-б: читать ключ HEALTH/HEALTH2 из LOG0 (0 - не читать; юниты без LOG0)
Const LH3_DBG_OUT As Long = 0   ' Ф53-в: ключ лога юнитов: 0 - выдачи выключены (рабочий режим), 1 - включить всё (равнозначно ключам HEALTH/HEALTH2)
Private csh1 As Boolean
Private AB_SLOT As Long
Private gMaxPart As Long     ' LH3 v15 (Ф20): максимальная длина части (контроль лимита компилятора)
Private Const LH3_SAFE_LINE As Long = 6000   ' Ф55: порог приёмки тела процедуры (DCC: 7100 прошло, 10611 - нет)
Private LH3_StopFlag As Boolean              ' Ф55: найдены тела выше порога
Private LH3_StopMsg As String                ' Ф55: список нарушений (до 10)
Private LH3_StopCnt As Long                  ' Ф55: всего нарушений
Private gNoParamSkipped As Long   ' LH3 v15 (Ф20/Ф14): счётчик процедур с параметрами, пропущенных при разрезании
Private gMonoPieces As Long   ' LH3 v21 (Ф32): суммарное число кусков в-блочной резки
Private gMonoBlocks As Long   ' LH3 v21 (Ф32): число блоков, разрезанных в-блочно
Private totNoParam As Long        ' LH3 v15: суммарный счётчик Ф14 по комплекту (замечание 5.1 PSR)
Private nDub As Long              ' LH3 v15 (Ф19): счётчик пропущенных дублей деклараций
Private L1_cnt_POWFIX As Long    ' LH3 v26 (Ф36.1): число оставленных Power (С1а-1: прочие основания + буквенный показатель)
Private L1_cnt_EXP2 As Long      ' LH3 v26 (Ф36.1): число корректных Exp-замен (С1а-1, основание 2.7182)

' LH3 v26 (Ф36.1): распознавание основания-константы e=2.7182 (ТОЧНО; LNG2E=ln(2.7182)=0.9999698915).
' Расширенные формы (2.71828, "e") НЕ принимаются: подмена Ln(a) на LNG2E для не-2.7182 и была багом С1а-1.
Private Function L1_IsEBase(ByVal s As String) As Boolean
    L1_IsEBase = (Trim(s) = "2.7182")
End Function

' LH3 v26 (Ф36.2): канарейка при старте конвертации.
' PASS-условия: (1) тождество Power(2.7182,0.23)=Exp(0.23*Log(2.7182)) с точн. 1e-9 отн.
'   (эталон - Log в рантайме, Ф41 v28; замена корректна по определению LNG2E=ln(2.7182);
'   + ПРОВЕРКА 1б: |LNG2E - Log(2.7182)| <= 1e-8 абс. - ловит подмену константы);
'   (2) критерий основания: L1_IsEBase("2.7182")=True, L1_IsEBase("2.72")=False,
'   L1_IsEBase("((po_p+1)/(pi_p+1))")=False (критерий не расширен);
'   (3) контрпример: Power(2,0.23)=1.1726 НЕ равен Exp(0.23*LNG2E)=1.5838 (расхождение есть).
' FAIL -> MsgBox + Exit Sub (конвертация не выпускается) - как Ф37.4 в CG4 v12.
Private Function L1_CanaryPowers() As Boolean
    Dim lhs As Double, rhs As Double
    L1_CanaryPowers = False
    ' ПРОВЕРКА 1 (Ф41 v28): эталон - Log(2.7182) в рантайме, а не округлённая константа
    lhs = (2.7182 ^ 0.23): rhs = Exp(0.23 * Log(2.7182))
    If Abs(lhs - rhs) > 0.000000001 * lhs Then Exit Function
    ' ПРОВЕРКА 1б (Ф41 v28): эмитируемая LNG2E точна до 1e-8 абс. (ловит подмену константы,
    ' не ловя её собственную погрешность 5.04e-9)
    If Abs(0.9999698915 - Log(2.7182)) > 0.00000001 Then Exit Function
    If Not L1_IsEBase("2.7182") Then Exit Function
    If L1_IsEBase("2.72") Then Exit Function
    If L1_IsEBase("((po_p+1)/(pi_p+1))") Then Exit Function
    lhs = (2# ^ 0.23): rhs = Exp(0.23 * 0.9999698915)
    If Abs(lhs - rhs) < 0.000001 * lhs Then Exit Function
    L1_CanaryPowers = True
End Function

' --- есть ли кириллица ВНЕ строк-литералов и вне "//" (Ф4 v2, перенос из LH1/LH2) ---
Private Function L1_CyrOut(ByVal s As String) As Boolean
    Dim q As String, i As Long, c As String, a As Long
    q = ""
    For i = 1 To Len(s)
        c = Mid(s, i, 1)
        If q <> "" Then
            If c = q Then q = ""
        ElseIf c = "'" Or c = Chr(34) Then
            q = c
        ElseIf c = "/" And i < Len(s) And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "/" Then
            Exit For
        Else
            a = Asc(c)
            If (a >= 192 And a <= 255) Or a = 168 Or a = 184 Then
                L1_CyrOut = True: Exit Function
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    L1_CyrOut = False
End Function

' --- флаги правил v4: локальные в Sub LH3_Convert (Dim + инициализация) ---

' --- простой идентификатор (безопасно гасить присваиванием) ---
Private Function L1_Simple(ByVal s As String) As Boolean
    Dim i As Long, c As String
    L1_Simple = False
    If Len(s) = 0 Then Exit Function
    For i = 1 To Len(s)
        c = Mid(s, i, 1)
        If Not ((c >= "a" And c <= "z") Or (c >= "A" And c <= "Z") Or (c >= "0" And c <= "9") Or c = "_") Then Exit Function
    Next i
    L1_Simple = True
End Function
' --- дельта глубины begin/try/repeat/case - end/until (вне строк-литералов) ---
Private Function L1_Delta(ByVal s As String) As Long
    Dim q As Boolean, i As Long, c As String, buf As String, t As String
    Dim d As Long, j As Long, w As String
    q = False: buf = ""
    For i = 1 To Len(s)
        c = Mid(s, i, 1)
        If c = "'" Then
            q = Not q: buf = buf & c
        ElseIf c = "/" And (Not q) And i < Len(s) And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "/" Then
            Exit For
        Else
            buf = buf & c
        End If
    Next i
    t = LCase(buf)
    If InStr(t, "begin") = 0 And InStr(t, "end") = 0 And InStr(t, "try") = 0 _
       And InStr(t, "repeat") = 0 And InStr(t, "until") = 0 And InStr(t, "case") = 0 Then
        L1_Delta = 0: Exit Function
    End If
    For i = 1 To Len(t)
        c = Mid(t, i, 1)
        If Not ((c >= "a" And c <= "z") Or (c >= "0" And c <= "9") Or c = "_") Then Mid(t, i, 1) = " "
    Next i
    d = 0
    Dim parts() As String
    parts = Split(t, " ")
    For j = LBound(parts) To UBound(parts)
        w = parts(j)
        If w = "begin" Or w = "try" Or w = "repeat" Then d = d + 1
        If w = "end" Or w = "until" Then d = d - 1
        If w = "case" Then d = d + 1
    Next j
    L1_Delta = d
End Function
' --- Power-фиксы v4 (С0-2, С1а-1, С1а-2): балансный разбор, скобки сохраняются ---
Private Function L1_FixPowers(ByVal s As String) As String
    Dim out As String, i As Long, p As Long, depth As Long, c As String
    Dim rest As String, args As String, j As Long, a1 As String, a2 As String
    out = "": i = 1
    Do While i <= Len(s)
        p = InStr(i, s, "Power(", vbTextCompare)
        If p = 0 Then out = out & Mid(s, i): Exit Do
        If p > 1 Then out = out & Mid(s, i, p - i)
        ' разбор скобок от p+5
        depth = 1: j = p + 6: args = ""
        Do While j <= Len(s) And depth > 0
            c = Mid(s, j, 1)
            If c = "(" Then depth = depth + 1
            If c = ")" Then
                depth = depth - 1
                If depth = 0 Then Exit Do
            End If
            args = args & c: j = j + 1
        Loop
        If depth = 0 Then
            ' args = A,B (A может быть выражением со скобками)
            depth = 0: a1 = "": a2 = ""
            For i = 1 To Len(args)
                c = Mid(args, i, 1)
                If c = "(" Then depth = depth + 1
                If c = ")" Then depth = depth - 1
                If c = "," And depth = 0 Then
                    a1 = Left(args, i - 1): a2 = Mid(args, i + 1): Exit For
