                End If
            Next i
            a1 = Trim(a1): a2 = Trim(a2)
            If a1 <> "" And a2 <> "" Then
                If a2 = "2" Then
                    out = out & "(" & a1 & ")*(" & a1 & ")"          ' С0-2
                ElseIf a2 = "0.5" Then
                    out = out & "Sqrt(" & a1 & ")"                    ' С1а-2
                ElseIf LCase(Left(a2, 1)) >= "a" Then
                    out = out & "Power(" & args & ")"                     ' С1а-1 v26 (Ф36.1): буквенный показатель - НЕ заменять (v4-v25 теряли a2)
                    L1_cnt_POWFIX = L1_cnt_POWFIX + 1
                Else
                    If L1_IsEBase(a1) Then
                        out = out & "Exp((" & a1 & ")*" & a2 & "*LH3_LNG2E)"   ' С1а-1 v26 (Ф36.1): основание 2.7182 - корректная замена (Ln(2.7182)=LNG2E)
                        L1_cnt_EXP2 = L1_cnt_EXP2 + 1
                    Else
                        out = out & "Power(" & args & ")"                      ' С1а-1 v26 (Ф36.1): прочие основания - Power как есть (Ln(a) не подменяется)
                        L1_cnt_POWFIX = L1_cnt_POWFIX + 1
                    End If
                End If
            Else
                out = out & "Power(" & args & ")"
            End If
            i = j + 1
        Else
            out = out & "Power(": i = p + 6
        End If
    Loop
    L1_FixPowers = out
End Function
' --- условие NaN/бред ---
Private Function L1_Bad(ByVal nm As String) As String
    If csh1 Then
        L1_Bad = "double.IsNaN(" & nm & ")||Math.Abs(" & nm & ")>1e300"
    Else
        L1_Bad = "IsNan(" & nm & ") or (Abs(" & nm & ")>1e300)"
    End If
End Function
' --- запись события (первое появление = порядковый номер) ---
Private Function L1_Rec(ByVal idx As String, ByVal nm As String, ByVal uz As Long, ByVal tp As Long) As String
    If csh1 Then
        L1_Rec = "if (" & idx & "==0) && (NaN_seq<1000) { NaN_seq++; " & idx & "=NaN_seq; NaN_nm[NaN_seq]=""" & nm & """; NaN_uz[NaN_seq]=" & CStr(uz) & "; NaN_tp[NaN_seq]=" & CStr(tp) & "; }"
    Else
        L1_Rec = "if (" & idx & "=0) and (NaN_seq<1000) then begin NaN_seq:=NaN_seq+1; " & idx & ":=NaN_seq; NaN_nm[NaN_seq]:='" & nm & "'; NaN_uz[NaN_seq]:=" & CStr(uz) & "; NaN_tp[NaN_seq]:=" & CStr(tp) & "; end;"
    End If
End Function
' --- условие вне диапазона давлений (активен при LIM_P_max>0) ---
Private Function L1_RngBad(ByVal nm As String) As String
    L1_RngBad = "(LIM_P_max>0) and (((" & nm & ")<LIM_P_min) or ((" & nm & ")>LIM_P_max))"
End Function
' --- условие живого last-good из зеркала ---
Private Function L1_Live(ByVal nm As String) As String
    L1_Live = "(Not IsNan(" & nm & ")) and (Abs(" & nm & ")<=1e300) and ((" & nm & ")>1e-9)"
End Function

'=====================================================================
' LH3 v15 (Ф20): a/b-РАЗБИЕНИЕ ПРОЦЕДУР - порт механики LH2 v37 дословно
' (L2_Split/L2_Disp/хелперы; пост-проход ПОСЛЕ вставок; Ф14/Ф15/Ф11/Ф12 учтены).
' Имена маркеров в эмите частей оставлены 'LH2 v22/v28' - как в рабочем эталоне.
'=====================================================================
Private Function L2_Simple(ByVal s As String) As Boolean
    Dim i As Long, c As String
    L2_Simple = False
    If Len(s) = 0 Then Exit Function
    For i = 1 To Len(s)
        c = Mid(s, i, 1)
        If Not ((c >= "a" And c <= "z") Or (c >= "A" And c <= "Z") Or (c >= "0" And c <= "9") Or c = "_") Then Exit Function
    Next i
    L2_Simple = True
End Function

' --- условие NaN/бред (pas-юниты) ---
Private Function L2_IsName(ByVal s As String) As Boolean
    Dim i As Long, c As String
    L2_IsName = False
    If Len(s) = 0 Then Exit Function
    For i = 1 To Len(s)
        c = Mid(s, i, 1)
        If Not ((c >= "a" And c <= "z") Or (c >= "A" And c <= "Z") Or (c >= "0" And c <= "9") Or c = "_" Or c = ".") Then Exit Function
    Next i
    L2_IsName = True
End Function

' --- имя процедуры из строки заголовка ---
Private Function L2_ProcName(ByVal t As String) As String
    Dim s As String, p As Long, head As String
    L2_ProcName = ""
    s = Trim(t)
    If LCase(Left(s, 10)) = "procedure " Then
        s = Mid(s, 11)
    ElseIf LCase(Left(s, 9)) = "function " Then
        s = Mid(s, 10)
    Else
        Exit Function
    End If
    s = Trim(s)
    p = InStr(s, "(")
    If p = 0 Then p = InStr(s, ":")
    If p = 0 Then p = InStr(s, ";")
    If p <= 1 Then Exit Function
    head = Trim(Left(s, p - 1))
    If InStr(head, ".") > 0 Then head = Trim(Mid(head, InStr(head, ".") + 1))
    If L2_Simple(head) Then L2_ProcName = head
End Function

' --- дельта глубины begin/try/repeat/case - end/until (вне строк-литералов) ---
Private Function L2_Tok(ByVal tok As String, ByVal oldw As String, ByVal neww As String) As String
    If StrComp(tok, oldw, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then L2_Tok = neww Else L2_Tok = tok
End Function

Private Function L2_RenameWord(ByVal s As String, ByVal oldw As String, ByVal neww As String) As String
    Dim r As String, i As Long, c As String, tok As String, q As Boolean
    r = "": tok = "": q = False
    For i = 1 To Len(s)
        c = Mid(s, i, 1)
        If c = "'" Then
            q = Not q
            If tok <> "" Then r = r & L2_Tok(tok, oldw, neww): tok = ""
            r = r & c
        ElseIf Not q And ((c >= "a" And c <= "z") Or (c >= "A" And c <= "Z") Or (c >= "0" And c <= "9") Or c = "_") Then
            tok = tok & c
        Else
            If tok <> "" Then r = r & L2_Tok(tok, oldw, neww): tok = ""
            r = r & c
        End If
    Next i
    If tok <> "" Then r = r & L2_Tok(tok, oldw, neww)
    L2_RenameWord = r
End Function

' --- имя класса юнита (строка "=class(" до implementation) ---
Private Function L2_Delta(ByVal s As String) As Long
    Dim q As Boolean, i As Long, c As String, buf As String, t As String
    Dim d As Long, j As Long, w As String
    q = False: buf = ""
    For i = 1 To Len(s)
        c = Mid(s, i, 1)
        If c = "'" Then
            q = Not q: buf = buf & c
        ElseIf c = "/" And (Not q) And i < Len(s) And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "/" Then
            Exit For
        Else
            buf = buf & c
        End If
    Next i
    t = LCase(buf)
    If InStr(t, "begin") = 0 And InStr(t, "end") = 0 And InStr(t, "try") = 0 _
       And InStr(t, "repeat") = 0 And InStr(t, "until") = 0 And InStr(t, "case") = 0 Then
        L2_Delta = 0: Exit Function
    End If
    For i = 1 To Len(t)
        c = Mid(t, i, 1)
        If Not ((c >= "a" And c <= "z") Or (c >= "0" And c <= "9") Or c = "_") Then Mid(t, i, 1) = " "
    Next i
    d = 0
    Dim parts() As String
    parts = Split(t, " ")
    For j = LBound(parts) To UBound(parts)
        w = parts(j)
        If w = "begin" Or w = "try" Or w = "repeat" Then d = d + 1
        If w = "end" Or w = "until" Then d = d - 1
        If w = "case" Then d = d + 1
    Next j
    L2_Delta = d
End Function

' --- замена идентификатора на границах слов, вне строк-литералов ---
Private Function L2_ClassName(ByRef lines() As String, ByVal impl As Long) As String
    Dim i As Long, t As String, p As Long
    L2_ClassName = "T_Hmodel_UKR"
    For i = 1 To impl - 1
        t = Trim(lines(i))
        p = InStr(LCase(t), "=class(")
        If p > 1 Then
            Dim w As String: w = Trim(Left(t, p - 1))
            If L2_Simple(w) Then L2_ClassName = w: Exit Function
        End If
    Next i
End Function

' --- есть ли кириллица ВНЕ строк-литералов и вне "//" (Ф5, перенос Ф4 из LH1) ---
Private Sub L2_CollectForVars(ByVal s As String, ByRef forSet As Object)
    Dim p As Long, e As Long, q2 As Long, nm2 As String
    p = 1
    Do While p > 0 And p <= Len(s)
        p = InStr(p, LCase(s), "for ")
        If p = 0 Then Exit Do
        q2 = p + 4
        Do While q2 <= Len(s) And Mid(s, q2, 1) = " "
            q2 = q2 + 1
        Loop
        e = q2
        Do While e <= Len(s)
            If InStr("abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789_", LCase(Mid(s, e, 1))) = 0 Then Exit Do
            e = e + 1
        Loop
        nm2 = LCase(Mid(s, q2, e - q2))
        If Len(nm2) > 0 Then
            If Not forSet.Exists(nm2) Then forSet.Add nm2, 1
        End If
        p = e
    Loop
End Sub

Private Sub L2_PushL(ByRef out() As String, ByRef m As Long, ByRef cap As Long, ByVal s As String)
    m = m + 1
    If m > cap Then cap = cap * 2: ReDim Preserve out(1 To cap)
    out(m) = s
End Sub

Private Sub L2_PushR(ByRef out() As String, ByRef m As Long, ByRef cap As Long, ByRef arr() As String, ByVal a As Long, ByVal z As Long)
    Dim i As Long
    For i = a To z
        L2_PushL out, m, cap, arr(i)
    Next i
End Sub

' --- строки регистрации прохождения процедуры (слоты Miss_proc 1..100 - штатный пакет м2) ---
Private Sub L2_RegLines(ByRef out() As String, ByRef m As Long, ByRef cap As Long, ByVal slot As Long, ByVal pn As String)
    L2_PushL out, m, cap, "    if (Miss_comm=0) then Miss_proc[ " & slot & "]:='" & pn & " kerr='+CStr(kerr)+'  IST='+CURRENT_I_NAME+' // ' ; // индикатор прохождения процедуры // LH2 v28:"
    L2_PushL out, m, cap, "    if (Miss_comm=0) then Miss_ind[ " & slot & "]:=Miss_ind[ " & slot & "]+1; // LH2 v28:"
    L2_PushL out, m, cap, "    if (Miss_comm=0) then Miss_kerr[ " & slot & "]:=Round(kerr); // LH2 v28:"
    L2_PushL out, m, cap, "    if (Miss_comm=1) then Miss_proc[ " & slot & "]:='' ; // индикатор прохождения процедуры // LH2 v28:"
    L2_PushL out, m, cap, "    if (Miss_comm=0) then Miss_proc_2[ " & slot & "]:=' '+CStr(kerr)+''; // индикатор прохождения процедуры // LH2 v28:"
    L2_PushL out, m, cap, "    if (Miss_comm=1) then Miss_proc_2[ " & slot & "]:='' ; // индикатор прохождения процедуры // LH2 v28:"
End Sub

Private Sub L2_RegLinesDisp(ByRef out() As String, ByRef m As Long, ByRef cap As Long, ByVal slot As Long, ByVal pn As String)
    L2_RegLines out, m, cap, slot, pn
    L2_PushL out, m, cap, "    if (INT_first_step=1) then Log(' " & pn & " - SUCSESS '); // помечаем проход части // LH2 v28:"
End Sub

' --- замена номера слота в гейте "if (Miss_ind[ N]<4) then" ---
Private Function L2_SlotSub(ByVal s As String, ByVal slot As Long) As String
    Dim p1 As Long, p2 As Long, num As String
    p1 = InStr(s, "Miss_ind[")
    If p1 = 0 Then L2_SlotSub = s: Exit Function
    p2 = InStr(p1, s, "]")
    If p2 <= p1 Then L2_SlotSub = s: Exit Function
    num = Trim(Mid(s, p1 + 9, p2 - p1 - 9))
    L2_SlotSub = Left(s, p1 + 8) & CStr(slot) & Mid(s, p2)
End Function

' --- замена номера слота во ВСЕХ рег-строках блока (Miss_proc/Miss_ind/Miss_kerr/Miss_proc_2) ---
Private Function L2_SlotAll(ByVal s As String, ByVal newS As Long) As String
    Dim r As String, p As Long, q As Long, keys As Variant, i As Long
    r = s
    keys = Array("Miss_proc[", "Miss_ind[", "Miss_kerr[", "Miss_proc_2[")
    For i = LBound(keys) To UBound(keys)
        p = InStr(r, keys(i))
        Do While p > 0
            q = InStr(p, r, "]")
            If q > p Then r = Left(r, p + Len(keys(i)) - 1) & CStr(newS) & Mid(r, q)
            p = InStr(p + 1, r, keys(i))
        Loop
    Next i
    L2_SlotAll = r
End Function

' --- максимальное тело процедуры в файле (исходник) ---
Private Function L2_MaxSourceBody(ByVal fPath As String) As Long
    Dim fn As Integer, n As Long, cap As Long, ln As String
    Dim lines() As String, i As Long, k As Long, t As String
    Dim np As Long, mx As Long, jb As Long, ke As Long, dd As Long, okk As Boolean
    L2_MaxSourceBody = 0
    If Len(Dir(fPath)) = 0 Then Exit Function
    fn = FreeFile
    Open fPath For Input As #fn
    n = 0: cap = 1000: ReDim lines(1 To cap)
    Do While Not EOF(fn)
        Line Input #fn, ln
        n = n + 1
        If n > cap Then cap = cap * 2: ReDim Preserve lines(1 To cap)
        lines(n) = ln
    Loop
    Close #fn
    ReDim Preserve lines(1 To n)
    Dim impl As Long: impl = 0
    For i = 1 To n
        If LCase(Trim(lines(i))) = "implementation" Then impl = i: Exit For
    Next i
    If impl = 0 Then Exit Function
    np = 0: mx = 0
    i = impl
    Do While i <= n
        t = Trim(lines(i))
        If (Left(LCase(t), 10) = "procedure " Or Left(LCase(t), 9) = "function ") And Right(t, 1) = ";" Then
            Dim nm As String: nm = L2_ProcName(t)
            jb = 0
            If Len(nm) > 0 Then
                Dim j As Long: j = i + 1
                Do While j <= n
                    If Trim(lines(j)) = "begin" Then jb = j: Exit Do
                    Dim t2 As String: t2 = Trim(lines(j))
                    If Left(LCase(t2), 10) = "procedure " Or Left(LCase(t2), 9) = "function " Then Exit Do
                    j = j + 1
                Loop
                If jb > i Then
                    ke = 0: okk = False: dd = 1
                    For k = jb + 1 To n
                        dd = dd + L2_Delta(lines(k))
                        If dd <= 0 Then ke = k: okk = True: Exit For
                    Next k
                    If okk Then
                        np = np + 1
                        If ke - jb > mx Then mx = ke - jb
                        i = ke + 1
                    Else
                        i = i + 1
                    End If
                Else
                    i = i + 1
                End If
            Else
                i = i + 1
            End If
        Else
            i = i + 1
        End If
    Loop
    L2_MaxSourceBody = mx
End Function

' --- точка разреза: коммент-строка или завершённая ';'-строка на нулевой глубине,
'     за которой не начинается продолжение выражения ---
' --- точка разреза: коммент-строка или завершённая ';'-строка на глубине depth,
'     за которой не начинается продолжение выражения ---
Private Sub L2_CutCandidates(ByRef body() As String, ByVal bn As Long, ByRef cand() As Long, ByRef ncand As Long, ByVal depth As Long)
    Dim k As Long, dd As Long, t As String, jn As Long, nx As String, badn As Boolean
    ncand = 0: dd = 0
    For k = 1 To bn
        dd = dd + L2_Delta(body(k))
        If dd = depth Then
            t = Trim(body(k))
            If Left(t, 2) = "//" Then
                ncand = ncand + 1
                If ncand > UBound(cand) Then ReDim Preserve cand(1 To ncand * 2)
                cand(ncand) = k
            ElseIf Right(t, 1) = ";" And LCase(Left(t, 3)) <> "end" And LCase(Left(t, 4)) <> "else" Then
                jn = k + 1
                Do While jn <= bn
                    If Len(Trim(body(jn))) > 0 Then Exit Do
                    jn = jn + 1
                Loop
                nx = ""
                If jn <= bn Then nx = LCase(Left(Trim(body(jn)), 4))
                badn = (nx = "else") Or (Left(nx, 3) = "end") Or (Left(nx, 4) = "unti") _
                    Or (Left(nx, 1) = "+") Or (Left(nx, 1) = "-") Or (Left(nx, 3) = "and") _
                    Or (Left(nx, 2) = "or") Or (Left(nx, 1) = ".")
                If Not badn Then
                    ncand = ncand + 1
                    If ncand > UBound(cand) Then ReDim Preserve cand(1 To ncand * 2)
                    cand(ncand) = k
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next k
End Sub

' --- суффикс части: a..z, затем za, zb, ... ---
Private Function L2_Suf(ByVal k As Long) As String
    If k <= 26 Then
        L2_Suf = Chr(96 + k)
    Else
        L2_Suf = "z" & CStr(k - 26)
    End If
End Function


' --- ближайшая к середине точка из списка кандидатов ---
Private Function L2_BestCut(ByRef cand() As Long, ByVal ncand As Long, ByVal half As Double) As Long
    Dim k As Long, bst As Long
    bst = 0
    For k = 1 To ncand
        If cand(k) > 0 And cand(k) < half * 2 Then
            If bst = 0 Or Abs(cand(k) - half) < Abs(bst - half) Then bst = cand(k)
        End If
    Next k
    L2_BestCut = bst
End Function

Private Function L2_MonoStrip(ByVal s As String) As String
    Dim i As Long, q2 As Boolean, r As String, c As String
    q2 = False: r = ""
    For i = 1 To Len(s)
        c = Mid(s, i, 1)
        If c = "'" Then
            q2 = Not q2
        ElseIf c = "/" And (Not q2) And i < Len(s) And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "/" Then
            Exit For
        ElseIf Not q2 Then
            r = r & c
        End If
    Next i
    L2_MonoStrip = r
End Function

Private Function L2_MonoWords(ByVal s As String) As String()
    Dim r() As String
    Dim i As Long, c As String, buf As String, n As Long
    For i = 1 To Len(s)
        c = Mid(s, i, 1)
        If (c >= "a" And c <= "z") Or (c >= "0" And c <= "9") Or c = "_" Then
            buf = buf & c
        Else
            If Len(buf) > 0 Then
                n = n + 1
                ReDim Preserve r(1 To n)
                r(n) = buf
                buf = ""
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    If Len(buf) > 0 Then
        n = n + 1
        ReDim Preserve r(1 To n)
        r(n) = buf
    End If
    If n = 0 Then
        ReDim r(1 To 1)
        r(1) = ""
    End If
    L2_MonoWords = r
End Function

'=====================================================================
' Ф32а v21: в-блочная резка монолитного блока с дублированием условия.
' Возвращает True, если резала. Если в блоке многострочный цикл - НЕ режет,
' ставит cycFlag=1 (для Ф32б). Правила и стражи - см. шапку v21.
'=====================================================================
Private Function L2_MonoSplit(ByRef base1() As String, ByRef bn1 As Long, _
    ByVal thrMono As Long, ByRef nParts As Long, ByRef maxPart As Long, _
    ByRef msg As String, ByRef cycFlag As Long) As Boolean
    Dim i As Long, j As Long, k As Long, dd As Long, pv As Long
    Dim bA As Long, be As Long, blen As Long
    Dim ifStart As Long, t As String, tt As String
    Dim condTxt() As String, nCond As Long
    Dim cvars() As String, nc As Long, ws() As String, w As String
    Dim badCond As Boolean, oneSided As Long, hasB As Boolean, hasE As Boolean, hasC As Boolean
    Dim cand() As Long, ncand As Long, jn As Long, nx As String, badn As Boolean
    Dim np As Long, tg As Long, bS As Long, lastc As Long
    Dim cts() As Long
    Dim out2() As String, m2 As Long, cap2 As Long, pLen As Long, pE2 As Long
    L2_MonoSplit = False: nParts = 0: maxPart = 0: msg = "": cycFlag = 0
    bA = 0: be = 0: dd = 0
    For i = 1 To bn1
        pv = dd
        dd = dd + L2_Delta(base1(i))
        If pv = 0 And dd > 0 Then bA = i
        If dd = 0 And pv > 0 And bA > 0 Then
            If (i - bA - 1) > thrMono Then
                be = i: Exit For
            Else
                bA = 0: be = 0
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    If bA = 0 Then Exit Function
    blen = be - bA - 1
    ifStart = bA
    For i = bA - 1 To 1 Step -1
        t = LCase(Trim(base1(i)))
        If Len(t) = 0 Then
            ifStart = i
        ElseIf Left(t, 3) = "if " Or Left(t, 3) = "if(" Or Left(t, 4) = "elseif" Then
            ifStart = i: Exit For
        Else
            ifStart = bA: Exit For
        End If
    Next i
    nCond = bA - ifStart + 1
    ReDim condTxt(1 To nCond)
    For i = ifStart To bA
        condTxt(i - ifStart + 1) = base1(i)
    Next i
    nc = 0: ReDim cvars(1 To 16)
    For i = ifStart To bA
        ws = L2_MonoWords(L2_MonoStrip(base1(i)))
        For j = LBound(ws) To UBound(ws)
            w = ws(j)
            If Len(w) > 0 And w <> "if" And w <> "then" And w <> "begin" _
               And w <> "and" And w <> "or" And w <> "not" And w <> "elseif" _
               And w <> "end" And w <> "=" Then
                If Not ((Left(w, 1) >= "0") And (Left(w, 1) <= "9")) Then
                    nc = nc + 1
                    If nc > UBound(cvars) Then ReDim Preserve cvars(1 To nc * 2)
                    cvars(nc) = w
                End If
            End If
        Next j
    Next i
    oneSided = 0: badCond = False
    For i = bA + 1 To be - 1
        tt = LCase(L2_MonoStrip(base1(i)))
        ws = L2_MonoWords(tt)
        hasB = False: hasE = False: hasC = False
        For j = LBound(ws) To UBound(ws)
            w = ws(j)
            If w = "begin" Or w = "repeat" Then hasB = True
            If w = "end" Or w = "until" Then hasE = True
            If w = "for" Or w = "while" Or w = "case" Then hasC = True
        Next j
        If hasC And Not hasB And Not hasE Then oneSided = oneSided + 1
        If hasC And hasB And Not hasE Then cycFlag = 1
        If (Not badCond) And nc > 0 Then
            For j = 1 To nc
                If InStr(1, tt, cvars(j) & ":=") > 0 Then
                    badCond = True
                    Exit For
                End If
                If InStr(1, tt, cvars(j) & " :=") > 0 Then
                    badCond = True
                    Exit For
                End If
            Next j
        End If
    Next i
    If oneSided > 0 Then
        msg = "Ф32 v21: блок длиной " & CStr(blen) & " НЕ РЕЗАН: одностороние цикл-строки (" & CStr(oneSided) & ") - резка могла бы разорвать цикл"
        Exit Function
    End If
    If badCond Then
        msg = "Ф32 v21: блок длиной " & CStr(blen) & " НЕ РЕЗАН: переменная условия присваивается внутри блока (дублирование условия небезопасно)"
        Exit Function
    End If
    If cycFlag = 1 Then
        msg = "Ф32 v21: блок длиной " & CStr(blen) & " содержит многострочный цикl - в-блочная резка с дублированием условия НЕЛЬЗЯ, путь Ф32б (вынос тела цикла)"
        Exit Function
    End If
    ncand = 0: ReDim cand(1 To 64)
    dd = 0
    For i = bA + 1 To be - 1
        t = base1(i)
        pv = dd
        dd = dd + L2_Delta(t)
        If dd = 0 And pv = 0 Then
            tt = Trim(t)
            If Left(tt, 2) = "//" Then
                ncand = ncand + 1
                If ncand > UBound(cand) Then ReDim Preserve cand(1 To ncand * 2)
                cand(ncand) = i - bA
            ElseIf Right(tt, 1) = ";" And LCase(Left(tt, 3)) <> "end" And LCase(Left(tt, 4)) <> "else" Then
                jn = i + 1
                Do While jn <= be - 1
                    If Len(Trim(base1(jn))) > 0 Then Exit Do
                    jn = jn + 1
                Loop
                nx = ""
                If jn <= be - 1 Then nx = LCase(Left(Trim(base1(jn)), 4))
                badn = (nx = "else") Or (Left(nx, 3) = "end") Or (Left(nx, 4) = "unti") _
                    Or (Left(nx, 1) = "+") Or (Left(nx, 1) = "-") Or (Left(nx, 3) = "and") _
                    Or (Left(nx, 2) = "or") Or (Left(nx, 1) = ".")
                If Not badn Then
                    ncand = ncand + 1
                    If ncand > UBound(cand) Then ReDim Preserve cand(1 To ncand * 2)
                    cand(ncand) = i - bA
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    If ncand = 0 Then
        msg = "Ф32 v21: блок длиной " & CStr(blen) & " НЕ РЕЗАН: точек реза внутри блока нет"
        Exit Function
    End If
    np = (blen + thrMono - 1) \ thrMono
    If np < 2 Then np = 2
    ReDim cts(0 To np)
    lastc = 0
    For k = 1 To np - 1
        tg = k * blen \ np
        bS = 0
        For j = 1 To ncand
            If cand(j) > lastc Then
                If bS = 0 Or Abs(cand(j) - tg) < Abs(bS - tg) Then bS = cand(j)
            End If
        Next j
        If bS = 0 Then bS = blen - 1
        If bS >= blen Then bS = blen - 1
        If bS <= lastc Then bS = lastc + 1
        cts(k) = bS: lastc = bS
    Next k
    cts(np) = blen
    cap2 = bn1 + np * (nCond + 2) + 16
    ReDim out2(1 To cap2): m2 = 0
    For i = 1 To bA
        L2_PushL out2, m2, cap2, base1(i)
    Next i
    For k = 1 To np
        pLen = cts(k) - cts(k - 1)
        If pLen > maxPart Then maxPart = pLen
        For i = bA + cts(k - 1) + 1 To bA + cts(k)
            L2_PushL out2, m2, cap2, base1(i)
        Next i
        If k < np Then
            ' копия end-строки БЕЗ kerr-хвоста (дублирование kerr недопустимо - Ф32 v21)
            pE2 = InStr(base1(be), ";")
            If pE2 > 0 Then
                L2_PushL out2, m2, cap2, Left(base1(be), pE2)
            Else
                L2_PushL out2, m2, cap2, base1(be)
            End If
            For i = ifStart To bA
                L2_PushL out2, m2, cap2, base1(i)
            Next i
        End If
    Next k
    For i = be To bn1
        L2_PushL out2, m2, cap2, base1(i)
    Next i
    ReDim Preserve base1(1 To m2)
    For i = 1 To m2
        base1(i) = out2(i)
    Next i
    bn1 = m2
    nParts = np
    msg = "Ф32а v21: блок " & CStr(blen) & " строк -> " & CStr(np) & " кусков, макс. кусок " & CStr(maxPart) & " (лимит в-блочной резки " & CStr(thrMono) & ")"
    L2_MonoSplit = True
End Function

' --- поиск строки декларации процедуры в interface (для вставки частей) ---
Private Function L2_FindDecl(ByRef lines() As String, ByVal impl As Long, ByVal nm As String) As Long
    Dim k As Long, t As String, dn As String
    L2_FindDecl = 0
    For k = 1 To impl - 1
        t = Trim(lines(k))
        If LCase(Left(t, 10)) = "procedure " Then
            dn = Mid(Trim(Mid(t, 11)), 1)
            If InStr(dn, ".") > 0 Then dn = Mid(dn, InStr(dn, ".") + 1)
            Dim sp1 As Long, sp2 As Long, sp3 As Long, dm As Long
            sp1 = InStr(dn, " "): sp2 = InStr(dn, "("): sp3 = InStr(dn, ";")
            dm = Len(dn) + 1
            If sp1 > 0 And sp1 < dm Then dm = sp1
            If sp2 > 0 And sp2 < dm Then dm = sp2
            If sp3 > 0 And sp3 < dm Then dm = sp3
            If dm > 1 Then dn = Trim(Left(dn, dm - 1)) Else dn = ""
            If StrComp(dn, nm, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then L2_FindDecl = k: Exit Function
        End If
    Next k
End Function


'=====================================================================
' ПОСТ-ПРОХОД: a/b-разбиение перегруженных процедур (LH2 v28)
' Порог = НАСТРАИВАЕМЫЙ лимит компилятора (v22; раньше - макс. исходника -
' при комплектах с исходными телами за лимитом разбивалось НОЛЬ процедур).
' Тело > порога делится на N частей (каждая <= порога). try-обёртка тела:
' кандидаты - границы узловых блоков ВНУТРИ try (dd=1). Переростки-блоки:
' тело блока выносится в части, вызовы - внутри блока. Локалы -> поля класса.
' Объявления частей - в interface. splitsD: имя -> число частей (для диспетчера).
'=====================================================================
Private Function L2_Split(ByVal fPath As String, ByVal thr As Long, ByRef nChunks As Long, ByRef splitsD As Object, ByRef mainOut As String) As Long
    Dim fn As Integer, lines() As String, n As Long, cap As Long, ln As String
    Dim i As Long, k As Long, t As String, dd As Long, kk As Long
    L2_Split = 0
    gNoParamSkipped = 0

    If Len(Dir(fPath)) = 0 Then Exit Function
    fn = FreeFile
    Open fPath For Input As #fn
    n = 0: cap = 1000: ReDim lines(1 To cap)
    Do While Not EOF(fn)
        Line Input #fn, ln
        n = n + 1
        If n > cap Then cap = cap * 2: ReDim Preserve lines(1 To cap)
        lines(n) = ln
    Loop
    Close #fn
    ReDim Preserve lines(1 To n)
    If n < 100 Then Exit Function
    If Len(mainOut) = 0 Then
        For i = 1 To n
            t = LCase(Trim(lines(i)))
            If Left(t, 10) = "procedure " And Right(t, 6) = ".work;" Then     ' v27: ".work;" = 6 символов; Right(t,7) никогда не совпадал -> mainOut не ставился, L2_Disp не вызывался
                mainOut = fPath: Exit For
            End If
        Next i
    End If
    Dim impl As Long: impl = 0
    For i = 1 To n
        If LCase(Trim(lines(i))) = "implementation" Then impl = i: Exit For
    Next i
    If impl = 0 Then Exit Function
    Dim cls As String: cls = L2_ClassName(lines, impl)
    ' --- фаза 1: инвентаризация процедур ---
    Dim pH() As Long, pB() As Long, pE() As Long, pn() As String
    Dim np As Long: np = 0
    ReDim pH(1 To 64): ReDim pB(1 To 64): ReDim pE(1 To 64): ReDim pn(1 To 64)
    Dim names As Object: Set names = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    i = impl
    Do While i <= n
        t = Trim(lines(i))
        If (Left(LCase(t), 10) = "procedure " Or Left(LCase(t), 9) = "function ") And Right(t, 1) = ";" Then
            Dim nm As String: nm = L2_ProcName(t)
            Dim jb As Long: jb = 0
            ' FIX v35 (Ф14): процедуры с параметрами НЕ режем - части теряют параметры (u1..u6/Tfact/Pfact, NOMist и пр.): FPC "Identifier not found"
            If Len(nm) > 0 And InStr(t, "(") = 0 Then
                Dim j As Long: j = i + 1
                Do While j <= n
                    If Trim(lines(j)) = "begin" Then jb = j: Exit Do
                    Dim t2 As String: t2 = Trim(lines(j))
                    If Left(LCase(t2), 10) = "procedure " Or Left(LCase(t2), 9) = "function " Then Exit Do
                    j = j + 1
                Loop
                If jb > i Then
                    Dim ke As Long, okk As Boolean
                    ke = 0: okk = False: dd = 1
                    For k = jb + 1 To n
                        dd = dd + L2_Delta(lines(k))
                        If dd <= 0 Then ke = k: okk = True: Exit For
                    Next k
                    If okk Then
                        np = np + 1
                        If np > UBound(pH) Then
                            ReDim Preserve pH(1 To np * 2): ReDim Preserve pB(1 To np * 2)
                            ReDim Preserve pE(1 To np * 2): ReDim Preserve pn(1 To np * 2)
                        End If
                        pH(np) = i: pB(np) = jb: pE(np) = ke: pn(np) = nm
                        If Not names.Exists(nm) Then names.Add nm, 1
                        i = ke + 1
                    Else
                        i = i + 1
                    End If
                Else
                    i = i + 1
                End If
            Else
                If InStr(t, "(") > 0 Then gNoParamSkipped = gNoParamSkipped + 1   ' FIX v35 (Ф14)
                i = i + 1
            End If
        Else
            i = i + 1
        End If
    Loop
    If np = 0 Then Exit Function
    ' --- фаза 2: пересборка ---
    Dim out() As String, m As Long, ocap As Long
    ocap = n + 512: ReDim out(1 To ocap): m = 0
    Dim pos As Long: pos = 1
    Dim nspl As Long: nspl = 0
    Dim inIdx() As Long, inTxt() As String, nin As Long, icap2 As Long
    icap2 = 16: ReDim inIdx(1 To icap2): ReDim inTxt(1 To icap2): nin = 0
    ' единый блок полей-локалов в начале первой секции видимости класса
    Dim Fall() As String, nfAll As Long
    ReDim Fall(1 To 16): nfAll = 0
    Dim gAll() As String, ng As Long   ' Ф12: глобальные for-счётчики юнита (копится ПО ВСЕМ процедурам - ВНЕ цикла!)
    ReDim gAll(1 To 16): ng = 0
    Dim clsLn As Long, secLn As Long
    clsLn = 0: secLn = 0
    Dim pi As Long
    For pi = 1 To np
        Dim h As Long, b As Long, e As Long
        h = pH(pi): b = pB(pi): e = pE(pi): nm = pn(pi)
        If h > pos Then L2_PushR out, m, ocap, lines, pos, h - 1
        pos = h
        If e - b <= thr Or L2_FindDecl(lines, impl, nm) = 0 Then   ' FIX v35 (Ф15): без декларации в interface не режем
            L2_PushR out, m, ocap, lines, h, e
            pos = e + 1
        Else
            Dim bL() As String, bln As Long
            bln = e - b - 1
            ReDim bL(1 To bln)
            For k = 1 To bln: bL(k) = lines(b + k): Next k
            ' отделяем хвост except (первый except на глубине 1)
            Dim idxExc As Long: idxExc = 0
            dd = 0
            For k = 1 To bln
                If dd = 1 And (Trim(bL(k)) = "except" Or Left(Trim(bL(k)), 7) = "except ") Then idxExc = k: Exit For
                dd = dd + L2_Delta(bL(k))
            Next k
            Dim inner() As String, inn As Long, icap3 As Long
            Dim exc() As String, exn As Long, ecap As Long
            icap3 = bln + 8: ReDim inner(1 To icap3): inn = 0
            ecap = bln + 8: ReDim exc(1 To ecap): exn = 0
            If idxExc > 0 Then
                For k = 1 To idxExc - 1: L2_PushL inner, inn, icap3, bL(k): Next k
                For k = idxExc To bln: L2_PushL exc, exn, ecap, bL(k): Next k
            Else
                For k = 1 To bln: L2_PushL inner, inn, icap3, bL(k): Next k
                exn = 0
            End If
            ' локалы -> поля класса (замена ссылок в inner/exc)
            ' Ф12 v34: for-счётчики НЕ в поля класса (FPC "Illegal counter variable"),
            ' а в глобальные переменные юнита (вставка перед implementation)
            Dim forSet As Object: Set forSet = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
            For kk = 1 To inn
                L2_CollectForVars inner(kk), forSet
            Next kk
            For kk = 1 To exn
                L2_CollectForVars exc(kk), forSet
            Next kk
            Dim fldNm() As String, nfl As Long
            ReDim fldNm(1 To 16): nfl = 0
            For k = h + 1 To b - 1
                t = Trim(lines(k))
                If Len(t) > 0 And LCase(t) <> "var" And Left(t, 2) <> "//" And Left(t, 1) <> "{" Then     ' v26: комментарии в var-секции НЕ локалы (там поля класса)
                    Dim pp As Long: pp = InStr(t, ":")
                    If pp > 1 Then
                        Dim lhs As String: lhs = Trim(Left(t, pp - 1))
                        Dim tp As String: tp = Trim(Mid(t, pp + 1))
                        If Right(tp, 1) = ";" Then tp = Trim(Left(tp, Len(tp) - 1))
                        Dim vv() As String: vv = Split(lhs, ",")
                        Dim vi As Long
                        For vi = LBound(vv) To UBound(vv)
                            Dim vn As String: vn = Trim(vv(vi))
                            If Len(vn) > 0 And L2_Simple(vn) Then
                                nfl = nfl + 1
                                If nfl > UBound(fldNm) Then ReDim Preserve fldNm(1 To nfl * 2)
                                If forSet.Exists(LCase(vn)) Then
                                    ' Ф12 v34: for-счётчик -> глобальная переменная юнита
                                    ng = ng + 1
                                    If ng > UBound(gAll) Then ReDim Preserve gAll(1 To ng * 2)
                                    gAll(ng) = nm & "_" & vn & ": " & tp & ";"
                                Else
                                    fldNm(nfl) = nm & "_" & vn
                                    nfAll = nfAll + 1
                                    If nfAll > UBound(Fall) Then ReDim Preserve Fall(1 To nfAll * 2)
                                    Fall(nfAll) = nm & "_" & vn & ": " & tp & ";  // LH2 v28: локал процедуры " & nm
                                End If
                                For kk = 1 To inn: inner(kk) = L2_RenameWord(inner(kk), vn, nm & "_" & vn): Next kk
                                For kk = 1 To exn: exc(kk) = L2_RenameWord(exc(kk), vn, nm & "_" & vn): Next kk
                            End If
                        Next vi
                    End If
                End If
            Next k
            If inn <= 0 Then
                L2_PushR out, m, ocap, lines, h, e
                pos = e + 1
            Else
                ' --- v22: try-обёртка + N-частичная резка ---
                Dim td1 As Long, bn1 As Long
                Dim base1() As String
                ReDim base1(1 To inn + 8)
                td1 = 0
                For k = 1 To inn: base1(k) = inner(k): Next k
                bn1 = inn
                If bn1 > 0 And (Trim(base1(1)) = "try" Or Left(Trim(base1(1)), 4) = "try ") Then
                    td1 = 1
                    For k = 2 To bn1: base1(k - 1) = inner(k): Next k
                    bn1 = bn1 - 1
                End If
                Dim di0 As Long
                ' Ф32 v21: в-блочная резка монолита с дублированием условия (решение владельца 24.11),
                ' ДО обычной N-резки: у монолита точек dd=0 нет, обычная резка дала бы кусок-монолит.
                ' Ф32б: если в блоке многострочный цикл - тело цикла выносится в части-процедуры (ниже).
                Dim monoApplied As Boolean, monoParts As Long, monoMax As Long, monoMsg As String
                Dim monoCyc As Long, lbA As Long, lbE As Long, llen As Long
                Dim lhasB As Boolean, lhasE As Boolean, ldd As Long, lpv As Long
                monoMsg = "": monoParts = 0: monoMax = 0: monoCyc = 0: lbA = 0: lbE = 0
                monoApplied = L2_MonoSplit(base1, bn1, 4000, monoParts, monoMax, monoMsg, monoCyc)   ' порог в-блочной резки = 4000
                If monoApplied Then
                    If td1 = 1 Then
                        ReDim Preserve inner(1 To bn1 + 1)
                        inner(1) = "try"
                        For k = 1 To bn1
                        inner(k + 1) = base1(k)
                        Next k
                        inn = bn1 + 1
                    Else
                        ReDim Preserve inner(1 To bn1)
                        For k = 1 To bn1
                        inner(k) = base1(k)
                        Next k
                        inn = bn1
                    End If
                    If monoMax > gMaxPart Then gMaxPart = monoMax
                    gMonoPieces = gMonoPieces + monoParts
                    gMonoBlocks = gMonoBlocks + 1
                    L1_Log "Ф32а v23 [" & nm & "]: " & monoMsg & " -> копии вставлены в тело, режет N-резка"
                End If
                If monoCyc = 1 Then
                    ' --- Ф32б v21: вынос тела цикла-переростка в части-процедуры ---
                    ' цикл ищется на ЛЮБОЙ глубине: открыватель for/while с begin в строке
                    ' и без end/until в той же строке; тело цикла - строки до возврата
                    ' глубины к значению перед циклом; ищем тело > 4000 строк
                    Dim lws() As String, ltt As String
                    Dim lcand() As Long, lncand As Long, ld As Long, lpv2 As Long, ljn As Long, lnx As String, lbadn As Boolean
                    Dim lcts() As Long, llast As Long, ltg As Long, lbs As Long, lj As Long, mp2 As Long, lnP2 As Long
                    Dim lpn2() As String, lqn2 As String, lnb() As String, lnn2 As Long, lncap As Long
                    Dim lnewInner() As String, lni2 As Long, lnicap As Long, lbufI2 As String, lpq2() As String, lpqn2 As Long
                    dd = 0: lbA = 0: lbE = 0
                    For i = 1 To bn1
                        t = base1(i)
                        lpv = dd
                        dd = dd + L2_Delta(t)
                        If lbA = 0 Then
                            ltt = LCase(L2_MonoStrip(t))
                            If InStr(1, ltt, "for ") > 0 Or InStr(1, ltt, "while ") > 0 Then
                                lws = L2_MonoWords(ltt)
                                lhasB = False: lhasE = False
                                For k = LBound(lws) To UBound(lws)
                                    If lws(k) = "begin" Or lws(k) = "repeat" Then lhasB = True
                                    If lws(k) = "end" Or lws(k) = "until" Then lhasE = True
                                Next k
                                If lhasB And Not lhasE Then
                                    lbA = i
                                    ldd = lpv + L2_Delta(t)
                                    For j = i + 1 To bn1
                                        ldd = ldd + L2_Delta(base1(j))
                                        If ldd = lpv Then
                                            lbE = j
                                            Exit For
                                        End If
                                    Next j
                                    If lbE > 0 And (lbE - lbA - 1) > 4000 Then
                                        Exit For
                                    Else
                                        lbA = 0: lbE = 0
                                    End If
                                End If
                            End If
                        End If
                    Next i
                    If lbA = 0 Then
                        L1_Log "Ф32б v23 [" & nm & "]: тело цикла-переростка не найдено при cycFlag=1 - остаётся как есть"
                        L2_PushR out, m, ocap, lines, h, e
                        If (e - b) > gMaxPart Then gMaxPart = e - b
                        pos = e + 1
                        GoTo NextPi
                    End If
                    llen = lbE - lbA - 1
                    lncand = 0: ReDim lcand(1 To 64): ld = 0
                    For i = lbA + 1 To lbE - 1
                        lpv2 = ld
                        ld = ld + L2_Delta(base1(i))
                        If ld = 0 And lpv2 = 0 Then
                            ltt = Trim(base1(i))
                            If Left(ltt, 2) = "//" Then
                                lncand = lncand + 1
                                If lncand > UBound(lcand) Then ReDim Preserve lcand(1 To lncand * 2)
                                lcand(lncand) = i - lbA
                            ElseIf Right(ltt, 1) = ";" And LCase(Left(ltt, 3)) <> "end" And LCase(Left(ltt, 4)) <> "else" Then
                                ljn = i + 1
                                Do While ljn <= lbE - 1
                                    If Len(Trim(base1(ljn))) > 0 Then Exit Do
                                    ljn = ljn + 1
                                Loop
                                lnx = ""
                                If ljn <= lbE - 1 Then lnx = LCase(Left(Trim(base1(ljn)), 4))
                                lbadn = (lnx = "else") Or (Left(lnx, 3) = "end") Or (Left(lnx, 4) = "unti") _
                                    Or (Left(lnx, 1) = "+") Or (Left(lnx, 1) = "-") Or (Left(lnx, 3) = "and") _
                                    Or (Left(lnx, 2) = "or") Or (Left(lnx, 1) = ".")
                                If Not lbadn Then
                                    lncand = lncand + 1
                                    If lncand > UBound(lcand) Then ReDim Preserve lcand(1 To lncand * 2)
                                    lcand(lncand) = i - lbA
                                End If
                            End If
                        End If
                    Next i
                    If lncand = 0 Then
                        L1_Log "Ф32б v23 [" & nm & "]: точек реза в теле цикла нет - остаётся как есть"
                        L2_PushR out, m, ocap, lines, h, e
                        If (e - b) > gMaxPart Then gMaxPart = e - b
                        pos = e + 1
                        GoTo NextPi
                    End If
                    di0 = L2_FindDecl(lines, impl, nm)
                    If di0 = 0 Then
                        L1_Log "Ф32б v23 [" & nm & "]: без декларации в interface не режем (Ф15) - остаётся как есть"
                        L2_PushR out, m, ocap, lines, h, e
                        If (e - b) > gMaxPart Then gMaxPart = e - b
                        pos = e + 1
                        GoTo NextPi
                    End If
                    lnP2 = (llen + 3999) \ 4000
                    If lnP2 < 2 Then lnP2 = 2
                    ReDim lcts(0 To lnP2)
                    llast = 0
                    For lj = 1 To lnP2 - 1
                        ltg = lj * llen \ lnP2
                        lbs = 0
                        For k = 1 To lncand
                            If lcand(k) > llast Then
                                If lbs = 0 Or Abs(lcand(k) - ltg) < Abs(lbs - ltg) Then lbs = lcand(k)
                            End If
                        Next k
                        If lbs = 0 Then lbs = llen - 1
                        If lbs >= llen Then lbs = llen - 1
                        If lbs <= llast Then lbs = llast + 1
                        lcts(lj) = lbs: llast = lbs
                    Next lj
                    lcts(lnP2) = llen
                    ReDim lpn2(1 To lnP2)
                    mp2 = 0
                    For k = 1 To lnP2
                        lqn2 = nm & L2_Suf(k)
                        Do While names.Exists(lqn2)
                            lqn2 = lqn2 & "x"
                        Loop
                        names.Add lqn2, 1
                        lpn2(k) = lqn2
                    Next k
                    lncap = 24: ReDim lnb(1 To lncap): lnn2 = 0
                    For k = 1 To lnP2
                        L2_PushL lnb, lnn2, lncap, "    " & lpn2(k) & ";  // LH2 v22: часть " & k & " из " & lnP2 & " цикла " & nm
                    Next k
                    lnicap = inn + lnn2 + 8
                    ReDim lnewInner(1 To lnicap): lni2 = 0
                    For i = 1 To lbA + 1
                        L2_PushL lnewInner, lni2, lnicap, inner(i)
                    Next i
                    For i = 1 To lnn2
                        L2_PushL lnewInner, lni2, lnicap, lnb(i)
                    Next i
                    For i = lbE To inn
                        L2_PushL lnewInner, lni2, lnicap, inner(i)
                    Next i
                    inner = lnewInner: inn = lni2: icap3 = lnicap
                    lbufI2 = ""
                    For k = 1 To lnP2
                        lpqn2 = lcts(k) - lcts(k - 1)
                        ReDim lpq2(1 To lpqn2)
                        If lpqn2 > gMaxPart Then gMaxPart = lpqn2
                        If lpqn2 > mp2 Then mp2 = lpqn2
                        For i = 1 To lpqn2
                            lpq2(i) = base1(lbA + lcts(k - 1) + i)
                        Next i
                        L2_PushL out, m, ocap, ""
                        L2_PushL out, m, ocap, "procedure " & cls & "." & lpn2(k) & ";"
                        L2_PushL out, m, ocap, "begin"
                        L2_PushR out, m, ocap, lpq2, 1, lpqn2
                        L2_PushL out, m, ocap, "    Miss_comm:=1; // LH2 v22: расчёт дошёл до конца части"
                        L2_PushL out, m, ocap, "end;"
                        lbufI2 = lbufI2 & "    procedure " & lpn2(k) & ";      // LH2 v22: часть " & k & " из " & lnP2 & " цикла " & nm & vbCrLf
                    Next k
                    nin = nin + 1
                    If nin > icap2 Then icap2 = icap2 * 2: ReDim Preserve inIdx(1 To icap2): ReDim Preserve inTxt(1 To icap2)
                    inIdx(nin) = di0: inTxt(nin) = lbufI2
                    L2_PushR out, m, ocap, lines, h, h
                    L2_PushL out, m, ocap, "begin"
                    If td1 = 1 Then L2_PushL out, m, ocap, "  try"
                    If td1 = 1 Then L2_PushR out, m, ocap, inner, 2, inn Else L2_PushR out, m, ocap, inner, 1, inn
                    L2_PushR out, m, ocap, exc, 1, exn
                    L2_PushL out, m, ocap, "end;"
                    nspl = nspl + 1
                    nChunks = nChunks + lnP2
                    L1_Log "Ф32б v23 [" & nm & "]: тело цикла " & CStr(llen) & " строк -> " & CStr(lnP2) & " частей, макс. часть " & CStr(mp2)
                        If Not splitsD.Exists(nm) Then splitsD.Add nm, lnP2 Else splitsD(nm) = lnP2   ' v23: Ф32б регистрирует число частей
                    pos = e + 1
                    GoTo NextPi
                End If
                di0 = L2_FindDecl(lines, impl, nm)
                Dim cand1() As Long, ncand1 As Long
                ReDim cand1(1 To 64)
                If bn1 > 0 Then L2_CutCandidates base1, bn1, cand1, ncand1, 0     ' v26: рез только между top-level строками (рез по dd=1 ломал циклы for)
                If bn1 < 2 Or ncand1 = 0 Then
                    ' --- обычная резка невозможна: блок-переросток (N частей) ---
                    Dim blkApplied As Boolean: blkApplied = False
                    Dim dSt As Long, stCnt As Long
                    Dim stStack() As Long: ReDim stStack(1 To 64): stCnt = 0
                    Dim bbA() As Long, bbE() As Long, nbb As Long
                    ReDim bbA(1 To 16): ReDim bbE(1 To 16): nbb = 0
                    Dim pv As Long
                    For k = 1 To inn
                        pv = dSt
                        dSt = dSt + L2_Delta(inner(k))
                        If pv = 0 And dSt > 0 Then
                            stCnt = stCnt + 1
                            If stCnt > UBound(stStack) Then ReDim Preserve stStack(1 To stCnt * 2)
                            stStack(stCnt) = k
                        End If
                        If dSt = 0 And pv > 0 And stCnt = 1 Then
                            If (k - stStack(1) - 1) >= 2 Then
                                nbb = nbb + 1
                                If nbb > UBound(bbA) Then ReDim Preserve bbA(1 To nbb * 2): ReDim Preserve bbE(1 To nbb * 2)
                                bbA(nbb) = stStack(1): bbE(nbb) = k
                            End If
                            stCnt = stCnt - 1
                        End If
                    Next k
                    Dim bi As Long
                    For bi = nbb To 1 Step -1
                        Dim oB As Long, eB As Long
                        oB = bbA(bi): eB = bbE(bi)
                        Dim bbLen As Long: bbLen = eB - oB - 1
                        If bbLen >= 2 Then
                            Dim bh() As String: ReDim bh(1 To bbLen)
                            For k = 1 To bbLen: bh(k) = inner(oB + k): Next k
                            Dim c2() As Long, n2 As Long
                            ReDim c2(1 To 64): n2 = 0
                            L2_CutCandidates bh, bbLen, c2, n2, 0
                            If n2 > 0 Then
                                Dim nP2 As Long
                                nP2 = 2
                                Dim cts2() As Long, last2 As Long, nk2 As Long, tg2 As Long, bs2 As Long, bi2 As Long
                                ReDim cts2(0 To nP2 + 1)     ' v26: 0-based - чтение cts2(nk2-1) при nk2=1
                                last2 = 0
                                For nk2 = 1 To nP2 - 1
                                    tg2 = nk2 * bbLen \ nP2
                                    bs2 = 0
                                    For bi2 = 1 To n2
                                        If c2(bi2) > last2 Then
                                            If bs2 = 0 Or Abs(c2(bi2) - tg2) < Abs(bs2 - tg2) Then bs2 = c2(bi2)
                                        End If
                                    Next bi2
                                    If bs2 = 0 Then bs2 = bbLen - 1
                                    If bs2 >= bbLen Then bs2 = bbLen - 1     ' v26: щит пустой части 2
                                    cts2(nk2) = bs2: last2 = bs2
                                Next nk2
                                cts2(nP2) = bbLen
                                Dim pn2() As String, qn2 As String
                                ReDim pn2(1 To nP2)
                                For nk2 = 1 To nP2
                                    qn2 = nm & L2_Suf(nk2)
                                    Do While names.Exists(qn2): qn2 = qn2 & "x": Loop
                                    names.Add qn2, 1
                                    pn2(nk2) = qn2
                                Next nk2
                                Dim nb() As String, nn2 As Long, ncap As Long
                                ncap = 24: ReDim nb(1 To ncap): nn2 = 0
                                For nk2 = 1 To nP2
                                    L2_PushL nb, nn2, ncap, "    " & pn2(nk2) & ";  // LH2 v22: часть " & nk2 & " из " & nP2 & " блока " & nm
                                Next nk2
                                Dim newInner() As String, ni2 As Long, nicap As Long
                                nicap = inn + nn2 + 8: ReDim newInner(1 To nicap): ni2 = 0
                                For k = 1 To oB + 1: L2_PushL newInner, ni2, nicap, inner(k): Next k
                                For k = 1 To nn2: L2_PushL newInner, ni2, nicap, nb(k): Next k
                                For k = eB To inn: L2_PushL newInner, ni2, nicap, inner(k): Next k
                                inner = newInner: inn = ni2: icap3 = nicap
                                Dim bufI2 As String: bufI2 = ""
                                For nk2 = 1 To nP2
                                    Dim pq2() As String, pqn2 As Long
                                    pqn2 = cts2(nk2) - cts2(nk2 - 1)
                                    ReDim pq2(1 To pqn2)
                                    If pqn2 > gMaxPart Then gMaxPart = pqn2
                                    For k = 1 To pqn2: pq2(k) = bh(cts2(nk2 - 1) + k): Next k
                                    L2_PushL out, m, ocap, ""
                                    L2_PushL out, m, ocap, "procedure " & cls & "." & pn2(nk2) & ";"
                                    L2_PushL out, m, ocap, "begin"
                                    L2_PushR out, m, ocap, pq2, 1, pqn2
                                    L2_PushL out, m, ocap, "    Miss_comm:=1; // LH2 v22: расчёт дошёл до конца части"
                                    L2_PushL out, m, ocap, "end;"
                                    bufI2 = bufI2 & "    procedure " & pn2(nk2) & ";      // LH2 v22: часть " & nk2 & " из " & nP2 & " блока процедуры " & nm & vbCrLf
                                Next nk2
                                nin = nin + 1
                                If nin > icap2 Then icap2 = icap2 * 2: ReDim Preserve inIdx(1 To icap2): ReDim Preserve inTxt(1 To icap2)
                                inIdx(nin) = di0: inTxt(nin) = bufI2
                                If Not splitsD.Exists(nm) Then splitsD.Add nm, nP2 Else splitsD(nm) = nP2
                                blkApplied = True
                                nChunks = nChunks + nP2
                                nspl = nspl + 1
                            End If
                        End If
                    Next bi
                    If Not blkApplied Then
                        ' разрез невозможен: остаётся как есть
                        L2_PushR out, m, ocap, lines, h, e
                        If (e - b) > gMaxPart Then gMaxPart = e - b
                    Else
                        ' обёртка try/вызовы/except (внутренний блок уже с вызовами частей)
                        L2_PushR out, m, ocap, lines, h, h
                        L2_PushL out, m, ocap, "begin"
                        If td1 = 1 Then L2_PushL out, m, ocap, "  try"     ' v26: try только если тело имело try
                        If td1 = 1 Then L2_PushR out, m, ocap, inner, 2, inn Else L2_PushR out, m, ocap, inner, 1, inn
                        L2_PushR out, m, ocap, exc, 1, exn
                        L2_PushL out, m, ocap, "end;"
                    End If
                Else
                    ' --- N частей обычной резки (с учётом try-обёртки) ---
                    Dim nP1 As Long
                    nP1 = 2
                    If bn1 > 6000 Then nP1 = (bn1 + 5999) \ 6000   ' Ф11 v34: части > 6000 строк режутся на N (слоты 2N-1/2N + 53..99)
                    Dim cts1() As Long, last1 As Long, nk1 As Long, tg1 As Long, bs1 As Long, bi1 As Long
                    ReDim cts1(0 To nP1 + 1)     ' v26: 0-based - чтение cts1(nk1-1) при nk1=1
                    last1 = 0
                    For nk1 = 1 To nP1 - 1
                        tg1 = nk1 * bn1 \ nP1
                        bs1 = 0
                        For bi1 = 1 To ncand1
                            If cand1(bi1) > last1 Then
                                If bs1 = 0 Or Abs(cand1(bi1) - tg1) < Abs(bs1 - tg1) Then bs1 = cand1(bi1)
                            End If
                        Next bi1
                        If bs1 = 0 Then bs1 = bn1 - 1
                        If bs1 >= bn1 Then bs1 = bn1 - 1     ' v26: щит пустой части 2
                        cts1(nk1) = bs1: last1 = bs1
                    Next nk1
                    cts1(nP1) = bn1
                    Dim pn1() As String, qn1 As String
                    ReDim pn1(1 To nP1)
                    For nk1 = 1 To nP1
                        qn1 = nm & L2_Suf(nk1)
                        Do While names.Exists(qn1): qn1 = qn1 & "x": Loop
                        names.Add qn1, 1
                        pn1(nk1) = qn1
                    Next nk1
                    L2_PushR out, m, ocap, lines, h, h
                    L2_PushL out, m, ocap, "begin"
                    If td1 = 1 Then L2_PushL out, m, ocap, "  try"
                    For nk1 = 1 To nP1
                        L2_PushL out, m, ocap, "    " & pn1(nk1) & ";  // LH2 v22: часть " & nk1 & " из " & nP1 & " процедуры " & nm
                    Next nk1
                    L2_PushR out, m, ocap, exc, 1, exn
                    L2_PushL out, m, ocap, "end;"
                    Dim bufI1 As String: bufI1 = ""
                    For nk1 = 1 To nP1
                        Dim pq1() As String, pqn1 As Long
                        pqn1 = cts1(nk1) - cts1(nk1 - 1)
                        ReDim pq1(1 To pqn1)
                        If pqn1 > gMaxPart Then gMaxPart = pqn1
                        For k = 1 To pqn1: pq1(k) = base1(cts1(nk1 - 1) + k): Next k
                        L2_PushL out, m, ocap, ""
                        L2_PushL out, m, ocap, "procedure " & cls & "." & pn1(nk1) & ";"
                        L2_PushL out, m, ocap, "begin"
                        L2_PushR out, m, ocap, pq1, 1, pqn1
                        L2_PushL out, m, ocap, "    Miss_comm:=1; // LH2 v22: расчёт дошёл до конца части"
                        L2_PushL out, m, ocap, "end;"
                        bufI1 = bufI1 & "    procedure " & pn1(nk1) & ";      // LH2 v22: часть " & nk1 & " из " & nP1 & " процедуры " & nm & vbCrLf
                    Next nk1
                    nin = nin + 1
                    If nin > icap2 Then icap2 = icap2 * 2: ReDim Preserve inIdx(1 To icap2): ReDim Preserve inTxt(1 To icap2)
                    inIdx(nin) = di0: inTxt(nin) = bufI1
                    If Not splitsD.Exists(nm) Then splitsD.Add nm, nP1 Else splitsD(nm) = nP1
                    nspl = nspl + 1
                    nChunks = nChunks + nP1
                End If
            End If
        End If
            pos = e + 1
NextPi:
        Next pi
    L2_PushR out, m, ocap, lines, pos, n
    ' --- единый блок полей в начало первой секции видимости класса ---
    If nfAll > 0 Then
        For k = 1 To impl - 1
            If InStr(LCase(lines(k)), "=class(") > 0 Then
                clsLn = k: Exit For
            End If
        Next k
        If clsLn > 0 Then
            For k = clsLn + 1 To impl - 1
                t = LCase(Trim(lines(k)))
                If t = "private" Or t = "public" Or t = "protected" Or t = "published" Then
                    secLn = k: Exit For
                End If
            Next k
        End If
        ' Ф10 v34: блок полей вставляется ПОСЛЕ цикла вставок (не через inIdx) - иначе
        ' fAll-вставка (первая из-за обратного обхода inIdx) сдвигает позиции bufI1-вставок,
        ' они попадают ВНУТРЬ блока полей и разрывают его: FPC "Fields cannot appear after a method".
        Dim faIdx As Long, faTxt As String
        faTxt = ""
        For k = 1 To nfAll
            faTxt = faTxt & "    " & Fall(k) & vbCrLf
        Next k
        If secLn > 0 Then
            faIdx = secLn
        Else
            ' FIX v36 (Ф16): БЕЗ вставки секции private - она делает ВСЕ последующие
            '      члены класса private, а private-члены родителя невидимы наследнику
            '      из другого юнита: в _0 не находились WorkLog1..WorkLogBU (FPC
            '      "Identifier not found", 9 ошибок). Воспроизведено и снято FPC
            '      3.2.2 на комплекте 13.11: снятие private в _01/_1/_2/main -
            '      все 8 юнитов компилируются 0 ошибок. Поля вставляются сразу
            '      после строки класса в видимости по умолчанию (как члены исходника).
            faIdx = clsLn
        End If
    End If
    ' --- вставка объявлений (после строк-якорей), в обратном порядке ---
    Dim ii As Long
    For ii = nin To 1 Step -1
        Dim nl() As String, nn As Long
        nn = 0
        Dim seg As Variant
        For Each seg In Split(inTxt(ii), vbCrLf)
            nn = nn + 1
            ReDim Preserve nl(1 To nn)
            nl(nn) = CStr(seg)
        Next seg
        If nn > 0 Then If Len(nl(nn)) = 0 Then nn = nn - 1
        Dim newOut() As String, mo As Long, co As Long, posn As Long
        co = m + nn + 8: ReDim newOut(1 To co): mo = 0
        For posn = 1 To inIdx(ii)
            L2_PushL newOut, mo, co, out(posn)
        Next posn
        For posn = 1 To nn
            L2_PushL newOut, mo, co, nl(posn)
        Next posn
        For posn = inIdx(ii) + 1 To m
            L2_PushL newOut, mo, co, out(posn)
        Next posn
        out = newOut: m = mo: cap = co
    Next ii
    ' Ф10 v34: единый блок полей - ПОСЛЕ всех вставок деклараций частей
    If nfAll > 0 And faIdx > 0 And Len(faTxt) > 0 Then
        Dim faLines() As String, faN As Long, faOut() As String, faM As Long, faCap As Long, faPos As Long
        faN = 0
        Dim faSeg As Variant
        For Each faSeg In Split(faTxt, vbCrLf)
            faN = faN + 1
            ReDim Preserve faLines(1 To faN)
            faLines(faN) = CStr(faSeg)
        Next faSeg
        If faN > 0 Then If Len(faLines(faN)) = 0 Then faN = faN - 1
        faCap = m + faN + 8: ReDim faOut(1 To faCap): faM = 0
        For faPos = 1 To faIdx
            L2_PushL faOut, faM, faCap, out(faPos)
        Next faPos
        For faPos = 1 To faN
            L2_PushL faOut, faM, faCap, faLines(faPos)
        Next faPos
        For faPos = faIdx + 1 To m
            L2_PushL faOut, faM, faCap, out(faPos)
        Next faPos
        out = faOut: m = faM: cap = faCap
    End If
    ' Ф12 v34: глобальные for-счётчики - блок "var ..." перед implementation юнита
    If ng > 0 Then
        Dim gTxt As String, gi2 As Long
        gTxt = ""
        For gi2 = 1 To ng
            gTxt = gTxt & "  " & gAll(gi2) & vbCrLf
        Next gi2
        Dim gImpl As Long
        gImpl = 0
        For gi2 = 1 To m
            If Trim(out(gi2)) = "implementation" Then
                gImpl = gi2: Exit For
            End If
        Next gi2
        If gImpl > 1 Then
            Dim gLines() As String, gN As Long, gOut() As String, gM As Long, gCap As Long, gPos As Long
            gN = 0
            ReDim gLines(1 To ng + 2)
            gN = gN + 1: gLines(gN) = "var"
            For gi2 = 1 To ng
                gN = gN + 1: gLines(gN) = "  " & gAll(gi2)
            Next gi2
            gCap = m + gN + 8: ReDim gOut(1 To gCap): gM = 0
            For gPos = 1 To gImpl - 1
                L2_PushL gOut, gM, gCap, out(gPos)
            Next gPos
            For gPos = 1 To gN
                L2_PushL gOut, gM, gCap, gLines(gPos)
            Next gPos
            For gPos = gImpl To m
                L2_PushL gOut, gM, gCap, out(gPos)
            Next gPos
            out = gOut: m = gM: cap = gCap
        End If
    End If
    fn = FreeFile
    Open fPath For Output As #fn
    For i = 1 To m
        Print #fn, out(i)
    Next i
    Close #fn
    L2_Split = nspl
End Function
'=====================================================================
' ДИСПЕТЧЕР main.Work: разбитые Work_N -> пары a/b (слоты 2N-1/2N),
' неразбитые -> слот 2N-1; WorkZero остаётся на своём слоте.
'=====================================================================
Private Function L2_Disp(ByVal fPath As String, ByRef splitsD As Object) As Long
    Dim fn As Integer, lines() As String, n As Long, cap As Long, ln As String
    Dim i As Long, k As Long, t As String
    Dim out() As String, m As Long, ocap As Long
    Dim nb As Long: nb = 0
    L2_Disp = 0
    If Len(Dir(fPath)) = 0 Then Exit Function
    fn = FreeFile
    Open fPath For Input As #fn
    n = 0: cap = 1000: ReDim lines(1 To cap)
    Do While Not EOF(fn)
        Line Input #fn, ln
        n = n + 1
        If n > cap Then cap = cap * 2: ReDim Preserve lines(1 To cap)
        lines(n) = ln
    Loop
    Close #fn
    ReDim Preserve lines(1 To n)
    If n < 50 Then Exit Function
    ' предскан блоков вызовов: [gate/begin/Miss_comm/call/6-reg/end;/Log]
    Dim bS() As Long, bC() As Long, be() As Long, bL() As Long
    ReDim bS(1 To 64): ReDim bC(1 To 64): ReDim be(1 To 64): ReDim bL(1 To 64)
    i = 1
    Do While i <= n
        Dim matched As Boolean: matched = False
        t = Trim(lines(i))
        ' v26: было Left(LCase(t), 6) - 'work_1' по 6 символам = 'work_1' и диспетчер не срабатывал НИКОГДА
        If (Left(LCase(t), 5) = "work_" Or LCase(t) = "workzero;") And Right(t, 1) = ";" And InStr(t, " ") = 0 Then
            If i >= 4 Then
                Dim g As String: g = Trim(lines(i - 3))
                If InStr(LCase(g), "miss_ind[") > 0 And Trim(lines(i - 2)) = "begin" And InStr(lines(i - 1), "Miss_comm") > 0 Then
                    Dim e0 As Long: e0 = i
                    Do While e0 < n
                        If Trim(lines(e0 + 1)) = "end;" Then Exit Do
                        e0 = e0 + 1
                    Loop
                    nb = nb + 1
                    If nb > UBound(bS) Then
                        ReDim Preserve bS(1 To nb * 2): ReDim Preserve bC(1 To nb * 2)
                        ReDim Preserve be(1 To nb * 2): ReDim Preserve bL(1 To nb * 2)
                    End If
                    bS(nb) = i - 3: bC(nb) = i: be(nb) = e0 + 1: bL(nb) = e0 + 2
                    i = e0 + 2
                    matched = True
                End If
            End If
        End If
        If Not matched Then i = i + 1
    Loop
    Dim starts As Object: Set starts = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    For i = 1 To nb: starts(CStr(bS(i))) = i: Next i
    ocap = n + 256: ReDim out(1 To ocap): m = 0
    i = 1
    Do While i <= n
        If starts.Exists(CStr(i)) Then
            Dim bi As Long: bi = starts(CStr(i))
            Dim ci As Long: ci = bC(bi)
            Dim nm As String: nm = Trim(Left(Trim(lines(ci)), Len(Trim(lines(ci))) - 1))
            Dim s As Long: s = 0
            For k = ci + 1 To ci + 6
                If k <= n Then
                    If InStr(lines(k), "Miss_proc[") > 0 Then
                        Dim sPos As Long: sPos = InStr(lines(k), "Miss_proc[")
                        Dim sEnd As Long: sEnd = InStr(sPos, lines(k), "]")
                        If sEnd > sPos Then s = CLng(Trim(Mid(lines(k), sPos + 11, sEnd - sPos - 11)))
                        Exit For
                    End If
                End If
            Next k
            If LCase(nm) = "workzero" Then
                L2_PushR out, m, ocap, lines, bS(bi), bL(bi)
            Else
                Dim nn As Long: nn = 0
                If LCase(Left(nm, 5)) = "work_" Then nn = CLng(Mid(nm, 6))
                If nn > 0 Then
                    Dim nPK As Long
                    nPK = 1
                    If splitsD.Exists(nm) Then nPK = CLng(splitsD(nm))
                    If nPK < 1 Then nPK = 1
                    Dim gate As String: gate = lines(bS(bi))
                    Dim lg As String
                    Dim pArr As Long, slK As Long, pnn As String
                    For pArr = 1 To nPK
                        If pArr = 1 Then
                            slK = 2 * nn - 1
                        ElseIf pArr = 2 Then
                            slK = 2 * nn
                        Else
                            If AB_SLOT > 99 Then slK = 99 Else slK = AB_SLOT: AB_SLOT = AB_SLOT + 1
                        End If
                        If nPK = 1 Then
                            pnn = nm
                        Else
                            pnn = nm & L2_Suf(pArr)
                        End If
                        If nPK > 1 Then L2_Disp = L2_Disp + 1
                        L2_PushL out, m, ocap, L2_SlotSub(gate, slK)
                        L2_PushL out, m, ocap, lines(bS(bi) + 1)
                        L2_PushL out, m, ocap, lines(bS(bi) + 2)
                        L2_PushL out, m, ocap, pnn & ";  // LH2 v22"
                        If nPK > 1 Then
                            L2_RegLinesDisp out, m, ocap, slK, pnn
                        Else
                            For k = ci + 1 To be(bi) - 1
                                L2_PushL out, m, ocap, L2_SlotAll(lines(k), slK)
                            Next k
                        End If
                        L2_PushL out, m, ocap, lines(be(bi))   ' v23: end; ВСЕГДА (раньше только при nPK>1 - терялся end; у нерезаных Work_N)
                        lg = lines(bL(bi))
                        If nPK > 1 Then lg = Replace(lg, " " & nm & " - SUCSESS ", " " & pnn & " - SUCSESS ")
                        L2_PushL out, m, ocap, lg
                    Next pArr
                Else
                    ' не Work_N (WorkZero и прочее) - без изменений
                    L2_PushR out, m, ocap, lines, bS(bi), bL(bi)
                End If
            End If
            i = bL(bi) + 1
        Else
            L2_PushL out, m, ocap, lines(i)
            i = i + 1
        End If
    Loop
    fn = FreeFile
    Open fPath For Output As #fn
    For i = 1 To m
        Print #fn, out(i)
    Next i
    Close #fn
End Function
'=====================================================================
' ДЕКЛАРАЦИИ (вставляются в декларационный юнит _00 после array-строки)
'=====================================================================
'=====================================================================
' ГЛАВНЫЙ КОНВЕРТОР v4: папка с юнитами -> *_LH3.txt
'=====================================================================
' --- LH3 v4: лог в файл (диагностика) - без модульных переменных ---
Private Sub L1_Log(ByVal s As String)
    Dim lf As Integer, lp As String
    On Error Resume Next
    lp = CurDir & "\LH3_convert_debug.log"
    lf = FreeFile
    Open lp For Append As #lf
    Print #lf, s
    Close #lf
End Sub

' Ф55: СТОП-инвариант приёмки. Меряет ТЕЛО процедуры dd-сканом от begin до парного end.
' Вызывается ПОСЛЕ последнего прохода резки по выходному файлу; накапливает нарушения.
' Решение о выпуске комплекта принимает основной процесс по LH3_StopFlag.
Private Sub LH3_StopCheck(ByVal pth As String, ByVal outn As String)
    Dim fPath As String, f1 As Integer
    Dim L() As String, n As Long, ln As String
    Dim i As Long, k As Long, hdr As Long, bi As Long, ei As Long
    Dim dd As Long, body As Long, t As String, nm As String
    fPath = pth & outn
    If Dir(fPath) = "" Then Exit Sub
    ReDim L(1 To 4096): n = 0
    f1 = FreeFile
    Open fPath For Input As #f1
    Do While Not EOF(f1)
        If n = UBound(L) Then ReDim Preserve L(1 To UBound(L) * 2)
        n = n + 1
        Line Input #f1, ln
        L(n) = ln
    Loop
    Close #f1
    If n = 0 Then Exit Sub
    ReDim Preserve L(1 To n)
    i = 1
    Do While i <= n
        t = Trim(L(i))
        If Left(t, 10) = "procedure " Then
            hdr = i
            Do While hdr <= n
                If Right(Trim(L(hdr)), 1) = ";" Then Exit Do
                hdr = hdr + 1
            Loop
            If hdr > n Then Exit Do
            nm = Trim(L(hdr))
            nm = Left(nm, Len(nm) - 1)
            If InStr(nm, "(") > 0 Then nm = Trim(Left(nm, InStr(nm, "(") - 1))
            bi = 0
            For k = hdr + 1 To n
                t = Trim(L(k))
                If t = "begin" Then
                    bi = k
                    Exit For
                End If
                If Left(t, 10) = "procedure " Or Left(t, 9) = "function " Then Exit For
                If t = "implementation" Or t = "end." Then Exit For
            Next k
            If bi > 0 Then
                dd = 0: ei = 0
                For k = bi + 1 To n
                    dd = dd + L2_Delta(L(k))
                    If dd < 0 Then
                        ei = k
                        Exit For
                    End If
                Next k
                If ei > bi Then
                    body = ei - bi - 1
                    If body > LH3_SAFE_LINE Then
                        LH3_StopCnt = LH3_StopCnt + 1
                        LH3_StopFlag = True
                        If LH3_StopCnt <= 10 Then
                            LH3_StopMsg = LH3_StopMsg & "  " & nm & " = " & CStr(body) & " строк  (" & outn & ")" & vbCrLf
                        End If
                    End If
                    i = ei
                End If
            End If
        End If
        i = i + 1
    Loop
End Sub
Sub LH3_Convert()
    LH3_StopFlag = False: LH3_StopMsg = "": LH3_StopCnt = 0   ' Ф55
    Dim pth As String, fn As String, outn As String, ln As String
    Dim s As String, nm As String, s2 As String, rest As String, gv As String
    Dim pl As String, hold As String
    Dim i As Long, p1 As Long, p2 As Long, p3 As Long, p4 As Long, n As Long
    Dim chi As Long, SZ As Long, csh As Boolean
    Dim f1 As Integer, f2 As Integer
    Dim curN As Long, hDone As Boolean, dDone As Boolean, noTail As Boolean
    Dim totB As Long, totH As Long
    Dim totC As Long
    Dim fileList() As String, nFiles As Long, fi As Long
    Dim PD As Object
    Dim dd As Long, ddA As Long
    Dim g48 As Long, g48lvl As Long, Fall As Boolean
    Dim nA1 As Long, nA2 As Long, nE27 As Long
    Dim EdgePairs As Object, Tprev As Object   ' v4: рёбра FR_C (N|S) и Tprev-имена
    Dim SW_T2 As Long, SW_T3 As Long, SW_A6 As Long, SW_F12 As Long, SW_B5 As Long, SW_A9 As Long, SW_PH As Long
    Dim SW_OIM As Long, SW_EGFIX As Long, SW_NORM As Long, SW_ROI As Long, SW_SHCW As Long, SW_SHCN As Long
    Dim SW_NOSWAP As Long, SW_ROSMO As Long, SW_STOR4 As Long, SW_B8 As Long, SW_B11 As Long, SW_B12 As Long
    Dim SW_B14 As Long, SW_B15 As Long, SW_FREPS As Long, SW_CNT As Long, SW_SYM As Long, SW_B2P As Long, SW_TST As Long, SW_SKL As Long
    Dim SW_PSC As Long, SW_KFP As Long, SW_RP As Long
    Dim buf8 As String                          ' v4: окно 8 строк (для F12) в проходе 1
    If Not L1_CanaryPowers() Then
        MsgBox "LH3 v26 (Ф36.2): канарейка Power<->Exp / критерий 2.7182 FAIL - конвертация не выпускается", vbCritical, "LH3 v26"
        Exit Sub
    End If
    Dim re As Object, mc As Object, ml As Object, mk As Variant
    Dim t0 As Single: Dim nPrn As Long: Dim mb As Single: Dim ixPrev As Long: Dim nStuck As Long: Dim dSB As Boolean: Dim dXX0 As Boolean, dZZ0 As Boolean, dISTO As Boolean
    Dim thr As Long, mainOut As String, tb As Long
    Dim totSp As Long, totCh As Long, totD As Long
    Dim nCImem As Long, nCIfail As Long
    Dim splitsD As Object: Set splitsD = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    thr = 0: mainOut = "": totSp = 0: totCh = 0: totD = 0: nDub = 0: totNoParam = 0: nCImem = 0: nCIfail = 0
    AB_SLOT = 53

    Set PD = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    ' --- инициализация флагов правил v4 (0 = выключить правило при отладке) ---
    SW_T2 = 0: SW_T3 = 1: SW_A6 = 1: SW_F12 = 1: SW_B5 = 1: SW_A9 = 1: SW_PH = 1
    SW_OIM = 1: SW_EGFIX = 1: SW_NORM = 1: SW_ROI = 1: SW_SHCW = 1: SW_SHCN = 1
    SW_NOSWAP = 1: SW_ROSMO = 1: SW_STOR4 = 1: SW_B8 = 1: SW_B11 = 1: SW_B12 = 1
    SW_B14 = 1: SW_B15 = 1: SW_FREPS = 1: SW_CNT = 1: SW_SYM = 1: SW_B2P = 0: SW_TST = 1: SW_SKL = 1
    SW_PSC = 1: SW_KFP = 1: SW_RP = 0
    Set EdgePairs = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Set Tprev = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")

    ' --- выбор папки: старт диалога с корня книги/документа с макросом ---
    Dim pth0 As String
    On Error Resume Next
    pth0 = Application.ActiveDocument.path           ' Word: активный документ
    If Err.Number <> 0 Then Err.Clear
    If Len(pth0) = 0 Then pth0 = Application.ActiveWorkbook.path   ' Excel: активная книга
    If Err.Number <> 0 Then Err.Clear
    If Len(pth0) = 0 Then pth0 = Application.VBE.ActiveVBProject.Filename   ' файл проекта, если доступ к VBE разрешён
    If Err.Number <> 0 Then Err.Clear
    On Error GoTo 0
    If InStrRev(pth0, ".") > InStrRev(pth0, "\") Then pth0 = Left(pth0, InStrRev(pth0, "\") - 1)   ' от файла проекта - до папки
    If Len(pth0) = 0 Then pth0 = CurDir
    Debug.Print "LH3 v25: старт; стартовая папка диалога: " & pth0: L1_Log "LH3 v24: старт " & Now: L1_Log "LH3 v24: старт " & Now
    ' Ф44 (v31): U4-интеграция БЕЗ compile-зависимости от модуля U4_UI (универсальный модуль):
    '      путь запрашивается через Application.Run("U4_GetPTH") - в Utilite_4 возвращает U4_PTH
    '      (кнопка кладёт туда ячейку C4 листа U4, после прогона U4_UI сбрасывает); в основном
    '      генераторе U4_UI нет -> Err 1004, путь остаётся пустым -> открывается свой диалог.
    '      Один и тот же модуль компилируется и в утилите, и в генераторе (при обеих открытых
    '      книгах Run берёт первую найденную U4_GetPTH - путь, как правило, пуст -> свой диалог).
    pth = ""
    On Error Resume Next
    pth = Application.Run("U4_GetPTH")   ' U4_UI: Public U4_GetPTH -> U4_PTH
    On Error GoTo 0
    If Len(pth) > 0 Then
        If Right(pth, 1) <> "\" Then pth = pth & "\" 
    Else
        With Application.FileDialog(msoFileDialogFolderPicker)
            .Title = "LH3 v4: папка с юнитами проекта 19.785 (м2-комплект)"
            If Len(pth0) > 0 Then
                If Right(pth0, 1) <> "\" Then pth0 = pth0 & "\"
                .InitialFileName = pth0
            End If
            If .Show <> -1 Then Exit Sub
            pth = .SelectedItems(1)
            If Right(pth, 1) <> "\" Then pth = pth & "\"
        End With
    End If

    ' --- проход 1: список, защита, chi, SZ, пресеты, рёбра FR_C, Tprev-имена ---
    ' --- форма прогресса (как в LH2 v34: ufProgress + PrPro из книги владельца) ---
    ufProgress.Show (0)
    PrPro 0, "LH3: подготовка..."
    Debug.Print "LH3 v5: проход 1 (скан файлов)..."
    chi = 0: csh = False: SZ = 0: nFiles = 0
    ReDim fileList(1 To 200)
    fn = Dir(pth & "*.txt")
    Do While fn <> ""
        If fn Like "*_LH3.txt" Then
            MsgBox "LH3: в папке уже есть файлы _LH3 (" & fn & ") - детектор уже применён к проекту." & vbCrLf & _
                   "Удалите старые *_LH3-файлы и запустите заново. Конверсия не выполнялась.", vbExclamation, "LH3 v4"
            On Error Resume Next: Unload ufProgress: On Error GoTo 0   ' LH3 v4: форма не должна висеть
            Exit Sub
        End If
        nFiles = nFiles + 1
        If nFiles > UBound(fileList) Then ReDim Preserve fileList(1 To UBound(fileList) + 200)
        fileList(nFiles) = fn
        fn = Dir
    Loop

    For fi = 1 To nFiles
        fn = fileList(fi)
        PrPro fi / nFiles * 10, "Скан: " & fn
        buf8 = ""
