        Debug.Print "    скан: " & fn
        f1 = FreeFile: Open pth & fn For Input As #f1
        Do While Not EOF(f1)
            Line Input #f1, ln
            If InStr(ln, "LH3 v") > 0 Or InStr(ln, "NaN_t[") > 0 Or InStr(ln, "INOFF") > 0 Then
                Close #f1
                MsgBox "LH3: детектор уже стоит в проекте (маркер найден в " & fn & ").", vbExclamation, "LH3 v4"
                On Error Resume Next: Unload ufProgress: On Error GoTo 0   ' LH3 v4: форма не должна висеть
                Exit Sub
            End If
            If InStr(ln, "for (int") > 0 Or InStr(ln, "Math.") > 0 Then csh = True
            p1 = InStr(ln, "Давление и расход")
            If p1 > 0 Then
                p2 = InStr(1, ln, "#")
                If p2 > 0 And p2 < p1 Then
                    n = Val(Trim(Mid(ln, p2 + 1, p1 - p2 - 1)))
                    If n > chi Then chi = n
                End If
            End If
            If InStr(ln, "ISTO_p") > 0 And InStr(ln, "array") > 0 Then
                p1 = InStr(ln, "array[1..")
                If p1 > 0 And SZ = 0 Then SZ = Val(Trim(Mid(ln, p1 + 9)))
            End If
            ' пресеты _0i
            If fn Like "*_0i.txt" Then
                s2 = Trim(ln)
                p1 = InStr(s2, ":=")
                If p1 > 1 And InStr(s2, "kerr") > 0 And Left(s2, 2) <> "//" Then
                    nm = Trim(Left(s2, p1 - 1))
                    p2 = InStr(p1 + 2, s2, ";")
                    If p2 > 0 And L1_Simple(nm) Then
                        s2 = Trim(Mid(s2, p1 + 2, p2 - p1 - 2))
                        If Len(s2) > 0 And InStr(s2, "'") = 0 And InStr(s2, Chr(34)) = 0 Then
                            If Not PD.Exists(nm) Then PD.Add nm, s2
                        End If
                    End If
                End If
            End If
            ' v4 С1б-9: рёбра FR_C -> словарь пар N|S (для Т-2 и С0-5)
            s2 = Trim(ln)
            If Left(s2, 6) = "FR_C(" And Right(s2, 2) = ");" Then
                p1 = InStr(s2, "("): p2 = InStr(p1, s2, ",")
                If p2 > p1 Then
                    nm = Trim(Mid(s2, p1 + 1, p2 - p1 - 1))
                    p1 = InStr(p2 + 1, s2, ",")
                    If p1 > 0 Then
                        p2 = InStr(p1 + 1, s2, ",")
                        If p2 > p1 Then
                            nm = nm & "|" & Trim(Mid(s2, p1 + 1, p2 - p1 - 1))
                            If Not EdgePairs.Exists(nm) Then EdgePairs.Add nm, 1
                        End If
                    End If
                End If
            End If
            ' v4 F12: Tprev-имена: из XX0[11]-строки (ближайшее Tc*-имя в окне 8)
            If InStr(Trim(ln), "aa2:=(0.5*aa2+") > 0 Then
                On Error Resume Next
                Err.Clear
                Set re = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
                re.Pattern = "(Tc[A-Za-z0-9_]*)"
                Dim tmptc As String
                If Not re Is Nothing Then
                    If re.Test(buf8) Then
                        tmptc = re.Execute(buf8)(0).SubMatches(0)
                        If Not Tprev.Exists(tmptc) Then Tprev.Add tmptc, 1
                    End If
                End If
                On Error GoTo 0
                If Err.Number <> 0 Then L1_Log "Tc-поиск ERROR err=" & CStr(Err.Number) & " buf8=" & Left(buf8, 80)   ' LH3 v19 Ф30
                Err.Clear
                Set re = Nothing
            End If
            buf8 = buf8 & "|" & Trim(ln)
            If Len(buf8) > 600 Then buf8 = Mid(buf8, InStr(Mid(buf8, 2), "|") + 1)
        Loop
        Close #f1
    Next fi
    csh1 = csh

    If chi = 0 Then
        MsgBox "LH3: блоки 'Давление и расход' не найдены - это сборка 19.785?", vbExclamation, "LH3 v4"
        On Error Resume Next: Unload ufProgress: On Error GoTo 0   ' LH3 v4: форма не должна висеть
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If SZ = 0 Then SZ = chi + 1
    Debug.Print "LH3 v5: проход 1 готов: узлов chi=" & CStr(chi) & ", SZ=" & CStr(SZ) & ", файлов=" & CStr(nFiles) & ", рёбер FR_C=" & CStr(EdgePairs.Count) & ", Tprev=" & CStr(Tprev.Count)
    On Error Resume Next
    dSB = Application.DisplayStatusBar
    Application.DisplayStatusBar = True   ' строка состояния может быть скрыта - прогресс не виден без этого
    On Error GoTo 0

    ' --- проход 2: конвертация каждого файла (массив строк: нужен lookahead) ---
    Dim lines() As String, nLines As Long, ix As Long, kx As Long
    Dim t2st As Long, t2frcline As String, t2dpline As String, t2N As Long, t2S As Long, t2Pown As String
    Dim t3st As Long, t3line As String
    Dim a6st As Long, a6line As String
    Dim f12st As Long, f12line As String
    Dim b12st As Long, b12line As String
    Dim b11st As Long
    Dim spActive As Boolean, spTxt As String, spL1 As Long
    Dim curLoopO As Boolean, curSpFrom As Long, curSpTo As Boolean
    Dim a11done As Boolean
    Dim b2done As Boolean, kfffdone As Boolean
    Dim nT2 As Long, nT3 As Long, nA6 As Long, nF12 As Long, nF12m As Long
    Dim nB14 As Long, nB11 As Long, nB12 As Long, nB5 As Long, nA9 As Long, nPH As Long
    Dim nF11 As Long, nC24 As Long, nA11 As Long, nPSC As Long, nOIM As Long
    Dim nNORM As Long, nEGFIX As Long, nROI As Long, nSHCW As Long, nSHCN As Long
    Dim nNOSWAP As Long, nROSMO As Long, nSTOR4 As Long, nFREPS As Long, nB15 As Long
    Dim nB15b As Long, nB8M As Long, nB2P As Long, nKFP As Long, nPW As Long, nTST As Long, nSKL As Long, nCI As Long
    Dim curTubeP As String, curTubeN As String, prevS As String, inLoop As Boolean
    curTubeP = "": curTubeN = "": prevS = "": inLoop = False
    Dim nEdges As Long
    Dim curLoopLvl As Long, oiProc As Boolean
    Dim tpv As Variant, tprevList As String
    For Each tpv In Tprev.keys
        tprevList = tprevList & IIf(Len(tprevList) > 0, "|", "") & tpv
    Next tpv
    nEdges = EdgePairs.Count

    For fi = 1 To nFiles
        fn = fileList(fi)
        If fn Like "*_LH3.txt" Then GoTo NextFile
        outn = Left(fn, Len(fn) - 4) & "_LH3.txt"
        noTail = (fn Like "*_0i.txt") Or (fn Like "*_000.txt")
        curN = 0: hDone = False: dDone = False: dd = 0: ddA = 0: totC = 0: dXX0 = False: dZZ0 = False: dISTO = False
        g48 = 0: g48lvl = 0: Fall = False: hold = ""
        t2st = 0: t3st = 0: a6st = 0: f12st = 0: b12st = 0: b11st = 0
        spActive = False: spTxt = "": spL1 = 0
        curLoopO = False: curSpFrom = 0: curSpTo = False: a11done = False
        b2done = False: kfffdone = False
        ' --- читать весь файл в массив (для lookahead) ---
        nLines = 0: ReDim lines(1 To 200000)
        f1 = FreeFile: Open pth & fn For Input As #f1
        Do While Not EOF(f1)
            Line Input #f1, ln
            nLines = nLines + 1
            If nLines > UBound(lines) Then ReDim Preserve lines(1 To UBound(lines) + 200000)
            lines(nLines) = ln
        Loop
        Close #f1
        PrPro 10 + (fi - 1) / nFiles * 85, "Конверсия: " & fn
    Debug.Print "LH3 v4: конверсия " & fn & " (" & CStr(nLines) & " строк) -> " & outn: L1_Log "ФАЙЛ " & fn & " строк " & CStr(nLines)
        t0 = Timer: nPrn = 0: ixPrev = 0: nStuck = 0: dSB = True
        f2 = FreeFile: Open pth & outn For Output As #f2
        ix = 1
        Do While ix <= nLines
            If Seek(f2) > 314572800 Then
                Debug.Print "!!! ОХРАНА: выход превысил 300 МБ при вход-строке " & CStr(ix) & "/" & CStr(nLines)
                Application.StatusBar = False: Application.DisplayStatusBar = dSB: On Error Resume Next: Unload ufProgress: On Error GoTo 0
                MsgBox "LH3 v4 ОХРАНА: выход превысил 300 МБ." & vbCrLf & "Строка входа: " & CStr(ix) & " из " & CStr(nLines) & vbCrLf & "Размер выхода: " & Format(Seek(f2) / 1048576, "0.0") & " МБ" & vbCrLf & "Файл: " & outn, vbCritical, "LH3 v4 охрана"
                Exit Do
            End If
            If ix = ixPrev And nStuck > 2 Then
                Debug.Print "!!! СТОП: ix не изменился трижды на строке: [" & Left(s, 90) & "]"
                Application.StatusBar = False: Application.DisplayStatusBar = dSB: On Error Resume Next: Unload ufProgress: On Error GoTo 0
                L1_Log "СТОП ix=" & CStr(ix) & " строка: " & Left(s, 120)
                MsgBox "LH3 v4 СТОП: строка не продвинулась трижды." & vbCrLf & "Строка входа (" & CStr(ix) & "): " & Left(s, 90) & vbCrLf & "Файл: " & fn, vbCritical, "LH3 v4 стоп"
                Exit Do
            End If
            ixPrev = ix
            If (ix Mod 10) = 1 Then
                mb = Seek(f2) / 1048576
                Application.StatusBar = "LH3 v4: " & fn & " -> " & outn & ": строка " & CStr(ix) & " / " & CStr(nLines) & " (" & Format(mb, "0.0") & " МБ)"
                Debug.Print "    ...строка " & CStr(ix) & "/" & CStr(nLines) & ", выход " & Format(mb, "0.0") & " МБ"
                DoEvents
            End If
            If (ix Mod 500) = 1 Then
                PrPro 10 + (fi - 1 + ix / nLines) / nFiles * 85, "Конверсия: " & fn & " (" & CStr(ix) & "/" & CStr(nLines) & ")"
            End If
            If (ix Mod 10) = 0 And ix <= 120 Then L1_Log "OUT ix=" & CStr(ix) & ": [" & Left(pl, 70) & "]"
            If (ix Mod 5000) = 1 Then L1_Log "строка " & CStr(ix) & "/" & CStr(nLines) & ", выход " & Format(Seek(f2) / 1048576, "0.0") & " МБ"
            prevS = s: ln = lines(ix): s = Trim(ln): pl = ln   ' LH3 v4 FIX: pl - исходная строка
            ' LH3 v16 (Ф19-б): дубли деклараций - перехват В НАЧАЛЕ итерации (на любом пути печати);
            ' флаги ставятся ТОЛЬКО в момент печати (вставка Decls4 / путь Out4) - иначе Out4-страж
            ' гасил первую декларацию ZZ0/ISTO_p
            If (Left(s, 4) = "XX0:") Or (Left(s, 4) = "ZZ0:") Or (Left(s, 7) = "ISTO_p:") Then
                If (Left(s, 4) = "XX0:" And dXX0) Or (Left(s, 4) = "ZZ0:" And dZZ0) Or (Left(s, 7) = "ISTO_p:" And dISTO) Then
                    L1_Log "DUB-SKIP @" & CStr(ix) & ": " & Left(s, 100)
                    nDub = nDub + 1
                    ix = ix + 1: GoTo IXDONE
                End If
            End If
            If L4_M(s, "^aa2:=([a-zA-Z_0-9]+)") And (Not L4_M(s, "^aa2:=aa2")) Then
                Dim tn As String: tn = L4_SM(s, "^aa2:=([a-zA-Z_0-9]+)", 0)
                If tn <> "aa2" Then curTubeP = tn
            End If
            If oiProc And s = "begin" Then
                Print #f2, s
                Print #f2, "  LH3_cnt_OI:=LH3_cnt_OI+1;  kerr:=-990; // LH3 v4 С0-1"
                oiProc = False: ix = ix + 1
            ElseIf SW_RP = 1 And LCase(s) = "implementation" Then
                Print #f2, ln: Print #f2, "{$R+} // LH3 v5 С0-5": ix = ix + 1
            ElseIf Len(s) = 0 Then
                Print #f2, ln: ix = ix + 1
            ElseIf noTail Then
                Print #f2, ln: ix = ix + 1
            Else
                ' v4 A11: сбор span (от V_r[1]:=1 до XX0[11]: внутри ОИ-петли)
                If curLoopO And Not spActive And s = "V_r[1]:=1;" Then
                    spActive = True: spTxt = ""
                ElseIf curLoopO And spActive And Not L4_M(s, "^XX0\[11\]:=") Then
                    spTxt = spTxt & "|" & s
                End If
                ddA = dd + L1_Delta(ln)
                ' A2-гейт (v3): ожидание begin после ttyr=48
                If g48 = 1 Then
                    If Len(s) = 0 Or Left(s, 2) = "//" Then
                        Print #f2, ln: dd = ddA: ix = ix + 1: GoTo IXDONE   ' LH3 v4 FIX
                    ElseIf s = "begin" Then
                        g48 = 2: g48lvl = ddA: dd = ddA: ix = ix + 1: GoTo IXDONE   ' LH3 v4 FIX
                    Else
                        Print #f2, hold: hold = "": g48 = 0
                        Print #f2, ln: dd = ddA: ix = ix + 1: GoTo IXDONE   ' LH3 v4 FIX
                    End If
                ElseIf g48 = 2 And ddA = g48lvl - 1 And Left(s, 3) = "end" Then
                    g48 = 0: nA2 = nA2 + 1: dd = ddA: ix = ix + 1
                Else
                    ' === v4: Power-фиксы (С0-2/С1а-1/С1а-2) ===
                    If InStr(pl, "Power(") > 0 Then
                        pl = L1_FixPowers(pl)
                        If InStr(pl, "Sqrt(") > 0 Or InStr(pl, ")*(") > 0 Or InStr(pl, "Exp(") > 0 Then nPW = nPW + 1
                    End If
                    ' === v4 Т-3: гашение U_ri при aa3<=0 ===
                    If L4_M(s, "^U_ri\[(\d+),(\d+)\]:=U_ri\[\d+,\d+\]/aa3;(.*)$") Then
                        nm = "U_ri[" & L4_SM(s, "^U_ri\[(\d+),(\d+)\]", 0) & "," & L4_SM(s, "^U_ri\[\d+,(\d+)\]", 0) & "]"
                        rest = L4_SM(s, "^U_ri\[\d+,\d+\]:=U_ri\[\d+,\d+\]/aa3;(.*)$", 0)
                        If rest Like "  kerr:= *" Then rest = Mid(rest, InStr(rest, "kerr:"))
                        If InStr(rest, "//") > 0 Then rest = Trim(Left(rest, InStr(rest, "//") - 1))
                        Print #f2, "if (aa3<=0) then " & nm & ":=0 else if (" & nm & ">0.000025) then " & s & " " & rest & " // LH3 v4 Т-3"
                        nT3 = nT3 + 1: dd = ddA: ix = ix + 1: GoTo IXDONE   ' LH3 v4 FIX
                    ElseIf L4_M(s, "^XX0\[14\]:=XX0\[12\]/\(XX0\[13\]\+e27\);") Then
                        ' === v4 A6: кламп подушки ===
                        Print #f2, "if (XX0[13]<0.005*10*(e27+10*ISTO_v[" & curN & "])) then"
                        Print #f2, "XX0[13]:=0.005*10*(e27+10*ISTO_v[" & curN & "]); LH3_cnt_clamp:=LH3_cnt_clamp+1;  kerr:= -961; // LH3 v4 A6: кламп Vmin"
                        Print #f2, "XX0[14]:=XX0[12]/XX0[13]; LH3_cnt_clamp:=LH3_cnt_clamp+1;  kerr:= -962; // LH3 v4 A6: без взрыва"
                        nA6 = nA6 + 1: dd = ddA: ix = ix + 1: GoTo IXDONE   ' LH3 v4 FIX
                    ElseIf L4_M(s, "^aa2:=\(0\.5\*aa2\+\(aa2\+1\)\*XX0\[14\]\*XX0\[15\]-1\)/1\.5;") Then
                        ' === v4 F12: dT/T ===
                        nm = ""
                        For kx = ix - 1 To IIf(ix - 8 > 1, ix - 8, 1) Step -1
                            If L4_M(Trim(lines(kx)), "Tc[A-Za-z0-9_]*") Then nm = L4_SM(Trim(lines(kx)), "(Tc[A-Za-z0-9_]*)", 0): Exit For
                        Next kx
                        If Len(nm) > 0 Then
                            Print #f2, "aa2:=(0.5*aa2+((aa2+1)*XX0[14]*XX0[15]*(273.15+" & nm & ")/(273.15+LH3_Tprev_" & Mid(nm, 3) & ")-1))/1.5;  kerr:= -963; // LH3 v4 F12: dT/T"
                            nF12 = nF12 + 1
                        Else
                            Print #f2, pl
                            nF12m = nF12m + 1
                        End If
                        dd = ddA: ix = ix + 1
                    ElseIf L4_M(s, "^XX0\[3\]:=1-(\w+);") Then
                        ' === v4 B14: клампы долей ===
                        Print #f2, s
                        Print #f2, "if (XX0[3]>1) then XX0[3]:=1; LH3_cnt_clamp:=LH3_cnt_clamp+1;  kerr:= -964; // LH3 v4 B14"
                        Print #f2, "if (XX0[3]<0.01) then XX0[3]:=0.01; LH3_cnt_clamp:=LH3_cnt_clamp+1;  kerr:= -964; // LH3 v4 B14"
                        nB14 = nB14 + 1: dd = ddA: ix = ix + 1: GoTo IXDONE   ' LH3 v4 FIX
                    ElseIf Left(s, 8) = "dL:=0.55" And L4_M(s, "^dL:=0\.55;") Then
                        ' === v4 B11: нижний кламп ===
                                        rest = Trim(Mid(s, InStr(s, ";") + 1))
                                        If SW_SKL = 1 Then
                                            Print #f2, "dL:=0.55*SK_L; " & rest & " // LH3 v7 С1б-4 B10: кламп dL с SK_L"
                                        Else
                                            Print #f2, pl
                                        End If
                        Print #f2, "if (dL<-0.55) then"
                                        If SW_SKL = 1 Then
                                            Print #f2, "dL:=-0.55*SK_L;  kerr:= -965; // LH3 v7 С1б-4 B11: нижний кламп с SK_L"
                                        Else
                                            Print #f2, "dL:=-0.55;  kerr:= -965; // LH3 v4 B11: нижний кламп"
                                        End If
                                        nB11 = nB11 + 1: nSKL = nSKL + 1: dd = ddA: ix = ix + 1: GoTo IXDONE   ' LH3 v7 FIX
                    ElseIf s = "if (dL=3) then" Then
                        ' === v4 B12: строка if не печатается (гейт переносится) ===
                        dd = ddA: ix = ix + 1
                    ElseIf L4_M(s, "^(\w+):=\1\+0\.01\*XX0\[8\];") Then
                        ' === v4 B12: гейт клапана роста ===
                        nm = L4_SM(s, "^(\w+):=", 0)
                        rest = L4_SM(s, "0\.01\*XX0\[8\];(.*)$", 0)
                        If InStr(rest, "//") > 0 Then rest = Trim(Left(rest, InStr(rest, "//") - 1))
                        Print #f2, "if (dL=3) and ((LIM_P_max<=0) or (" & nm & "+0.01*XX0[8]<LIM_P_max)) then"
                        Print #f2, nm & ":=" & nm & "+0.01*XX0[8]; " & rest & " // LH3 v4 B12: гейт клапана роста"
                        nB12 = nB12 + 1: dd = ddA: ix = ix + 1: GoTo IXDONE   ' LH3 v4 FIX
                    ElseIf L4_M(s, "^W_out:=W_out\*(\w+)/(S_out\+e27);") Then
                        ' === v4 B5: третья фаза в лимитерах ===
                        Print #f2, s
                        Print #f2, "G_out:=G_out*" & L4_SM(s, "^W_out:=W_out\*(\w+)", 0) & "/(S_out+e27);  kerr:= -966; // LH3 v4 B5: третья фаза"
                        nB5 = nB5 + 1: dd = ddA: ix = ix + 1: GoTo IXDONE   ' LH3 v4 FIX
                    ElseIf L4_M(s, "^W_in:=W_in\*(\w+)/(S_in\+e27);") Then
                        Print #f2, s
                        Print #f2, "G_in:=G_in*" & L4_SM(s, "^W_in:=W_in\*(\w+)", 0) & "/(S_in+e27);  kerr:= -966; // LH3 v4 B5: третья фаза"
                        nB5 = nB5 + 1: dd = ddA: ix = ix + 1: GoTo IXDONE   ' LH3 v4 FIX
                    ElseIf L4_M(s, "^L_out:=L_out\+W_out-\(3600\*MAIN_c\*[^\)]+\+W_in\);") Then
                        ' === v4 A9: перекладка вода->УВ убрана ===
                        Print #f2, "// LH3 v4 A9: перекладка вода->УВ убрана"
                        nA9 = nA9 + 1: dd = ddA: ix = ix + 1: GoTo IXDONE   ' LH3 v4 FIX
                    ElseIf L4_M(s, "^G_out:=G_out\+L_out-\(3600\*MAIN_c\*[^\)]+\+L_in\);") Then
                        Print #f2, "// LH3 v4 A9: перекладка УВ->газ убрана"
                        nA9 = nA9 + 1: dd = ddA: ix = ix + 1: GoTo IXDONE   ' LH3 v4 FIX
                    ElseIf L4_M(s, "^L_out:=L_out\+G_out-\(3600\*MAIN_c\*[^\)]+\+G_in\);") Then
                        Print #f2, "// LH3 v4 A9: перекладка газ->УВ убрана"
                        nA9 = nA9 + 1: dd = ddA: ix = ix + 1: GoTo IXDONE   ' LH3 v4 FIX

                                        Print #f2, "  if LH3_oK < LH3_oKn then LH3_oKn := LH3_oK;"

                                        Print #f2, "else if (LH3_oKn >= 1) then eMM := 1        // LH3 v4: F(1)>=0 - перегретый пар"

                                        Print #f2, "      LH3_oFL := LH3_oFL + z[j] * (LH3_oK - 1) / LH3_oDen;"
                                        Print #f2, "    end;"
                                        Print #f2, "    if (Abs(LH3_oFL) < 1e-12) then break;"

                                        Print #f2, "  end;"
                                        Print #f2, "  eMM := (LH3_oLo + LH3_oHi) / 2;"

                                        Print #f2, "if eMM < 1-0.9999999  then eMM := 1-0.99999999;"
                                        Print #f2, "eM := eMM; eG:= eMM; kerr:=-4;"
                                        nOIM = nOIM + 1: dd = ddA: ix = ix + 33: GoTo IXDONE   ' LH3 v4 FIX
                                    ' OI_NORMFIX: нормировка без доборов
                                    ElseIf s = "if (sx<1) then x[6]:=x[6]+(1-sx);" And L4_M(Trim(lines(ix + 3)), "^end; kerr:=-5;$") Then
                                        Print #f2, "// LH3 v4 OI_NORMFIX: доборы убраны, нормировка на текущую сумму"

            If ix = ixPrev Then

        totD = L2_Disp(mainOut, splitsD)
        LH3_StopCheck pth, mainOut   ' Ф62
        If LH3_StopFlag Then   ' Ф62: СТОП-инвариант и ПОСЛЕ диспетчера (main.Work растёт от блоков L2_Disp)
            MsgBox "СТОП: тело процедуры выше порога " & CStr(LH3_SAFE_LINE) & " строк (после диспетчера)." & vbCrLf & _
               "Нарушений: " & CStr(LH3_StopCnt) & vbCrLf & LH3_StopMsg & _
               "Комплект НЕ выпущен.", vbCritical, "LH3 v37 СТОП (после диспетчера)"
            Application.StatusBar = False
            On Error Resume Next: Unload ufProgress: On Error GoTo 0
            Exit Sub
        End If
    End If
Application.StatusBar = False: Application.DisplayStatusBar = dSB
    PrPro 100, "Готово"
    On Error Resume Next: Unload ufProgress: On Error GoTo 0   ' LH3 v4: закрыть форму прогресса
    L1_Log "ГОТОВО: застрявших строк (принудительный инкремент): " & CStr(nStuck)
    Debug.Print "LH3 v4: готово."
    MsgBox "LH3 v15: готово (стражи v21 + ПОСТ-ПРАВКИ A1/A2 + весь перечень м2.5 + Ф19/Ф20)." & vbCrLf & _
           "Узлов (chi) = " & CStr(chi) & ", размер массива = " & CStr(SZ) & _
           IIf(csh1, ", стиль C#", ", стиль pas") & vbCrLf & _
           "A1 (окно/клампа): " & CStr(nA1) & ", A2 (ttyr=48): " & CStr(nA2) & ", +e27 в K_v: " & CStr(nE27) & vbCrLf & _
           "Стражей входа: " & CStr(totB) & ", хвостов: " & CStr(totH) & ", Ф4-бред-декл: " & CStr(totC) & vbCrLf & "Застрявших (принудительно): " & CStr(nStuck) & vbCrLf & _
           "Т-2/Т-1 (канон ребра): " & CStr(nT2) & ", Т-3 (гашение U_ri): " & CStr(nT3) & vbCrLf & _
           "A6 (кламп подушки): " & CStr(nA6) & ", F12 (dT/T): " & CStr(nF12) & " (пропусков: " & CStr(nF12m) & ")" & vbCrLf & _
           "B14: " & CStr(nB14) & ", B11: " & CStr(nB11) & ", B12: " & CStr(nB12) & ", B5: " & CStr(nB5) & ", A9 (перекладки убраны): " & CStr(nA9) & vbCrLf & _
           "F11 (якоробиан): " & CStr(nF11) & ", 24К-счётчики: " & CStr(nC24) & ", A11 (пересчёт фаз): " & CStr(nA11) & vbCrLf & _
           "B8: " & CStr(nB8M) & ", B15: " & CStr(nB15) & "/" & CStr(nB15b) & ", B2P: " & CStr(nB2P) & ", KFP: " & CStr(nKFP) & ", Power-фиксы: " & CStr(nPW) & ", PH: " & CStr(nPH) & ", TST: " & CStr(nTST) & ", SKL: " & CStr(nSKL) & ", CI: " & CStr(nCI) & vbCrLf & _
           "_0-блоки: Psat=" & CStr(nPSC) & " OI_MODE=" & CStr(nOIM) & " NORM=" & CStr(nNORM) & " EGFIX=" & CStr(nEGFIX) & " ROI=" & CStr(nROI) & vbCrLf & _
           "SHCW=" & CStr(nSHCW) & " SHCN=" & CStr(nSHCN) & " NOSWAP=" & CStr(nNOSWAP) & " ROSMO=" & CStr(nROSMO) & " STOR4=" & CStr(nSTOR4) & " FREPS=" & CStr(nFREPS) & vbCrLf & _
           "Рёбер FR_C (Т-2/С0-5): " & CStr(nEdges) & ", Tprev-полей: " & CStr(Tprev.Count) & vbCrLf & _
           "Разрезание (Ф20/Ф32): процедур разбито " & CStr(totSp) & ", частей " & CStr(totCh) & ", блоков в диспетчере " & CStr(totD) & ", макс. часть " & CStr(gMaxPart) & " строк (лимит ~9000), в-блочных кусков (Ф32а) " & CStr(gMonoPieces) & ", блоков (Ф32) " & CStr(gMonoBlocks) & "." & vbCrLf & _
           "Процедур с параметрами пропущено (не режем, Ф14): " & CStr(totNoParam) & ". Дублей деклараций пропущено (Ф19): " & CStr(nDub) & vbCrLf & _
           "Членов якобиана С4-1 восстановлено (Ф21): " & CStr(nCImem) & ", пропущено: " & CStr(nCIfail) & " (0 = обе группы распознаны)." & vbCrLf & _
                      "С1а-1 v26 (Ф36.1): Power оставлено=" & CStr(L1_cnt_POWFIX) & ", Exp замен=" & CStr(L1_cnt_EXP2) & vbCrLf & _
                      "Файлы: *_LH3.txt (исходные м2 не тронуты). База: пайплайн tools/lh3_v4_pipeline.py (FPC 3.2.2 проверен).", vbInformation, "LH3 v37"
    PrPro 100, "Готово!"
    On Error Resume Next: Unload ufProgress: On Error GoTo 0   ' Ф43 v30: форма не должна висеть
End Sub

' --- регулярная замена (VBScript.RegExp, v4) ---
Private Function L4_Re(ByVal s As String, ByVal pat As String, ByVal rep As String, ByVal nCnt As Long) As String
    Static re As Object, mc As Object
    L4_Re = s
    If re Is Nothing Then Set re = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")   ' v4: объект кэшируется - иначе CreateObject на каждый вызов
    If re Is Nothing Then
        L1_Log "L4_Re ERROR: объект RegExp недоступен pat=" & Left(pat, 120)   ' LH3 v19 Ф30
        Exit Function
    End If
    On Error Resume Next
    Err.Clear
    re.Global = True: re.MultiLine = True: re.IgnoreCase = False
    re.Pattern = pat
    Set mc = re.Execute(s)
    On Error GoTo 0
    If Err.Number <> 0 Or mc Is Nothing Then
        ' LH3 v19 Ф30: паттерн не принят - лог и возврат исходной строки
        L1_Log "L4_Re ERROR err=" & CStr(Err.Number) & " " & Err.Description & " pat=" & Left(pat, 120)
        Err.Clear
        Exit Function
    End If
    nCnt = mc.Count
    If nCnt > 0 Then L4_Re = re.Replace(s, rep) Else L4_Re = s
End Function

' --- проверка совпадения и подстрока по группе ---
Private Function L4_M(ByVal s As String, ByVal pat As String) As Boolean
    Static re As Object
    L4_M = False
    If re Is Nothing Then Set re = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")   ' v4: объект кэшируется
    If re Is Nothing Then
        L1_Log "L4_M ERROR: объект RegExp недоступен pat=" & Left(pat, 120)   ' LH3 v19 Ф30
        Exit Function
    End If
    On Error Resume Next
    Err.Clear
    re.Global = False: re.IgnoreCase = False
    re.Pattern = pat
    Dim bM As Boolean
    bM = re.Test(s)
    On Error GoTo 0
    If Err.Number <> 0 Then
        ' LH3 v19 Ф30: паттерн не принят - лог и деградация к False
        L1_Log "L4_M ERROR err=" & CStr(Err.Number) & " " & Err.Description & " pat=" & Left(pat, 120)
        Err.Clear
        Exit Function
    End If
    L4_M = bM
End Function
Private Function L4_SM(ByVal s As String, ByVal pat As String, ByVal idx As Long) As String
    Static re As Object, mc As Object
    If re Is Nothing Then Set re = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")   ' v4: объект кэшируется
    re.Global = False: re.IgnoreCase = False
    On Error Resume Next
    Err.Clear
    re.Pattern = pat
    Set mc = re.Execute(s)
    On Error GoTo 0
    If Err.Number <> 0 Or mc Is Nothing Then
        ' LH3 v18 Ф27: паттерн (или объект) не принят - фиксируем в лог и деградируем к ""
        L1_Log "L4_SM ERROR err=" & CStr(Err.Number) & " " & Err.Description & " pat=" & Left(pat, 120)
        Err.Clear
        L4_SM = "": Exit Function
    End If
    If mc.Count > 0 Then
        If mc(0).SubMatches.Count > idx Then
            L4_SM = mc(0).SubMatches(idx)
        Else
            L4_SM = ""   ' LH3 v12: группы нет - пусто вместо Err 5
        End If
    Else
        L4_SM = ""
    End If
End Function

' --- блок деклараций v4 (конст-блок SW + поля v4 + Tprev-поля) ---
Private Function L1_Decls4(ByVal SZ As Long, ByVal tprevList As String, ByVal nEdges As Long) As String
    Dim r As String
    r = "NaN_t: array[1.." & CStr(SZ) & "] of integer; // LH3 v3: номер события по хвосту ISTO_p (0=чист)" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "NaN_in: array[1.." & CStr(SZ) & "] of integer; // LH3 v3: номер события на входе узла (0=чист)" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "NaN_seq: integer; // LH3 v3: порядковый номер NaN/бред-события (первый = 1)" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "NaN_call, NaN_cnt, NaN_i: integer; // LH3 v3: счётчики скана" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "NaN_nm: array[1..1000] of string; // LH3 v3: имена виновников (по номеру события)" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "NaN_uz: array[1..1000] of integer; // LH3 v3: узел события" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "NaN_tp: array[1..1000] of integer; // LH3 v3: тип: 1=вход 2=хвост" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "NaN_rng: integer; // LH3 v4: число зеркал ISTO_p вне диапазона LIM_P" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "NaN_str: string; // LH3 v3" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "LH3_frzI: array[1.." & CStr(SZ) & "] of boolean; // Ф52: вход-страж уже сообщён (сброс при восстановлении)" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "LH3_frzT: array[1.." & CStr(SZ) & "] of boolean; // Ф52: хвост-страж уже сообщён" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "// LH3 v4: конст-блок, поля и таблицы" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "const" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "LH3_F11 = 1; LH3_A6 = 1; LH3_F12 = 1; LH3_B5 = 1; LH3_A9 = 1; LH3_A11 = 1; LH3_OIM = 1; LH3_EGFIX = 1; LH3_NORM = 1; LH3_ROI = 1;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "LH3_SHCW = 1; LH3_SHCN = 1; LH3_NOSWAP = 1; LH3_ROSMO = 1; LH3_STOR4 = 1; LH3_T2 = 0; LH3_T3 = 1; LH3_B8 = 1; LH3_B11 = 1; LH3_B12 = 1; LH3_B14 = 1; LH3_B15 = 1; LH3_FREPS = 1; LH3_PH = 1;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "LH3_CNT = 1; LH3_RNG = 0; LH3_SYM = 1; LH3_B2P = 0; LH3_MARR = 1; LH3_PSC = 1; LH3_KFP = 1; LH3_TST = 1; LH3_SKL = 1; LH3_LNG2E = 0.9999698915; // LH3 v16 Ф22" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "public" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "LH3_DBG: integer; // Ф53-в: режим выдач: 0 - молчание, 1 - включить всё (равнозначно ключам HEALTH/HEALTH2 в LOG0)" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "W_damp: Double; // LH3 v4 F11" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "LH3_cnt_OI: integer; LH3_cnt_24K: integer; LH3_cnt_OIfail: integer; LH3_cnt_clamp: integer; LH3_cnt_guard: integer; // LH3 v4 С0-1" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "LH3_avail, LH3_sc: Double; // LH3 v4 B5" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "LH3_symmax: Double; LH3_eReady: Boolean; LH3_eMax: integer; // LH3 v4 С0-5" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "LH3_eN: array[1.." & CStr(IIf(nEdges > 0, nEdges, 1)) & "] of integer;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "LH3_eS: array[1.." & CStr(IIf(nEdges > 0, nEdges, 1)) & "] of integer;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "LH3_eNS: array[1.." & CStr(IIf(nEdges > 0, nEdges, 1)) & "] of integer;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "LH3_Ci: Double; LH3_R: Double; LH3_J: Double; // LH3 v10 С4-4" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "LH3_eSS: array[1.." & CStr(IIf(nEdges > 0, nEdges, 1)) & "] of integer;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "AA_oi: array[1..100] of double; BB_oi: array[1..100] of double; LH3_PsReady: Boolean; // LH3 v4 С0-3" & vbCrLf
    Dim p As Variant
    For Each p In Split(tprevList, "|")
        If Len(p) > 0 Then r = r & "LH3_Tprev_" & Mid(p, 3) & ": Double; // LH3 v16 Ф24: без префикса Tc (как в использовании)" & vbCrLf
    Next p
    L1_Decls4 = r
End Function

' --- HEALTH-скан v4 (после "У И: n=") + счётчики + симметрия рёбер ---
Private Function L1_Scan4(ByVal SZ As Long, ByVal nEdges As Long) As String
    Dim r As String
    r = "LH3_DBG := " & CStr(LH3_DBG_OUT) & "; // Ф53-в: базовый режим (0 - молчание, 1 - всё)" & vbCrLf
        If LH3_KEYLOG = 1 Then
            r = r & "// Ф53-б: ключ лога HEALTH/HEALTH2 в LOG0 открывает вывод статистики (владелец 26.11)" & vbCrLf
            r = r & "if (LOG0[1]='HEALTH') or (LOG0[1]='HEALTH2') or (LOG0[2]='HEALTH') or (LOG0[2]='HEALTH2') or (LOG0[3]='HEALTH') or (LOG0[3]='HEALTH2') or (LOG0[4]='HEALTH') or (LOG0[4]='HEALTH2') or (LOG0[5]='HEALTH') or (LOG0[5]='HEALTH2') or (LOG0[6]='HEALTH') or (LOG0[6]='HEALTH2') or (LOG0[7]='HEALTH') or (LOG0[7]='HEALTH2') or (LOG0[8]='HEALTH') or (LOG0[8]='HEALTH2') or (LOG0[9]='HEALTH') or (LOG0[9]='HEALTH2') or (LOG0[10]='HEALTH') or (LOG0[10]='HEALTH2') then LH3_DBG := 1;" & vbCrLf
        End If
    r = r & "// LH1-NaN-scan start (v21, только давления)" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "if (LH3_DBG >= 1) and (NaN_seq>0) then begin" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  Log(' LH1 NaN-виновники (давления): всего='+cstrf(NaN_seq,0)+' - NaN/бред/диапазон гашены last-good/пресетом');  kerr:= -790;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  NaN_i:=1;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  while (NaN_i<=NaN_seq) do begin" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "    if (NaN_i<31) then begin" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "      NaN_str:='';" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "      if (NaN_tp[NaN_i]=1) then NaN_str:='вход';" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "      if (NaN_tp[NaN_i]=2) then NaN_str:='хвост';" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "      if (NaN_tp[NaN_i]=3) then NaN_str:='вход (диапазон)';" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "      if (NaN_tp[NaN_i]=4) then NaN_str:='хвост (диапазон)';" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "      if (LH3_DBG >= 1) and (NaN_uz[NaN_i]>0) then begin Log(' LH1 виновник №'+cstrf(NaN_i,0)+': узел #'+cstrf(NaN_uz[NaN_i],0)+' - '+NaN_nm[NaN_i]+' ('+NaN_str+')');  kerr:= -791; end;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "      if (LH3_DBG >= 1) and (NaN_uz[NaN_i]=0) then begin Log(' LH1 виновник №'+cstrf(NaN_i,0)+': граница - '+NaN_nm[NaN_i]+' ('+NaN_str+')');  kerr:= -792; end;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "    end else begin" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "      if (LH3_DBG >= 1) and (NaN_i=31) then begin Log(' LH1 ... всего '+cstrf(NaN_seq,0)+' событий - показаны первые 30');  kerr:= -792; end;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "    end;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "    NaN_i:=NaN_i+1;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  end;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "end;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "NaN_cnt:=0;  NaN_rng:=0;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "NaN_i:=1;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "while (NaN_i<=" & CStr(SZ) & ") do begin" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  if IsNan(ISTO_p[NaN_i]) or (Abs(ISTO_p[NaN_i])>1e300) then NaN_cnt:=NaN_cnt+1;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  if (LIM_P_max>0) and (ISTO_p[NaN_i]>1e-9) and ((ISTO_p[NaN_i]<LIM_P_min) or (ISTO_p[NaN_i]>LIM_P_max)) then NaN_rng:=NaN_rng+1;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  NaN_i:=NaN_i+1;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "end;" & vbCrLf
r = r & "if (NaN_cnt=0) and (NaN_rng=0) then begin NaN_seq:=0; NaN_i:=1; while (NaN_i<=" & CStr(SZ) & ") do begin NaN_t[NaN_i]:=0; NaN_i:=NaN_i+1; end; end;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "if (LH3_DBG >= 1) and (NaN_cnt>0) then begin Log(' LH1 ВНИМАНИЕ: NaN/бред в зеркалах ISTO_p: '+cstrf(NaN_cnt,0)+' (прорвалось мимо стражей)');  kerr:= -793; end;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "if (LH3_DBG >= 1) and (NaN_rng>0) then begin Log(' LH1 ВНИМАНИЕ: зеркал ISTO_p вне диапазона LIM_P: '+cstrf(NaN_rng,0)+' (проверьте границы _0i)');  kerr:= -793; end;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "if (LH3_DBG >= 1) and (NaN_seq=0) and (NaN_cnt=0) and (NaN_rng=0) then begin Log(' LH1 NaN-скан: чисто - NaN/бред/диапазон не обнаружены (скан #'+cstrf(NaN_call,0)+')');  kerr:= -794; end;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "NaN_call:=NaN_call+1;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "// LH1-NaN-scan end" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "// LH3 v4 С0-1: счётчики сходимости/метода" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "if (LH3_DBG >= 1) then Log(' LH3 С0-1: OI вызовов='+cstrf(LH3_cnt_OI,0)+' 24К-циклов='+cstrf(LH3_cnt_24K,0)+' OIfail='+cstrf(LH3_cnt_OIfail,0)+' clamp='+cstrf(LH3_cnt_clamp,0)+' guard='+cstrf(LH3_cnt_guard,0));  kerr:= -989;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "// LH3 v4 С0-5: симметрия рёбер (только консервативная пара)" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "if (LH3_eReady) then begin" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  LH3_symmax:=0;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  NaN_i:=1;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  while (NaN_i<=LH3_eMax) do begin" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "    if (Abs(Fc_r[LH3_eN[NaN_i],LH3_eS[NaN_i]]+Fc_r[LH3_eNS[NaN_i],LH3_eSS[NaN_i]])>LH3_symmax) then LH3_symmax:=Abs(Fc_r[LH3_eN[NaN_i],LH3_eS[NaN_i]]+Fc_r[LH3_eNS[NaN_i],LH3_eSS[NaN_i]]);" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "    NaN_i:=NaN_i+1;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  end;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "  if (LH3_DBG >= 1) then Log(' LH3 симметрия рёбер: max|Fc_r[i,k]+Fc_r[j,k'']|='+cstrf(LH3_symmax,6));  kerr:= -995;" & vbCrLf
    r = r & "end;"
    L1_Scan4 = r
End Function
